<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://store.steampowered.com/news/group/45479024/view/685257114654870245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steam Machine launches today</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steam 机器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1895</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632884' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48632884' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve&#x27;s Steam Machine hardware page surfaces its long-running attempt to bring PC gaming into the living room with console-like form factors. The effort illustrates the ongoing tension between performance, usability, and ecosystem control in living-room gaming hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 在 Steam 机器硬件页再次出现，展示其将 PC 游戏带入客厅的长期尝试，强调性能、易用性与生态系统控制之间的权衡。此举反映出游戏硬件在家庭娱乐中的定位与挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clan.fastly.steamstatic.com/images/45479024/d2ce7e96bb0ab51817ba37cf61e3ca919d6f7209.jpg' data-full='https://clan.fastly.steamstatic.com/images/45479024/d2ce7e96bb0ab51817ba37cf61e3ca919d6f7209.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48675179' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48675179' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scrollprize.org/firstscroll' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An entire Herculaneum scroll has been read for the first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>整卷赫库伦尼亚卷轴首次被解读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1668</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48675179' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have read an entire Herculaneum scroll for the first time, using imaging and computer reconstruction to recover texts long hidden inside carbonized parchment. The achievement demonstrates advances in non-destructive archaeology and could unlock more ancient writings from the disaster-stricken library.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员首次通过成像与重建，完整解读了赫库伦尼亚卷轴中被碳化羊皮包裹的文本。此项突破体现数字考古的进步，未来可能揭示更多古罗马文献及日常生活细节。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scrollprize.org/img/firstscroll/og.webp' data-full='https://scrollprize.org/img/firstscroll/og.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48678852' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://om.co/2026/06/24/1966-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Om Malik has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Om Malik 去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1322</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48678852' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tech journalism loses a distinctive voice with the passing of Om Malik, a longtime commentator and founder of a notable tech publication. His work helped shape Silicon Valley discourse and investor-friendly analysis during a pivotal era of tech growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Om Malik 的离世让科技新闻界失去一位独特观点的记者。他长期推动硅谷话语体系，并以投资者友好的分析风格影响了科技黄金时期的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://om.co/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/image-og-fallback-om-left-black-vb.png' data-full='https://om.co/wp-content/uploads/2021/05/image-og-fallback-om-left-black-vb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48690101' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48690101' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/technology/2026/06/26/openai-says-us-government-will-vet-users-its-latest-ai-model/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. government will decide who gets to use GPT-5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国政府将决定谁能使用 GPT‑5.6</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690101' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48690101' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The government will vet users of the latest GPT-5.6, signaling a move toward centralized access controls for advanced AI models. This could slow broad adoption, shape compliance requirements, and influence who can deploy AI-powered services in sensitive domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府将对最新的 GPT‑5.6 用户进行审查，标志着对高级 AI 模型实行集中化访问控制的趋势。这可能放缓大规模采用，改变合规要求，并影响在敏感领域部署 AI 驱动服务的主体。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48679608' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48679608' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://expression.fire.org/p/the-papers-please-era-of-the-internet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &#x27;papers, please&#x27; era of the internet will decimate your privacy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网的“请出示证件”时代将侵蚀你的隐私</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 597</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48679608' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As services demand more identity checks and credentialing, pervasive surveillance and data collection erode personal privacy. The piece argues that the future internet will track who you are across apps, payments, and services, unless privacy-preserving authentication tools gain traction, such as zero-knowledge proofs and selective disclosure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着各类服务越来越依赖身份验证，隐私将被广泛监控和数据化。文章提示，未来的互联网将跨应用、跨支付持续跟踪个人身份，除非引入如零知识证明等隐私保护的身份认证方案来抵消这种趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ikRw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9049285c-dc2e-420a-b023-0f94858244b3_1000x667.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ikRw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F9049285c-dc2e-420a-b023-0f94858244b3_1000x667.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48689028' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48689028' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/previewing-gpt-5-6-sol/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Previewing GPT‑5.6 Sol: a next-generation model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>预览 GPT‑5.6 Sol：下一代模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 731</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48689028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A preview of GPT-5.6 Sol hints at a next‑generation model with stronger reasoning and more reliable tool use. It emphasizes speed and safety controls, and sheds light on deployment tradeoffs for developers building assistant-powered apps. The preview signals growing importance of governance and cost management as models scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT‑5.6 Sol 的预览揭示了一个更强推理和更可靠工具调用的下一代模型。其强调速度和安全控制，并揭示开发者在构建智能助手时的部署取舍与成本管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/48XpR5dxhE6jJQ03HTUnWZ/ac68fbfb7761fa72ac7442c6744dba56/_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/48XpR5dxhE6jJQ03HTUnWZ/ac68fbfb7761fa72ac7442c6744dba56/_.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48626137' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48626137' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.deno.com/runtime/desktop/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deno Desktop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Deno 桌面版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1083</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48626137' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Deno Desktop introduces a cross-platform desktop runtime for building apps with TypeScript and JavaScript using the Deno toolchain. The docs outline how to create, package, and run desktop apps with Deno, bridging web UI tech with native OS capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Deno Desktop 引入一个跨平台桌面运行时，方便用 TypeScript/JavaScript 基于 Deno 构建桌面应用。文档介绍了创建、打包与运行桌面应用的方式，以及与原生操作系统能力的对接。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.deno.com/runtime/desktop/index.png' data-full='https://docs.deno.com/runtime/desktop/index.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48645173' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48645173' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2026/06/23/destroy-the-village/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What we call &quot;age verification&quot; is actually mass surveillance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们所谓的年龄验证其实是在进行大规模监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 933</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645173' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 500</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48645173' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Age verification systems end up acting as mass surveillance, aggregating data across sites and enabling detailed behavior profiling. The piece argues these schemes are inherently invasive and proposes alternative privacy-preserving approaches or abolishing them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>年龄验证系统被视为大规模监控，跨站点收集数据以核验年龄，具有隐私风险。文章主张摒弃或重设计这类系统，倡导更隐私友好的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/23Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/23Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698617' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48698617' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/bikini/exploitarium' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anonymous GitHub account mass-dropping undisclosed 0-days</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>匿名 GitHub 账户大规模披露未披露的零日漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 917</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698617' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48698617' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An anonymous GitHub account is dropping a flood of undisclosed zero-day exploits, with no vendor disclosure. This creates an elevated risk surface for downstream developers and security teams, who must assume exploits exist in the wild until patched. The incident highlights ongoing tensions in vulnerability disclosure norms and the difficulty of policing zero-day leaks in public repos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个匿名的 GitHub 账户大量公开尚未披露的零日漏洞，且未向厂商通报。此举扩大了开发者和安全团队的攻击面，迫使他们尽快修补并加强威胁情报共享。事件暴露了漏洞披露规范的不足，以及在公开环境中对待零日漏洞的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19797bcdf2beaf6340ca6e126e505f6bf35ca396eb8a06a50113566dafca8968/bikini/exploitarium' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/19797bcdf2beaf6340ca6e126e505f6bf35ca396eb8a06a50113566dafca8968/bikini/exploitarium' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48657030' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48657030' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bunny.net/blog/were-making-bunny-dns-free/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We’re making Bunny DNS free</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bunny DNS 免费开放</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 894</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657030' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48657030' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bunny DNS announces it will make its domain name system service free, lowering the barrier for personal sites and startups to adopt fast, resilient DNS. The move could pressure competitors to offer similar free tiers and raises questions about sustainability and future monetization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bunny DNS 将其域名解析服务免费化，降低个人站点和初创企业采用高效 DNS 的门槛。此举可能对竞争对手施压，促使行业普遍提供免费层，但也引发对未来盈利模式与可持续性的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bunny.net/blog/content/images/2026/06/DNS_-Free-DNS-blog-post--1-.png' data-full='https://bunny.net/blog/content/images/2026/06/DNS_-Free-DNS-blog-post--1-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48672732' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48672732' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/asia-pacific/apple-raises-prices-macbooks-ipads-memory-costs-skyrocket-2026-06-25/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple raises prices of MacBooks, iPads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果提高 MacBook 与 iPad 价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 830</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48672732' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple raised prices on MacBooks and iPads, citing higher costs for memory and components. The move tightens premium device margins and could influence consumer purchasing behavior, particularly during upcoming refresh cycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果因内存等组件成本上涨，提高了 MacBook 与 iPad 的定价。这一举措收窄了高端设备的利润空间，可能影响消费者在未来升级周期的购买决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622867' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622867' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.newgas.net/did-my-old-job-only-exist-because-of-fraud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Did my old job only exist because of fraud?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的旧工作是不是只是因为欺诈而存在？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 822</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 417</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622867' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning whether some roles exist due to misaligned incentives or fraud, the piece explores the fragility of certain job markets and the potential for automation. It advocates for transparency, better verification, and data-driven evaluation of roles rather than anecdotal narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑某些岗位是否因激励错位或欺诈而存在，探讨了部分职业市场的脆弱性与自动化的潜在影响。倡导提高透明度、强化核验，并以数据驱动来评估岗位，而非仅凭主观叙述。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://david.newgas.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-4-1024x148.png' data-full='https://david.newgas.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-4-1024x148.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48663324' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48663324' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/24/openai-unveils-its-first-custom-chip-built-by-broadcom/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI unveils its first custom chip, built by Broadcom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 发布首个定制芯片，由 Broadcom 打造</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48663324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 813</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48663324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48663324' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI has unveiled its first custom silicon, built in partnership with Broadcom. The move signals a shift toward owning more of the hardware stack to optimize model serving and efficiency, potentially reducing reliance on Nvidia GPUs. If successful, it could reshape cloud economics and push competitors to accelerate in-house hardware efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 公布了首款定制芯片，与 Broadcom 合作打造，旨在优化自家模型的推理与训练性能。此举标志对硬件环节更深度掌控，可能降低对外部供应商的依赖，并影响云端成本与定价竞争。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-2026-06-24-at-9.49.59-AM.jpg?resize=1200,670' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-2026-06-24-at-9.49.59-AM.jpg?resize=1200,670' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48630020' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48630020' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitchellh.com/writing/zig-donation-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pledging another $400k to the Zig software foundation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>再向 Zig 软件基金会捐赠40万美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 803</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 288</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630020' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig&#x27;s ecosystem keeps growing as a further $400k is pledged to its software foundation. The donation signals continued community support and could accelerate language tooling, compiler work, and ecosystem initiatives that push Zig toward broader adoption and stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 生态系统再次获得资金支持，向其软件基金会再捐出40万美元，显示社区的持续支持。捐款有望推动语言工具链、编译器改进和生态项目，加速 Zig 的普及与稳定性提升。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48618455' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48618455' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14328960-identity-verification-on-claude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Identity verification on Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的身份验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 791</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 661</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48618455' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude includes an identity verification flow to control access for sensitive features and enterprise usage. The guide covers when verification is required, what methods are supported, and how it affects access privileges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 提供身份验证流程以控制对敏感功能和企业用途的访问。指南说明何时需要验证、支持哪些方法，以及对权限访问的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' data-full='https://downloads.intercomcdn.com/i/o/lupk8zyo/792494/717b10d3ebd874823db64841659b/76f3062d78ebbb04863fb1de3ef9cca0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48673671' data-tags='Show HN,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48673671' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hackernewstrends.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I made Google Trends for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of comments</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：用 18 年评论做成 Hacker News 的 Google 趋势</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 788</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673671' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48673671' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hobbyist built a Google Trends-style tool for Hacker News by indexing 18 years of HN comments. It lets you track topic popularity, spikes around events, and compare keywords over time. Demonstrates how public datasets can be mined for long-term trend analysis without official APIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位热爱者通过对 18 年 Hacker News 评论进行索引，打造了一个类似 Google Trends 的趋势分析工具。它可以追踪话题热度、事件引发的峰值，并对比不同关键词在时间轴上的变化，展示了在没有官方 API 的情况下对公开数据进行长期趋势分析的可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hackernewstrends.com/opengraph-image?72011d0134607af3' data-full='https://hackernewstrends.com/opengraph-image?72011d0134607af3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48696585' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48696585' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec/blob/main/DSpark_paper.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DSpark: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DSpark：推测解码加速大语言模型推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 775</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696585' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48696585' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DSpark introduces speculative decoding to speed up LLM inference, presenting a method to trade a bit of accuracy for substantial gains in throughput. The paper includes benchmarks and comparisons against baseline decoding strategies, highlighting practical implications for deploying faster AI services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DSpark 提出一种推测解码方法以加速大语言模型推理，在保持精度损失可控的前提下提升吞吐量。论文给出基准测试和与常规解码的对比，展示在部署更快的 AI 服务时的实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2cbed0be823a39c0476db61ecfc9cfafe5981ecfd4cd97014635836537d5311/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/c2cbed0be823a39c0476db61ecfc9cfafe5981ecfd4cd97014635836537d5311/deepseek-ai/DeepSpec' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48664814' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48664814' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/china/anthropic-says-alibaba-illicitly-extracted-claude-ai-model-capabilities-2026-06-24/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic says Alibaba illicitly extracted Claude AI model capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic称阿里巴巴非法提取Claude AI模型能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 773</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664814' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48664814' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic accuses Alibaba of illicitly extracting Claude&#x27;s capabilities, alleging IP theft and copying of proprietary model features. The claim highlights tensions over access to foundation-model technology and could affect licensing, partnerships, and regulatory scrutiny as Alibaba expands its AI stack. If proven, it could spur litigation and reshape cross-border AI collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 指控阿里巴巴非法提取 Claude 模型的能力，指控涉及知识产权和对专有特征的盗取。此举凸显基础模型领域对接入方式的激烈竞争和潜在法律风险，可能影响许可、伙伴关系及合规审查。若指控成立，可能引发诉讼并重塑跨境 AI 合作格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48697560' data-tags='Open Source,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48697560' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openra.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenRA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenRA 开源实时策略游戏引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 764</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48697560' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenRA remains a vibrant open-source platform that reimagines classic Command &amp; Conquer titles with a modern engine, cross-platform play, and active modding support. It illustrates how community-driven game projects keep retro favorites accessible and extensible long after the original releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenRA 作为一个开源平台，持续以现代引擎重新演绎经典指挥与征服系列，支持跨平台对战与活跃的模组生态。该项目证明了社区驱动的游戏项目如何让怀旧游戏在当代保持可访问与可扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openra.net/images/social.jpg?t=2026-04-30-18-11' data-full='https://www.openra.net/images/social.jpg?t=2026-04-30-18-11' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48630066' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48630066' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nevergivethemyourface.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Never Give Them Your Face</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永远别让他们得到你的脸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 741</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630066' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biometric privacy takes center stage as the piece argues you should not hand over facial data to services. It explains the risks of irreversible exposure and outlines practical steps to minimize biometric leakage and design identity systems with privacy in mind.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章聚焦生物识别隐私，主张避免向服务提供脸部数据，因为一旦暴露难以撤回。文中给出实际步骤以降低面部识别数据的泄露风险，并探讨在身份设计中应考虑的隐私治理要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nevergivethemyourface.com/assets/images/og.png' data-full='https://nevergivethemyourface.com/assets/images/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698684' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48698684' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pluralistic.net/2026/06/27/zuckerstreisand-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zuckerberg&#x27;s war on whistleblowers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格对吹哨人的战争</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 715</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48698684' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how Meta handles whistleblowers, including legal tactics and public messaging that factions describe as aggressive suppression. The piece surveys the implications for corporate accountability, transparency, and whistleblower protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评估 Meta 对吹哨人的应对策略，涉及法律手段与公共叙事，被指控为对吹哨人进行压制。讨论对企业问责、透明度以及吹哨人保护的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/27Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/craphound.com/images/27Jun2026.jpg?w=840&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48649011' data-tags='Career,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48649011' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/JPoehnelt/status/2069482265953087602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fired by Google for creating the Google workspace CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因为创建 Google Workspace CLI 而被解雇</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48649011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 705</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48649011' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 413</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48649011' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A former Google employee reports being fired for building the Google Workspace CLI, raising questions about internal policies toward external tooling and open-source contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称一名前 Google 员工因开发 Google Workspace CLI 而被解雇，引发关于内部政策对外部工具和开源贡献态度的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HLhHJvWaoAEBgxd.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HLhHJvWaoAEBgxd.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48648619' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swipe.futo.tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FUTO Swipe – A new swipe typing model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FUTO Swipe 轻扫输入模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 695</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648619' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FUTO Swipe introduces a new swipe-typing model designed to improve accuracy and speed on mobile keyboards. The model promises smarter prediction and resilience across layouts, with a public demo at the project site.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FUTO Swipe 推出全新的滑动输入模型，旨在提升移动端键盘的准确性与输入速度。该模型声称在不同布局下都具备更智能的预测能力，公开演示在项目站点可见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://swipe.futo.tech/hero-2.webp' data-full='https://swipe.futo.tech/hero-2.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48709670' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48709670' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://semgrep.dev/blog/2026/we-have-mythos-at-home-glm-52-beats-claude-in-our-cyber-benchmarks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 beats Claude in our benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 5.2 在基准测试中击败 Claude</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 694</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48709670' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 beat Claude in our benchmarks, showing that newer open models are narrowing the gap with top proprietary rivals. The result reinforces that cost, licensing, and ecosystem maturity can influence real-world decisions as AI hardware and tooling evolve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 5.2 在我们的基准测试中击败 Claude，显示较新的开源模型正在缩小与顶级专有模型的差距。该结果强调，在推理硬件和工具集持续演进的背景下，成本、许可与生态系统成熟度可能成为实际选型的关键因素。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/glm-5.2-beats-claude-in-our-cybersecurity-benchmarks.png' data-full='https://semgrep.dev/assets/blog/glm-5.2-beats-claude-in-our-cybersecurity-benchmarks.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48669534' data-tags='Web,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48669534' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Half-Life 2 in a Browser</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中运行的《半条命 2》</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48669534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 662</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48669534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48669534' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based recreation of Valve&#x27;s classic runs Half-Life 2 in the browser, showcasing a WebAssembly/WebGL-style approach to bring a full 3D game to the web without native installs. The project highlights the browser’s growing capacity to host heavy games and preserve classic titles, while also pointing to the performance and licensing trade-offs of in-browser emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器里直接运行《半条命 2》的演示，展示了通过网页技术将完整 3D 游戏带入浏览器的可能。它凸显了浏览器承载重度游戏与保留经典作品的潜力，同时也暴露了实现此类功能时的性能与授权权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/assets/hl2.png' data-full='https://hl2.slqnt.dev/assets/hl2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48647799' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48647799' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/future-file-format/f3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F3 文件格式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 648</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48647799' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>F3 introduces a new forward-looking file format designed for simplicity and future-proofing. It emphasizes extensibility and interoperability, aiming to reduce data fragmentation across tools and ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F3 将推出一种面向未来的新型文件格式，强调简单、可扩展和向后兼容性，旨在提高数据交换的鲁棒性。该项目体现了对自描述数据格式和跨工具互操作性的重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2aee7f5c2eb5989ac5282ff7184380e36c39a4aed5c2d048b141e565ea12b63e/future-file-format/F3' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2aee7f5c2eb5989ac5282ff7184380e36c39a4aed5c2d048b141e565ea12b63e/future-file-format/F3' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707719' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48707719' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/double-threat-to-private-communications-undemocratic-chat-control-backroom-deals-and-imminent-concessions-spark-relaunch-of-fightchatcontrol-eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU to legislate about Chat Control behind closed doors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟在幕后推动聊天监控立法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 362</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48707719' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports indicate the EU plans to legislate on Chat Control behind closed doors, raising concerns about transparency and civil liberties. The move signals a push to regulate private communications and could set norms that influence global tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，欧盟正在幕后推动“聊天控制”相关立法，引发对透明度和公民自由的担忧。此举表明欧洲正推动对私人通信的规制，可能对全球科技政策产生重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' data-full='https://www.patrick-breyer.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/240130_Breyer-Alternativen-engl.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48634694' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48634694' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipvm.com/reports/police-chiefs-track' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock-Powered Police Chiefs Stalking Women Shows Why Warrants Are Needed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 监控网络让警察追踪女性，凸显为何需要搜令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634694' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48634694' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report shows police using a private surveillance network to track a woman, underscoring the risks of warrantless, opaque data sharing. It reinforces why warrants, oversight, and clear data governance are essential when relying on third-party camera networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示警察依赖私人监控网络追踪一名女性，凸显在使用第三方摄像网络时，缺乏授权与透明度的监控带来的风险。强调在此类数据资源下，必须有搜令、监督与明确的数据治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.ipvm.com/uploads/919f/fb61/b283b4f1-bf9b-4ece-9362-d9d759874b0e.jpg' data-full='https://s.ipvm.com/uploads/919f/fb61/b283b4f1-bf9b-4ece-9362-d9d759874b0e.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48674446' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48674446' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-25/ford-has-been-rehiring-quality-inspectors-after-ai-fell-short' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ford AI hiccups push carmaker to rehire ‘gray beard’ inspectors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>福特因AI小故障再聘资深检验员</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674446' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 321</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48674446' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ford&#x27;s AI-driven quality checks fell short, prompting the automaker to rehire veteran inspectors. This move underscores the current limits of AI in high-stakes manufacturing and the value of human-in-the-loop quality control. It signals that even at a major company, experienced staff remain essential to catch edge cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>福特在AI质量检测表现不佳后，重新雇佣资深检验员。此举凸显了AI在高风险制造中的局限，以及人机混合质量控制的价值。即便是大公司，也需要经验丰富的人员来应对边缘情况。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers practical steps to run the GLM-5.2 model locally, enabling offline inference without relying on cloud services. Running locally improves privacy and latency and gives developers more control, but it also demands hardware and setup considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供将 GLM-5.2 本地化运行的实用步骤，使离线推理不再依赖云端。 本地运行提升隐私与延迟控制，但需要合适的硬件和安装配置。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纪念在文本下方标出红色和绿色波浪线的工程师</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 593</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648959' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A memorial piece recalls the engineer who introduced red and green squiggles under words in text editors, highlighting how tiny UX cues shaped modern editing and software behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>追忆在文本编辑器中首次引入“红色/绿色下划线”提示的工程师，文章强调这类细微的用户体验信号对现代文本编辑和软件行为的深远影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48658718' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48658718' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://paolino.me/founding-a-company-in-germany/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Founding a company in Germany: €9600, 152 days and I still can&#x27;t send an invoice</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post recounts the experience of starting a company in Germany, highlighting costs and bureaucracy: €9,600 and 152 days, yet still unable to issue invoices due to local constraints. The ordeal underscores startup friction in EU jurisdictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>博主讲述在德国创办公司的经历，成本高、手续繁琐（约9600欧元、152天），甚至无法开具正式发票，暴露出初创阶段的制度瓶颈。此类经验凸显在德经商和创业环境中的现实挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada outlines a nuclear renaissance with up to 10 reactors by 2040, signaling a larger low-carbon electricity mix. The plan emphasizes energy security and emissions goals while acknowledging regulatory, cost, and public-acceptance hurdles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大提出到2040年建设多达10座核反应堆的核能复兴计划，意在扩大低碳电力结构。该方案强调能源安全与减排目标，同时面临监管、成本与公众接受度的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48692995' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.semafor.com/article/06/27/2026/us-releases-powerful-anthropic-model-mythos-to-some-us-companies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. allows Anthropic to release Mythos AI to ‘trusted’ US organizations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国允许 Anthropic 将 Mythos AI 提供给“可信”的美国机构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 766</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692995' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. government has allowed Anthropic to release its Mythos AI model to a subset of trusted US organizations, signaling a controlled expansion of access to powerful AI. This move raises questions about governance, safety testing, data handling, and how enterprise deployments will shape model behavior and risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国政府允许 Anthropic 将 Mythos AI 向一批“可信”美国机构发布，体现对强大人工智能的受控扩展。这一举措引发关于治理、安全测试、数据处理，以及企业部署在模型行为与风险管理中的影响的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.semafor.com/50a98e4cab1aadfa340671d35d9027ed27e6899b-740x493.png?rect=0,53,740,389&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://img.semafor.com/50a98e4cab1aadfa340671d35d9027ed27e6899b-740x493.png?rect=0,53,740,389&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620090' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48620090' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sandimetz.com/blog/2016/1/20/the-wrong-abstraction' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prefer duplication over the wrong abstraction (2016)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宁可重复，也别用错的抽象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 533</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620090' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Duplicating code can keep intent clear when an abstraction would misfit. Premature, poorly fitting abstractions hurt maintainability; the post argues that clarity beats clever architecture and that refactoring can come later when the need is real.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在抽象不合适时，重复实现能保持意图清晰。过早的、与场景不符的抽象会降低可维护性；文章主张清晰胜过花哨的架构，真正需要时再进行重构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' data-full='https://images.squarespace-cdn.com/content/v1/537c0374e4b0f52ed92942e6/1406255585576-8V9OA9Q3VR7LM1P1NCAE/logo-color.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/mfts0/status/2070080422482977095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hey Nico, you didn&#x27;t vibe code your data room but stole it from Papermark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>嘿 Nico，你没有为数据房写代码却从 Papermark 偷来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 527</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672328' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48672328' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An accusation that Nico didn’t code his data room but copied it from Papermark, highlighting the importance of provenance and originality in data tooling. The thread serves as a cautionary note on attribution, tooling trust, and the risks of reusing others&#x27; work without proper credit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有人指控 Nico 没有自己实现数据房的代码，而是抄袭自 Papermark，这凸显了数据工具中来源证明与署名的重要性。此事强调在工具生态中对原创性和 attribution 的信任风险，以及对他人工作未正确致谢的潜在后果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2070080422482977095' data-full='https://jf.x.com/images/media-preview/2070080422482977095' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622778' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apertvs.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apertus – Open Foundation Model for Sovereign AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apertus：面向主权 AI 的开放基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 526</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apertus unveils an open foundation model designed for sovereign AI deployment with data sovereignty baked in. It supports open weights and configurable safety controls to reduce dependence on centralized providers, enabling in-country hosting and governance. This matters for governments and organizations seeking transparency and autonomy in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apertus 发布一个开放的基础模型，支持主权 AI 的部署与数据自主管控。提供开源权重和可配置的安全策略，旨在降低对大型厂商的依赖，使其能在本地或受监管环境中运行。此举对政府与重视数据主权与透明度的机构尤为重要。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techstackups.com/comparisons/glm-5.2-vs-opus/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM 5.2 vs. Opus</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 GLM 5.2 与 Opus 的对比，聚焦在准确性、解码、效率与部署场景上的权衡，帮助开发者在本地部署与云端 API 之间做出选择。比较凸显了部署场景对模型选择的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48671468' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48671468' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/06/23/lastpass-notifies-users-of-yet-another-data-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LastPass notifies users of yet another data breach</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LastPass 再次通知用户数据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48671468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48671468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48671468' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LastPass alerts users to another data breach, underscoring the enduring security risks in credential managers. The update reinforces the case for enabling multi-factor authentication, using device-based security keys, and adopting zero-trust practices for sensitive accounts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LastPass 再次警示用户发生数据泄露，凸显凭证管理工具持续存在的安全风险。此次更新强调开启多因素认证、使用基于设备的安全密钥，以及对敏感账户实行零信任策略的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2024/03/lastpass-outage.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2024/03/lastpass-outage.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48692051' data-tags='Legal,Politics,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48692051' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/we-can-still-stop-californias-3d-printer-surveillance-scheme' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We can still stop California&#x27;s 3D printer surveillance scheme</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们仍有机会阻止加州的3D 打印监控计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692051' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes ongoing efforts to derail California&#x27;s proposed 3D printer surveillance regime, citing civil liberties concerns, policy pathways, and legal challenges. The piece frames maker freedoms and open hardware as a battleground in digital fabrication governance, with organizers mobilizing public pressure and legislative action.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阐述阻止加州拟议的3D打印监控计划的努力，强调公民自由与开源硬件的相关性，以及通过法律与公共动员寻求改变的路径。文章指出监管举措对创客与数字制造的影响以及未来政策走向的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/3dprintca.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/3dprintca.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48645152' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48645152' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/ocr-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral OCR 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral OCR 4 版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 496</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48645152' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral OCR 4 is the latest open-model OCR release, promising higher recognition accuracy and faster inference across scripts. It highlights improvements for multi-language text and robustness on noisy scans, expanding on-device deployment options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral OCR 4 是最新的光学字符识别模型版本，提升识别准确性和推理速度，支持多语言并更适合边缘设备部署。新版本在嘈杂文本场景下表现更稳健，便于在实际应用中落地。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/OCR-OG.png' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/OCR-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48626930' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48626930' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/28224' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex logging bug may write TBs to local SSDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Codex 日志记录漏洞可能将 TB 数据写入本地 SSD</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48626930' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An issue in Codex&#x27;s logging subsystem can cause terabytes of data to be written to local SSDs, risking disk exhaustion on developer machines. This highlights the fragility of client-side logging in AI-assisted tooling and the need for safer defaults and rate-limiting. The incident underscores the importance of robust storage safeguards when using code-generation copilots locally.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Codex 的日志记录子系统存在漏洞，可能把 TB 级数据写入本地 SSD，风险是磁盘空间耗尽。此事暴露了在 AI 辅助工具中进行本地日志记录的脆弱性，需更安全的默认设置和速率限制。强调在使用代码生成助手进行本地开发时加强存储保护的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be9e4adcbaa9b9afbdb33d6b53cd738d34be03879707fb328e1886db4c63bdc2/openai/codex/issues/28224' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/be9e4adcbaa9b9afbdb33d6b53cd738d34be03879707fb328e1886db4c63bdc2/openai/codex/issues/28224' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48642784' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48642784' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/middle-east/israel-targeted-gaza-children-resulting-genocide-un-inquiry-says-2026-06-23/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israel targeted Gaza children resulting in genocide, UN inquiry says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联合国调查称以色列针对加沙儿童的行动构成种族灭绝</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 484</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48642784' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A UN inquiry concluded that Israeli actions in Gaza targeted children and amounted to genocide, prompting debates over accountability and international law. The report underscores calls for independent investigations and civilian protections in conflict zones.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联合国调查称以色列在加沙地区针对儿童的行动构成种族灭绝，引发关于问责与国际法的广泛讨论。该报告强化了推动独立调查与在冲突地区保护平民的呼声。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48617990' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48617990' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.beyondallreason.info' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Beyond All Reason (Free Total Annihilation Inspired RTS)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>超越一切理性：免费 Total Annihilation 风格的 RTS</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 482</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 288</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48617990' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beyond All Reason is a free, community-driven RTS inspired by Total Annihilation, offering large-scale battles and classic resource economics. Built with modern, mod-friendly architecture, it emphasizes open development and cross-platform play, sustaining retro RTS enthusiasm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Beyond All Reason 是一款免费、由社区驱动的实时策略游戏，受《Total Annihilation》启发，提供大规模战斗与经典资源系统。它采用现代化、便于修改的架构，强调开源开发与跨平台对战，延续对经典 RTS 的热爱。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5c68622246b367adf6f3041d/60d97aab6fdf2731bde767e8_BAR_Web_OG-media-2021_1200_V2.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/5c68622246b367adf6f3041d/60d97aab6fdf2731bde767e8_BAR_Web_OG-media-2021_1200_V2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48643426' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48643426' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/baidu/Unlimited-OCR' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Unlimited OCR: One-shot long-horizon parsing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Unlimited OCR：一次性长时域解析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48643426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 478</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48643426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48643426' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Unlimited OCR introduces one-shot long-horizon parsing for documents, enabling robust recognition and parsing over long content in a single pass. This advancement improves efficiency for enterprise document processing and hints at broader capabilities for scalable OCR in long-form workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Unlimited OCR 提出一次性对长篇文档进行解析的能力，提升单次完成对长文本的识别与解析的鲁棒性与效率。该进展有望显著优化企业级文档处理，并推动长文档工作流中的 OCR 能力扩展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/56b00cab11a6810ad8f366b6cda9fc06a5a10c5e9362a37b862ef91955abaa78/baidu/Unlimited-OCR' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/56b00cab11a6810ad8f366b6cda9fc06a5a10c5e9362a37b862ef91955abaa78/baidu/Unlimited-OCR' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48660178' data-tags='Hardware,Energy,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660178' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.nvidia.com/blog/liquid-cooling-ai-factories/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>45°C cooling design cuts data center water use to near zero</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英伟达的 45°C 冷却设计将数据中心用水量降至近零</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 471</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660178' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 412</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660178' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA outlines a cooling strategy that uses 45°C water to remove heat, dramatically cutting data center water use and enabling simpler plumbing. The approach could lower OPEX for AI/data workloads and influence future data center infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英伟达介绍了一种使用 45°C 的热水进行散热的设计，显著降低数据中心的用水量并简化管路。该方案有望降低 AI/数据工作负载的数据中心运营成本，并可能影响未来的基础设施设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/45CLiquidCooling.jpg' data-full='https://blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/45CLiquidCooling.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48697335' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48697335' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dervis.de/physical/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case for physical media ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>实体媒介所有权的必要性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697335' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 315</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48697335' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In an era of streaming and digital licensing, ownership of physical media remains an assertion of durable access and value. The piece argues that owning discs or prints offers resilience against platform changes, licensing headaches, and data loss, complementing digital options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在流媒体和数字授权盛行的时代，拥有实体媒介被视为对长期可访问性和价值的保障。文章主张实体收藏能规避平台变更、授权限制和数据丢失等风险，与数字内容互为补充。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48666927' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48666927' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/blogging-stating-the-obvious/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blogging can just be stating the obvious</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>博客也可以只是把显而易见的事情说清楚</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48666927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48666927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48666927' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that blogging doesn&#x27;t have to chase novelty; crisp, clear articulation of obvious ideas can be valuable for documenting and clarifying thinking. Consistency and practical sharing often outweigh grandiose takes, lowering the barrier to regular writing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为博客不必追逐新奇，清晰表达显而易见的观点本身也有价值，有助于记录与梳理思路。坚持输出、强调实用性往往比追逐轰动观点更能推动持续写作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48682737' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48682737' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://akrites.org/letter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We all depend on open source. We will defend it together</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章强调开源作为基础设施的脆弱性，面对政策压力与生态疲劳。呼吁社区团结共同行动，强化治理、资金与协作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://explorer.oxide.computer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oxide computer 3D rack guided tour</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过三维导览了解 Oxide Computer 的机架系统，展示模块化、面向数据中心工作负载的高密度计算节点，以及集成散热和密集互连。此举体现了向紧凑、以软件定义硬件为核心的基础设施演进趋势。</p>
