<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660711' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rubyllm.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RubyLLM: A Ruby framework for all major AI providers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RubyLLM：面向所有主流 AI 提供商的 Ruby 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660711' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660711' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RubyLLM provides a unified Ruby framework for major AI providers, letting developers write provider-agnostic code and swap backends with minimal changes. The move highlights how language ecosystems are competing on developer ergonomics as AI tooling expands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RubyLLM 提供一个统一接口，让 Ruby 开发者在不同主要 AI 提供商之间切换时无需重写代码。通过消除厂商绑定，它可能加速 Ruby 在 AI 应用的落地，并反映出各语言生态在开发者体验上的竞争。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rubyllm.com/assets/images/og/pages/rubyllm.png' data-full='https://rubyllm.com/assets/images/og/pages/rubyllm.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48645437' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tikz.dev/editor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: TikZ Editor – WYSIWYG editor for figures in LaTeX</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: TikZ 编辑器——LaTeX 图形的所见即所得编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 442</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48645437' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN: TikZ Editor offers a WYSIWYG interface for creating LaTeX figures, exporting clean TikZ code and live previews. It lowers the barrier to publication-ready diagrams and speeds up LaTeX workflows by removing the steep TikZ syntax learning curve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该工具提供可视化编辑 TikZ 的界面，直接绘制并导出整洁的 TikZ 代码与预览。对科研写作者而言，降低图形制作门槛，提升文档可视化表达效率，缩短从草图到成品的时间。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/interactive/2026/06/21/us/trucks-suv-pedestrian-crashes.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The deadly rise of giant trucks and SUVs</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48623347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48623347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 692</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48623347' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An in-depth look at how the rise of giant trucks and SUVs correlates with more severe pedestrian crashes. The piece links vehicle size to visibility and crash dynamics, urging policymakers and manufacturers to consider safety-by-design and regulatory changes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对巨型卡车与SUV日益增多与行人事故严重性上升之间的关系进行了深入分析。研究指出车辆体积对可见性和碰撞后果的影响，呼吁在设计、安全标准和监管方面做出调整。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48625823' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Danish privacy activist known for anti-surveillance advocacy had his home raided by police, highlighting ongoing tension between privacy campaigns and law enforcement. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丹麦知名隐私倡导者因反监控活动而遭警方突袭，凸显了隐私倡议与执法部门之间持续的紧张关系。此事凸显了在挑战数字监控时，活动家可能面临的个人风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/06/260614012006.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The brain was not designed for this much bad news</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大脑并非为如此大量的坏消息而设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 426</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615569' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Research shows constant exposure to alarming news can overwhelm attention and degrade mental health.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示持续暴露于让人不安的新闻会耗尽注意力并损害心理健康。文中讨论媒体摄取的影响，以及限制暴露、保护认知资源的实际策略。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48706560' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/kids-act-would-require-age-checks-get-online' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The KIDS Act would require age checks to get online</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>KIDS Act 将强制在线年龄验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48706560' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The KIDS Act proposes mandatory online age verification, aiming to shield minors but raising privacy and profiling concerns. Critics warn it could normalize surveillance and create security risks for users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>KIDS Act 提议对在线服务强制进行年龄验证，旨在保护未成年人；但也引发隐私与画像化担忧。批评者警告这可能使监控常态化并带来安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverificationbanner-3.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ageverificationbanner-3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48616304' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://princechazz.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Chess-Inspired Roguelike</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以棋为灵感的 Roguelike 游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 414</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48616304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48616304' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This Show HN introduces a roguelike that borrows chess-inspired mechanics, mapping piece movement and capture rules into dungeon navigation and combat. The design demonstrates how classic board-game concepts can reframe turn-based exploration and strategy in roguelikes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款 Show HN 将棋子走法与棋子规则融入 Roguelike 的地牢探索与战斗之中，提供新颖的策略体验。通过将棋类概念映射到回合制玩法，展示了棋盘游戏思路在 Rogue 类游戏中的再应用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48646659' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.krea.ai/blog/krea-2-technical-report' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Krea 2: SOTA open-weights 12B image model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Krea 2：12B 开源权重的图像模型达到 SOTA</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646659' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48646659' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Krea 2 releases an open-weight 12B image model that achieves state-of-the-art results on several benchmarks. The open weights lower barriers to experimentation and allow researchers and startups to fine-tune and deploy more freely. The development underscores the rapid progress and growing impact of open-model ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Krea 2 发布了具开源权重的 12B 图像模型，在多个基准上达到最新性能。开源权重降低了实验门槛，研究人员和初创企业可以更自由地微调与部署。该进展凸显了开放模型生态的快速成长及影响力。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48616800' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google now serves about half of its traffic over IPv6, marking a milestone in the long drive toward IPv6 ubiquity. This shift reduces reliance on IPv4 and suggests major networks and clients are ready for native IPv6, with implications for routing, edge services, and future protocol deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 现在约有一半流量通过 IPv6 传输，标志着向 IPv6 普及的里程碑。这一转变表明主要网络与客户端已为原生 IPv6 做好准备，可能影响路由、边缘服务配置以及未来的协议部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/50-percent_ft.jpg' data-full='https://blog.apnic.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/50-percent_ft.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48653060' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48653060' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lse.ac.uk/granthaminstitute/events/extreme-heat-improving-governance-and-strengthening-action-around-the-world/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Extreme Heat conference cancelled due to extreme heat warning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>因极端高温警告取消的极端高温治理大会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 404</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 467</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48653060' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A conference on extreme heat governance was canceled due to a heat warning, underscoring how climate risks disrupt policy-oriented events. The session would have explored governance improvements and concrete actions needed worldwide.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>因高温警报，极端高温治理峰会被取消，凸显气候风险对学术与政策活动的现实影响。主办方强调需要加强全球治理与行动应对。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.lse.ac.uk/granthaminstitute/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/London_heat.jpg' data-full='https://www.lse.ac.uk/granthaminstitute/wp-content/uploads/2025/07/London_heat.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48642699' data-tags='Finance,Energy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48642699' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stephendiehl.com/posts/bad_place_2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crypto in 2026: Oh, This Is the Bad Place</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年的加密货币：这地方真糟糕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 401</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 503</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48642699' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Crypto in 2026 is portrayed as a troubling landscape, with regulatory crackdowns, scams, energy concerns, and hype that may not sustain. The piece advises caution and skepticism about long-term viability, urging readers to separate real tech progress from marketing noise and hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年的加密货币前景被描述为充满隐忧——监管加强、骗局横行、能耗与炒作并存。文章劝诫读者保持谨慎，辨别真正的技术进步与市场噪声，评估项目和代币的长期可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.stephendiehl.com/images/sd-social-preview.png' data-full='https://www.stephendiehl.com/images/sd-social-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708941' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48708941' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antoine.fi/mri-analysis-using-claude-code-opus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I used Claude Code to get a second opinion on my MRI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用 Claude Code 为我的 MRI 做了第二意见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708941' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A clinician uses Claude Code to review an MRI, illustrating how AI copilots can augment radiology while raising caveats. This kind of usage emphasizes AI as an assistive, not replacement, tool in clinical workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者用 Claude Code 对 MRI 进行了二次意见的评估，展示了 AI 辅助工具在放射学领域的潜在作用。文中指出了如快速获取第二意见和广泛模式识别等实际好处，同时警示了可靠性、幻觉输出与隐私方面的局限性，强调 AI 仅应作为临床流程中的辅助工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://antoine.fi/rails/active_storage/blobs/proxy/eyJfcmFpbHMiOnsiZGF0YSI6Mzk4LCJwdXIiOiJibG9iX2lkIn19--d3e6efb19dc4b4dc72f3345968d90c00d2d027fb/avatar.jpeg' data-full='https://antoine.fi/rails/active_storage/blobs/proxy/eyJfcmFpbHMiOnsiZGF0YSI6Mzk4LCJwdXIiOiJibG9iX2lkIn19--d3e6efb19dc4b4dc72f3345968d90c00d2d027fb/avatar.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622518' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622518' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marble.onl/posts/cancel_claude.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is minimal downside to switching to open models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>切换到开源模型几乎没有坏处</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 314</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622518' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Switching to open models is argued to reduce risk and vendor lock-in, improving transparency and control for users. The piece adds that practical trade-offs exist—such as ecosystem maturity, tooling, and support—but are manageable for most deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张切换到开源模型可以降低风险并减少对厂商的依赖，提升透明度与可控性。也指出现实中的权衡包括生态成熟度、工具链和支持，但对大多数部署而言是可以管理的。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48639240' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48639240' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>VibeThinker: 3B param model that beats Opus 4.5 on reasoning with novel SFT+GRPO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>VibeThinker：3B 参数模型在推理方面击败 Opus 4.5，采用新颖的 SFT+GRPO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48639240' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>VibeThinker is a 3B-parameter model that reportedly beats Opus 4.5 on reasoning thanks to a novel SFT+GRPO training regime. This challenges the idea that bigger models are needed for strong reasoning and highlights new fine-tuning strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>VibeThinker 是一个 3B 参数模型，据称在推理任务中胜过 Opus 4.5，使用了新颖的 SFT+GRPO 训练方法。这一结果挑战了“大模型才有强推理能力”的直觉，并突出了新的微调策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48674967' data-tags='Hardware,Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48674967' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsroom.ibm.com/2026-06-25-ibm-debuts-worlds-first-sub-1-nanometer-chip-technology' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IBM debuts sub-1 nanometer chip technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IBM 发布亚一纳米芯片技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 387</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 204</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48674967' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>IBM unveiled the world&#x27;s first sub-1-nanometer chip technology, signaling a new scaling frontier for semiconductors. The approach could dramatically improve transistor density and energy efficiency, though practical manufacturing still faces significant challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>IBM 公布全球首个亚一纳米芯片技术，标志半导体在晶体管密度与能效方面迈入新阶段。初步成果显示有望继续推进摩尔定律的扩展，但在实际制造仍面临重大挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://newsroom.ibm.com/image/IBM-Research_Newsroom_Banner_4_Light+%281%29.jpg' data-full='https://newsroom.ibm.com/image/IBM-Research_Newsroom_Banner_4_Light+%281%29.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48686834' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48686834' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/why-have-papers-one-history-s-most-famous-physicists-been-retracted' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Springer Nature has removed two studies by Max Planck</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>斯普林格自然出版社撤回两篇马克斯·普朗克相关研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48686834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48686834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48686834' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Springer Nature has retracted two studies associated with Max Planck. The withdrawals underscore concerns about data integrity and reproducibility in published research, highlighting the ongoing need for rigorous verification of legacy work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>斯普林格自然出版社撤回了与马克斯·普朗克相关的两项研究。原因涉及数据完整性与可重复性问题，凸显对历史研究持续审查及加强结果可靠性的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48647486' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48647486' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dynomight.net/vitamin-d/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The worthlessness of Vitamin D is mildly exaggerated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维生素D的作用被略微夸大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48647486' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven analysis argues that while severe Vitamin D deficiency matters, sweeping claims about supplements&#x27; health benefits are overstated. The article suggests benefits are modest at best and mostly confined to those with clear deficiencies, with sunlight and balanced diet as the primary sources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇基于证据的分析指出，虽然严重缺乏维生素D 的风险值得关注，但关于补充剂的普遍健康效益的夸大说法并不成立。研究显示，除了明确缺乏的人群，补充维生素D 的收益通常有限，日晒和均衡饮食才是关键。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dynomight.net/img/vitamin-d/ap74.37.jpg' data-full='https://dynomight.net/img/vitamin-d/ap74.37.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48703759' data-tags='Entertainment,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48703759' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marfapublicradio.org/podcast/marfa-public-radio-puts-you-to-sleep' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Marfa Public Radio Puts You to Sleep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Marfa Public Radio 让你入睡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 380</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48703759' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Marfa Public Radio experiments with sleep-friendly audio, leaning into long silences and soothing tones to help listeners drift off. This signals a growing niche in audio focused on relaxation and sleep, influencing how stations design bedtime content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Marfa Public Radio 正在尝试睡前友好型节目，通过长时间静默和舒缓声效帮助听众入眠。此趋势反映出音频内容日益聚焦放松与睡眠，可能影响电台和播客的 bedtime 内容设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/c3f0a81/2147483647/strip/true/crop/3000x1575+0+40/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe6%2F56%2F79316759410d8ce45efb655b32b3%2Fmpr-sleep-v2-logo-1.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims4/default/c3f0a81/2147483647/strip/true/crop/3000x1575+0+40/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fe6%2F56%2F79316759410d8ce45efb655b32b3%2Fmpr-sleep-v2-logo-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48653216' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48653216' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/vuln-reports/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vulnerability reports are not special anymore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>漏洞报告已不再特殊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653216' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48653216' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vulnerability reports are no longer rare or special, circulating in volumes that overwhelm maintainers. The post argues for better triage, clearer disclosure processes, and stronger incentives to treat reports as actionable signals rather than novelty. Without refined processes, the value of responsible disclosure risks eroding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>漏洞报告如今大量涌现，已不再具有稀缺性和紧迫性。文章主张需要更高效的分流、明确的披露流程，以及将报告转化为可执行信号的激励机制。若不改进，负责任披露的价值可能受损。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/1ec4ddf5-598f-4d72-8d55-10dc1328aa76.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/1ec4ddf5-598f-4d72-8d55-10dc1328aa76.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48642510' data-tags='DevOps,Security,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48642510' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/aws/run-isolated-sandboxes-with-full-lifecycle-control-aws-lambda-introduces-microvms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MicroVMs: Run isolated sandboxes with full lifecycle control</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroVMs：在完整生命周期控制下运行隔离沙盒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 372</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48642510' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS introduces microVMs to run isolated sandboxes with full lifecycle control, enabling fine-grained isolation for serverless functions and custom runtimes. The approach aims to combine the flexibility of containers with the security of VMs, at the cost of added complexity and startup latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 推出 MicroVMs，允许在完整生命周期控制下运行隔离沙盒，从而为无服务器函数和自定义运行时提供更细粒度的隔离。该技术力图把容器的灵活性与虚拟机的安全性结合起来，但也带来额外的实现复杂度和启动延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/da4b9237bacccdf19c0760cab7aec4a8359010b0/2026/06/22/AdobeStock_44802230-1260x630.jpeg' data-full='https://d2908q01vomqb2.cloudfront.net/da4b9237bacccdf19c0760cab7aec4a8359010b0/2026/06/22/AdobeStock_44802230-1260x630.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48668033' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48668033' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/oauth-for-all/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OAuth for all</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare推出面向所有人的自托管OAuth</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 371</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48668033' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare announces a self-managed OAuth offering deployable on customers&#x27; own infra, enabling organizations to run their own OAuth provider without relying on Cloudflare-hosted services. This reduces vendor lock-in and lets teams apply their own security controls while preserving standard OAuth flows. It signals a trend toward more self-contained identity infrastructure in the SaaS era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出可在客户自有基础设施上部署的自托管 OAuth 方案，企业无须依赖 Cloudflare 托管的服务即可搭建自己的认证提供者。此举有助于降低对单一厂商的依赖，并让团队把安全策略掌控在自己手中，同时保持标准的 OAuth 流程。它体现了在 SaaS 时代对更自主身份基础设施的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6fEPzehWZ8mfroJDIf30mC/636b651767a7cc41dd8b4e7093490a11/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__40_.png' data-full='https://cf-assets.www.cloudflare.com/zkvhlag99gkb/6fEPzehWZ8mfroJDIf30mC/636b651767a7cc41dd8b4e7093490a11/OG_Share_2024-2025-2026__40_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48675435' data-tags='Show HN,Open Source,AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48675435' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/inkeep/open-knowledge' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: OpenKnowledge – open source AI-first alternative to Obsidian/Notion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenKnowledge — 开源 AI 优先的 Obsidian/Notion 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 370</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675435' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48675435' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenKnowledge is an open-source knowledge-management app designed as an AI-first alternative to Obsidian and Notion. It emphasizes local-first data, AI-assisted notes and linking, and user control over privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenKnowledge 是一个开源知识管理应用，定位为 Obsidian/Notion 的 AI 优先替代方案，强调本地优先的数据存储、AI 辅助的笔记与链接，以及对数据隐私的控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ee36f49183eb71d8bf945ea701a3789dd38282a60621c98959e3dc6afd818182/inkeep/open-knowledge' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ee36f49183eb71d8bf945ea701a3789dd38282a60621c98959e3dc6afd818182/inkeep/open-knowledge' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48692946' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48692946' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://physics.stackexchange.com/questions/535/why-does-kinetic-energy-increase-quadratically-not-linearly-with-speed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why does kinetic energy increase quadratically, not linearly, with speed? (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么动能随速度的增加呈二次方增长，而不是线性？（2011）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 366</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692946' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692946' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kinetic energy scales with the square of speed because KE = 1/2 m v^2, a result you can derive from the work-energy principle. From the integral form of work, or using power F·v, the dependence on v^2 emerges, not v. This has practical implications for energy budgeting, braking, and high-speed safety since modest speed changes dramatically affect energy and forces involved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>动能与速度的平方成正比，来自于 KE = 1/2 m v^2 以及功的定义。通过功的积分或功率 F·v 的推导可见，动能随速度的平方增加，而非线性关系。这个关系在能量预算、制动与高速安全等方面具有现实意义，因为速度的微小变化会带来成倍的能量和力的差异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://physics.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/physics/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=f58801408228' data-full='https://physics.stackexchange.com/Content/Sites/physics/Img/apple-touch-icon@2.png?v=f58801408228' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48681687' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48681687' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fernandoi.cl/posts/hackmyclaw/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What happened after 2k people tried to hack my AI assistant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两千人尝试破解我的 AI 助手后的经历</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48681687' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experiment where roughly 2,000 testers tried to hack the author&#x27;s AI assistant, pressing prompts to jailbreak, induce leaks, or extract hidden data. The post analyzes which defenses held—auditing prompts, controlling memory, and limiting tool access—and underscores practical security lessons for deploying chat-based AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇博文记录大约两千人尝试破解作者的 AI 助手，包括诱导提示、越狱式请求和试图提取隐私数据等攻击手段。文中分析哪些防御有效——如严格的提示审计、对记忆的控制，以及对工具访问的限制——并给出将 AI 部署到生产环境中的实际安全教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fernandoi.cl/images/hackmyclaw/opus-prompt-injection.png' data-full='https://www.fernandoi.cl/images/hackmyclaw/opus-prompt-injection.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://labs.iximiuz.com/tutorials/ssh-tunnels' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Practical Guide to SSH Tunnels: Local and Remote Port Forwarding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SSH 隧道实用指南：本地与远程端口转发</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 364</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606222' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a hands-on walkthrough of SSH local and remote port forwarding, with real-world use cases from secure access to services behind firewalls. Includes concrete command examples and tips to avoid common pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供 SSH 本地与远程端口转发的实用指南，涵盖穿透防火墙、通过跳板机访问受限服务等场景，并给出具体命令示例与常见坑点提示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://english.elpais.com/education/2026-06-28/ai-fraud-at-brown-university-academic-integrity-is-at-risk.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Professor denounces mass AI fraud on an exam at Brown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>布朗大学教授谴责考试中的大规模 AI 作弊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708991' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 473</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708991' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Brown University professor condemns widespread AI-assisted cheating on an exam, highlighting threats to academic integrity. The discussion explores policy responses, detection strategies, and the need for robust assessment design in the AI era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布朗大学一位教授谴责在考试中广泛使用 AI 作弊，强调对学术诚信的威胁。讨论探讨了政策应对、检测策略以及在 AI 时代加强评估设计的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patreon.com/DanyBittel/posts/printing-splats-161333338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Printing Gaussian Splats</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>打印高斯斑点（Gaussian Splats）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48618481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48618481' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A creative demonstration of 3D printing Gaussian splats—soft, blob-like shapes based on Gaussian distributions. The project covers generation techniques and practical tips for printing, useful for generative art and data visualization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个展示如何用3D打印实现高斯斑点（Gaussian splats）的创意项目，斑点呈现出柔和的 blob 形状，基于高斯分布生成。文中分享了生成方法和打印技巧，适合生成艺术与数据可视化的应用。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707763' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48707763' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>5k menus from the New York Public Library’s Buttolph Collection (1880-1920)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纽约公共图书馆 Buttolph 收藏的五千份菜单（1880-1920）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48707763' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NYPL’s Buttolph Collection includes around 5,000 menus from 1880–1920, offering a rich dataset for historians and data scientists. Text mining these menus reveals shifts in cuisine, pricing, and social norms across a transformative era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约公共图书馆的 Buttolph 收藏约五千份菜单，覆盖 1880–1920 年代，为历史研究者与数据科学家提供丰富的数据集。通过对菜单中的菜名、价格与语言进行挖掘，可以揭示这一变革时期的饮食、定价与社会风俗的变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/assets/social-facebook.jpg' data-full='https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/assets/social-facebook.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710232' data-tags='Hardware,Privacy,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48710232' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/librepods-org/librepods' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Librepods: AirPods liberated</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Librepods：AirPods 自由化项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710232' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments. The project advocates for ownership and privacy but may raise legal, warranty, and security questions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。该项目倡导拥有权与隐私，但也可能带来法律、保修与安全方面的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/863717537/80b4f2f1-52f9-4c3e-b32a-779933701089' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/863717537/80b4f2f1-52f9-4c3e-b32a-779933701089' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48597221' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48597221' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dahl.dev/posts/my-mathematical-regression/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My Mathematical Regression</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的数学回归</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者反思在数学思考中的转变，认识到纯粹模型的局限并尝试引入直觉或替代方法。文章以回归的隐喻探讨数学实践如何随经验成长与修正。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.interconnects.ai/p/glm-52-is-the-step-change-for-open' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.2 is a step change for open agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.2：开放代理的质变</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48639840' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 represents a significant leap for open AI agents, improving reasoning, planning, and tool-use capabilities in open-weight models. This advancement lowers the barrier to building capable open agents and accelerates community experimentation and innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.2 对开放代理来说是一次显著跃升，提升了推理、计划和工具使用能力的开放模型。这一进步降低了构建强大开放代理的门槛，推动社区在实验与创新方面的进展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Of7h!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F267cdc82-6fbc-4f68-bcba-160091396dfd_3182x1790.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Of7h!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F267cdc82-6fbc-4f68-bcba-160091396dfd_3182x1790.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48642474' data-tags='Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48642474' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/plbrault/youre-the-os' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A game where you&#x27;re an OS and have to manage processes, memory and I/O events</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你就是操作系统的游戏：管理进程、内存与 I/O 事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 334</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48642474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48642474' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This game puts you in the role of an OS kernel, forcing you to schedule processes, manage memory, and handle I/O events. It crystallizes core concepts of OS design and can serve as a hands-on lab for students and hobbyists to grasp how systems orchestrate tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>玩家扮演操作系统内核，需完成进程调度、内存管理与 I/O 事件处理等任务。这款游戏将操作系统设计的核心概念变成直观的实践，适合作为学生和爱好者的动手实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d32cffac676b2f62d3a6524101d12fd5ce0306be6fbbe4fe40ce91890188638e/plbrault/youre-the-os' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d32cffac676b2f62d3a6524101d12fd5ce0306be6fbbe4fe40ce91890188638e/plbrault/youre-the-os' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48653575' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48653575' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/meta-pauses-employee-tracking-program-following-internal-security-breach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Pauses Employee-Tracking Program Following Internal Data Leak</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 暂停员工跟踪计划，因内部数据泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 332</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48653575' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta paused its employee-tracking program after an internal data leak, signaling a pivot toward stricter data governance and privacy considerations. The move underscores how security incidents can reshape corporate policies around monitoring and data access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 因内部数据泄露而暂停了员工跟踪计划，体现公司在隐私与数据治理方面的权衡。这次事件凸显安全事故如何推动监控政策与数据访问的调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a39a2a46c59c72c499e05ba/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/2281617231' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6a39a2a46c59c72c499e05ba/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/2281617231' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48646276' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dshr.org/2026/06/ais-affordability-crisis.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI&#x27;s Affordability Crisis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 的可负担性危机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 415</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48646276' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that AI costs are rising as models grow and infrastructure demands pressure budgets, threatening broad access. It advocates efficiency, open weights, and hardware innovations to keep AI affordable for startups and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出模型规模增大、基础设施成本上升正在拉高 AI 的使用成本，威胁到普及和创新。呼吁通过更高效的模型、开源权重和硬件优化来保持可负担性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEiuTLj-6oHxAz8yvEo1928xM8bxW3K58OrmxbvNnesyd-o37-lY8Pw6fPFmMupStauhJB8xXh6l7FY-rVHwwPqKyBFsI-WLbaXLSytoAuk6FTRcf02DIWUo7ib3FpP5yc0AKOH6XxmNL9gcIr4H8_K5oxDlhU-_voTtMPgbPY-u2OfM4TuEFG5ukArx99S9/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/200B-Question.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEiuTLj-6oHxAz8yvEo1928xM8bxW3K58OrmxbvNnesyd-o37-lY8Pw6fPFmMupStauhJB8xXh6l7FY-rVHwwPqKyBFsI-WLbaXLSytoAuk6FTRcf02DIWUo7ib3FpP5yc0AKOH6XxmNL9gcIr4H8_K5oxDlhU-_voTtMPgbPY-u2OfM4TuEFG5ukArx99S9/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/200B-Question.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48703452' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jayacunzo.com/blog/your-move-chief' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The best response to AI slop and online noise is from Robin Williams</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 AI 乱象与网络噪声的最佳回应来自罗宾·威廉姆斯</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48703452' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that compassionate, high-quality communication is the best antidote to low-effort AI outputs and online noise, drawing on Robin Williams as a model for humane expression. Encourages creativity, empathy, and thoughtful discourse as countermeasures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张以富有同情心的高质量表达来对抗低劣 AI 产出与网络噪声，以罗宾·威廉姆斯为人文表达楷模，强调用创造力、同理心和深思熟虑的讨论来抵御喧嚣。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707673' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48707673' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.engadget.com/2203000/flock-cameras-recording-license-plate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock cameras track more than your license plate, and they&#x27;re spreading fast</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 摄像头不仅记录车牌，还在快速扩张</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 327</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707673' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48707673' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flock Safety&#x27;s license-plate cameras capture more data than just plates, including locations and patterns of movement, as deployments spread rapidly. Privacy advocates warn about mission creep and potential misuse, while municipalities cite public safety benefits and data-sharing concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flock 的车牌识别摄像头不仅记录车牌，还可能捕捉更多数据，且部署范围正在快速扩张。隐私倡议者警告可能的隐私侵蚀与滥用风险，而一些城市则强调公共安全收益与数据共享的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.engadget.com/img/gallery/flock-cameras-track-more-than-your-license-plate-and-theyre-spreading-fast/l-intro-1782508847.jpg' data-full='https://www.engadget.com/img/gallery/flock-cameras-track-more-than-your-license-plate-and-theyre-spreading-fast/l-intro-1782508847.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48630535' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48630535' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://patrickmccanna.net/the-text-in-claude-codes-extended-thinking-output-is-not-authentic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The text in Claude Code’s “Extended Thinking” output</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的“扩展思考”输出中的文本并非真实思考</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630535' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Extended Thinking content appears to be AI-generated text rather than genuine human-like reasoning. This raises concerns about how much we should trust explanations from coding assistants and the need for transparent disclosure of reasoning processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>扩展思考输出看似 AI 生成的文本，而非真实的推理过程。这让人们反思在代码助手中对解释的信任程度，以及是否应透明披露推理过程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://patrickmccanna.net/wp-content/uploads/2017/07/cropped-Trail-1.jpeg' data-full='https://patrickmccanna.net/wp-content/uploads/2017/07/cropped-Trail-1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48691346' data-tags='Legal,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48691346' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kotaku.com/playstation-store-movies-digital-studio-canal-terminator-2000711013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PlayStation Is Deleting 551 Movies from Customers&#x27; Accounts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PlayStation 将账户中的551部电影删除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48691346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 323</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48691346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48691346' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony&#x27;s store removed 551 titles from users&#x27; libraries due to licensing changes; owners may lose access to previously purchased or rented content. The incident highlights fragility of digital ownership when licenses govern access, and it raises questions about refund policies and long-term access guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道索尼因授权变更从用户账户中删除了551部电影，用户可能失去对已购买或租借内容的访问。事件暴露了数字所有权受许可证约束的脆弱性，并引发对退款与长期可访问性保障的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/06/terminator-2-1200x675.jpg' data-full='https://kotaku.com/app/uploads/2026/06/terminator-2-1200x675.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48681220' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48681220' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/framework-10g-ethernet-module-usb-c-complexity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Framework&#x27;s 10G Ethernet module exposes USB-C&#x27;s complexity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Framework 的 10G 以太网模组暴露了 USB-C 的复杂性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 319</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48681220' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Framework 10G Ethernet module highlights the intricate realities of USB-C, from power delivery and alternate modes to lane negotiation and cable quality. This setup exposes edge cases and dependency on spec compliance, underscoring cautions for hardware designers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Framework 的 10G 以太网模组凸显了 USB-C 的复杂性，涉及供电协商、替代模式、信道映射和线缆品质等多重要素。此举暴露了把高带宽网络接到 USB-C 时的边缘情况和对规范遵从的强依赖，对硬件设计者具有重要警示意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/framework-10g-ethernet-module-usb-c-complexity/wisdpi-framework-10g-module.jpeg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/framework-10g-ethernet-module-usb-c-complexity/wisdpi-framework-10g-module.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48635112' data-tags='Career,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48635112' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://urflow.bearblog.dev/jobs-and-software-is-fucked/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jobs and Software Is Fucked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>工作与软件都完蛋了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48635112' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that the current state of jobs and software is dire, with automation, outsourcing, and platform-driven work reshaping career paths and productivity. The piece contends that the status quo is unsustainable and urges a rethink of how work is rewarded, how engineers adapt to tooling-centric economies, and the policy landscape around tech jobs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章指出，自动化、外包和平台驱动的工作模式正在重塑职业路径与生产力，导致就业生态趋于不稳定。作者呼吁重新思考工作回报、技能提升以及相关政策，以适应以工具为核心的经济格局。核心在于做好应对更严峻就业市场、推动再培训和政策调整的准备。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630171' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 200M-parameter image inpainting model claims performance on par with much larger models. If validated, it demonstrates dramatic efficiency gains from architectural improvements and training tricks, making high-quality inpainting more accessible on consumer hardware. This could shift how we deploy AI-assisted image editing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个约 2 亿参数的图像修复模型声称达到 10B 参数级别的性能。若属实，这意味着通过架构改进和训练技巧实现更高效的修复能力，使 AI 辅助的图像编辑在普通硬件上更易落地，可能改变部署方式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/madison-square-garden-made-dossier-on-activists-who-opposed-facial-recognition/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Madison Square Garden compiled a list of activists against facial recognition</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48644781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48644781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48644781' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Madison Square Garden compiled a dossier on activists who opposed facial recognition, signaling how venues can track organizing and potentially deter dissent. The move raises privacy, civil-liberties concerns and highlights the chilling effect of corporate surveillance on protest movements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>麦迪逊广场花园对反对人脸识别的活动人士编制档案，显示场馆也在对抗议者进行追踪。此举引发隐私、言论自由与冷却效应的担忧，暴露企业监控对社会运动的潜在压制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48700834' data-tags='Security,Privacy,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48700834' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipcrawl.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IP Crawl: Living atlas of open webcams discovered on the public internet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IP Crawl：公开互联网可访问摄像头的活地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48700834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48700834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48700834' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An atlas of publicly exposed webcams that is continually updated as devices come online or go offline. The project highlights privacy and security risks from misconfigurations and weak defenses on the internet, and shows how researchers can map this surface at scale. It underscores why internet facing devices need better defaults and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目持续绘制公开暴露在互联网上的摄像头，设备上线或下线时不断更新。此现象暴露出因配置错误或安全防护薄弱而带来的隐私与安全风险，展示了如何以可扩展的方式映射这一区域。它强调需要改进网络设备的默认设置和持续监控。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-25/apple-to-skip-high-end-m6-mac-chips-to-launch-m7-pro-m7-max-m7-ultra-instead?embedded-checkout=true' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple to skip high-end M6 Mac chips in favor of AI-focused M7 line</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果将跳过高端 M6 芯片，转而推出以 AI 为核心的 M7 系列</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48676795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48676795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48676795' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple plans to skip the high-end M6 chips in favor of an AI-focused M7 family (Pro/Max/Ultra). The move signals a pivot to heavy AI workloads on Mac hardware, likely incorporating stronger ML accelerators and memory bandwidth to support on-device inference and large-model workflows, with implications for developers targeting macOS.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果计划跳过高端 M6 芯片，改为以 AI 为核心的 M7 系列（Pro/Max/Ultra）。这一举动表明将把 Mac 硬件定位于高强度 AI 工作负载，可能集成更强的机器学习加速单元与更高的内存带宽，为本地推理和大模型工作流提供支持，对开发者生态有重要影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48672764' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48672764' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mullvad.net/en/why-privacy-matters/state-mass-surveillance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Countries are competing to see which can carry out mass surveillance the best</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>各国竞相比拼大规模监控能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 309</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48672764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48672764' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>States are racing to deploy more extensive mass surveillance systems, trading privacy protections for perceived security gains. The race is shaping technologies, legal regimes, and oversight, with broad implications for civil liberties and global norms around surveillance. Privacy advocates warn that the move risks normalizing pervasive monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>各国竞相部署更加广泛的大规模监控系统，以换取看似更高的安全收益。此竞争在技术、法律框架和监督机制上形成影响，给公民自由和全球监控治理规范带来深远影响。隐私倡导者警告，这类做法可能让普遍化监控成为新常态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mullvad.net/press/MullvadVPN_logo_Round_RGB_Color_positive.png' data-full='https://mullvad.net/press/MullvadVPN_logo_Round_RGB_Color_positive.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://commerce.jolla.com/products/jolla-phone-october-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jolla Phone (October 2026)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48687272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48687272' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48687272' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jolla unveils a follow‑up smartphone running Sailfish OS, emphasizing user control and open ecosystem over mainstream app store lock‑in. Expect privacy‑conscious design, modular features, and continued focus on European hardware philosophy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jolla 发布新款手机，搭载 Sailfish OS，强调对用户的控制和开放生态，避开主流应用商店的锁定。具备隐私导向的设计、模块化功能，以及延续对欧洲硬件理念的坚持。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622626' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622626' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexkritchevsky.com/2026/05/25/everything-is-logarithms.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everything is logarithms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一切都是对数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622626' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622626' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many real-world relationships reveal linear structure when plotted on a log scale, making comparisons across orders of magnitude practical. The piece connects this idea to data visualization, ML, and decision-making, arguing that the right scale reveals hidden patterns. Embracing logarithmic thinking can sharpen models and dashboards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>许多现实世界的关系在对数坐标下呈现线性，使跨数量级的比较更加实用。本文将这一观念与数据可视化、机器学习和决策制定联系起来，强调选择合适的尺度能揭示隐藏的模式。掌握对数思维有助于提升建模与仪表盘的清晰度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48662381' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xteink 的新款 X4 电子墨水阅读器致力于提升对比度与可读性，兼具低功耗与潜在的笔记功能，展示了电子纸设备在平板主导的市场中的持续可行性与广度。</p>
