<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48692058' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48692058' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.doubleword.ai/frontier-os-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The gap between open weights LLMs and closed source LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源权重大模型与闭源大模型之间的差距</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 299</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 239</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692058' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An assessment of how open-weight LLMs stack up against closed-source models, highlighting capability gaps, safety tooling, and ecosystem maturity. It notes progress toward parity but emphasizes ongoing tradeoffs around licensing, support, and governance that affect adoption by developers and researchers. The takeaway is that the choice of model architecture and access model shapes who can build, customize, and deploy advanced AI today.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比开源权重的大模型与闭源大模型的能力、安全工具和生态成熟度，指出差距在缩小但仍然存在。对开发者在许可证、支持与治理方面的权衡进行了强调，展示了模型可用性如何影响定制与落地创新。核心信息是访问形式和实现细节决定了谁能在现实世界中部署先进的AI。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/aa16655ef76925dff4822424ce01557f018c1290-800x800.png?w=96&amp;h=96' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/g1zo7y59/production/aa16655ef76925dff4822424ce01557f018c1290-800x800.png?w=96&amp;h=96' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48657049' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48657049' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dev.karltryggvason.com/you-cant-unit-test-for-taste/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can&#x27;t unit test for taste</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你不能用单元测试来评估口味</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657049' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48657049' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that taste is subjective and utterly not amenable to unit tests. The piece suggests focusing on objective signals and user outcomes instead of chasing &#x27;favorable&#x27; taste in software design, as taste evolves with context.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章直指口味本质的主观性，认为无法通过单元测试来衡量。作者建议在软件设计中以客观信号和用户实际效果为核心，而不是去追求所谓的“好口味”，因为口味会随情境改变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dev.karltryggvason.com/you-cant-unit-test-for-taste/itlr-poi-screenshot.png' data-full='https://dev.karltryggvason.com/you-cant-unit-test-for-taste/itlr-poi-screenshot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48614715' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48614715' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/business/media/polymarket-social-media-bets-prediction-market-441cdeb5?st=HhTZY2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Polymarket has flooded social media with deceptive videos by paid creators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Polymarket 用付费创作者制作的误导性视频充斥社媒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 293</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48614715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48614715' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket has flooded social media with deceptive videos created by paid creators, raising concerns about misinformation and platform governance. The development underscores the risks of monetized, manipulated content in decentralized social ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket 通过付费创作者制作的误导性视频充斥社交媒体，引发对错讯信息与平台治理的担忧。这反映出去中心化社交生态中货币化、操控内容的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48699928' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48699928' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cauenapier.com/blog/townsquare_release/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn your site into a place people can bump into each other</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把你的网站变成让人们偶遇的场所</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 291</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48699928' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new project lets websites become spaces for serendipitous encounters, letting visitors bump into each other in shared virtual spaces or via presence features. It aims to foster real-world community and casual interactions, though privacy and moderation concerns for site operators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新项目让网站成为可供用户偶遇的场所，通过共享空间和在场感知功能促进用户的意外相遇。它旨在培养在线社区的随遇而安的互动，但也带来隐私与内容监管的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/by-invitation/2026/06/02/how-the-war-on-terror-primed-america-for-autocracy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The war on terror primed America for autocracy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>恐怖主义战争为美国铺路走向专制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 289</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48654367' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The war on terror is framed as a driver of American autocratic tendencies, with long-standing implications for civil liberties and government power. The piece argues that security regimes have hardened centralized authority, shaping policy beyond the battlefield.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章将恐怖主义战争视为推动美国走向专制的因素，讨论对公民自由与政府权力的长期影响。作者认为安全体制强化了集中的权力，影响超越前线战争的政策走向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.the3dprintingnerd.com/ab2047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California AB 2047 makes 3D printers off-limits to students, educators, business</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48652184' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California AB 2047 imposes strict restrictions on 3D printing access across schools, educators, and businesses, affecting curriculum, product development, and innovation pipelines. Proponents cite safety concerns or IP protection; critics warn of stifling hands-on STEM education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州 AB 2047 对学校、教育工作者和企业在3D 打印方面设限，可能削弱动手学习和快速原型开发。支持者强调安全与知识产权保护，反对者担忧对STEM教育的负面影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48681949' data-tags='Open Source,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48681949' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://graphicore.github.io/librebarcode/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Libre Barcode Project</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Libre Barcode 项目</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 282</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48681949' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48681949' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Libre Barcode Project offers open-source barcode fonts and related tooling, enabling free and permissive use in printing and software without proprietary licensing. By standardizing common barcode symbologies like Code 128 and EAN/UPC, it lowers cost and friction for startups, educators, and public sector deployments, while inviting community contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Libre Barcode 项目提供开源条码字体与相关工具，使印刷和软件使用不再受限于专有许可。通过标准化常用的条码符号（如 Code 128 与 EAN/UPC），它为初创公司、教育机构和公共部门降低成本和使用门槛，同时欢迎社区贡献。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Slate Auto unveils an electric pickup with a base price of 24,950 dollars, signaling a push toward affordable EVs in the pickup segment. The aggressive pricing could force rivals to compete on price, efficiency, and features, potentially accelerating broader EV adoption in work-ready trucks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Slate Auto 推出起价为 24,950 美元的电动皮卡，标志着在皮卡市场向平价电动车的推进。这样的定价可能迫使竞争对手在价格、续航与配置上展开竞争，推动工作用皮卡领域的电动化进程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ziglang.org/devlog/2026/#2026-06-25' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig&#x27;s new bitCast semantics and LLVM back end improvements</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Zig language project updates BitCast semantics and LLVM backend. The changes aim to make bit-level casts safer and more predictable while improving code generation and optimization on the LLVM backend, helping Zig reach more production-grade use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发团队对 Zig 的 BitCast 语义进行了更新，并对 LLVM 后端进行了改进。修改旨在让位运算强制转换更安全、行为更可预测，同时提升 LLVM 后端的代码生成和优化能力，推动 Zig 在生产环境中的应用。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/06/streaming-services-obnoxiously-loud-ads-become-illegal-on-july-1-in-california/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Streaming services&#x27; obnoxiously loud ads become illegal on July 1 in California</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州7月1日起，流媒体广告音量过高将违法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48697768' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California will ban excessively loud ads on streaming services starting July 1. The measure requires ads to match the volume of the content they accompany, pressuring platforms to normalize audio and adjust measurement pipelines. The change could remake ad-ops workflows and improve viewer comfort across services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州将于7月1日起禁止流媒体广告音量过高，要求广告与节目声量保持一致。该规定迫使平台调整音量归一化和监测流程，影响广告运营和合规成本。此举有望提升用户观看体验，同时影响广告技术生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-1987915833-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-1987915833-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48660267' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660267' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/nubjs/nub' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Nub – A Bun-like all-in-one toolkit for Node.js</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Nub——一个 Bun 风格的 Node.js 全能工具包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660267' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660267' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nub is a Bun-like toolkit for Node.js, providing a single binary with ecosystem utilities, bundling, package management, and more. If adopted, it could reshape local dev environments and reduce dependency friction for Node developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nub 将 Node.js 开发环境整合成一个 Bun 风格的工具包，包含运行时、包管理和常用开发工具，简化本地环境。若获得广泛采用，可能降低依赖冲突、提升上手速度，并推动 Node 生态的工具链整合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4131c5af915fb12c608755b345dfa0aea315d3d12c6fc8656dfd8a880a4427d0/nubjs/nub' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4131c5af915fb12c608755b345dfa0aea315d3d12c6fc8656dfd8a880a4427d0/nubjs/nub' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48702273' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48702273' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evilbit.de/dns-resolver-guide.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Choosing a Public DNS Resolver</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>选择公共 DNS 解析器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 125</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48702273' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The guide dissects factors in choosing a public DNS resolver—privacy, performance, and security trade-offs—against different providers&#x27; policies and features. It helps readers balance speed with control over who can see their queries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南比较了公共 DNS 解析器在隐私、性能和安全方面的取舍，帮助读者在速度与可控性之间做权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48648039' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48648039' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/news/introducing-claude-tag' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Tag</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Tag 标签</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 266</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648039' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Tag is a new feature that allows tagging and annotation of model outputs to improve safety, auditability, and governance of AI interactions. It enables better traceability for decisions and facilitates compliance workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Tag 是新推出的标签功能，允许对模型输出进行标记以提升安全性、可审计性与治理效果。该工具有助于追溯决策过程并简化合规流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/b9ba85fd4d8beaf4efe04a4cf6cec14761e52c78-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/b9ba85fd4d8beaf4efe04a4cf6cec14761e52c78-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710092' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48710092' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dam.stanford.edu/memory-prices.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Historical memory prices 1960-2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1960–2026 的内存价格历史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710092' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Stanford memory prices history chart tracks the long-run decline in memory prices, including RAM and storage. The data show dramatic declines over decades, with spikes tied to supply shocks and tech cycles, informing hardware budgeting and product lifecycles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份数据集揭示了记忆体（如 RAM）价格在 1960 年到 2026 年的长期下降趋势，并受供给冲击与科技周期的影响出现波动。这些数据为硬件预算与产品生命周期提供了参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dam.stanford.edu/img/dam-logo-short.png' data-full='https://dam.stanford.edu/img/dam-logo-short.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48635954' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48635954' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spur.us/blog/smart-tv-apps-residential-proxy-sdks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nearly half of LG smart TV apps contain residential proxy SDKs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>近半数 LG 智能电视应用内嵌住宅代理 SDK</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635954' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48635954' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation finds that nearly half of LG smart TV apps embed residential proxy SDKs, enabling user traffic to be routed through proxy networks. This raises privacy, security, and potential misuse concerns for both users and developers within the smart-TV app ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调查显示，近一半的 LG 智能电视应用内嵌住宅代理 SDK，可能将用户流量导至代理网络，带来隐私与安全风险，以及对开发者和平台治理的挑战。这揭示了智能电视生态在第三方 SDK 供应链中的治理难题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/b9pzoci3/production/2adc1982dab065cba0b11ab1021585c8aafb239a-1000x1000.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/b9pzoci3/production/2adc1982dab065cba0b11ab1021585c8aafb239a-1000x1000.jpg?w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48714529' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48714529' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Age verification is just a precursor to automated attribution of speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>年龄验证只是自动化语音归因的前提</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48714529' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech. As identity becomes tied to voice data, safeguards around privacy, consent, and misuse become critical, with implications for policy and AI system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。当声音数据与身份绑定时，隐私、同意和滥用防护就变得尤为关键，对政策和 AI 系统设计都会产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致敬 memcached</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48638886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48638886' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 102</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48638886' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memcached is celebrated for its enduring simplicity and low-latency caching, proving surprisingly effective at scale. The piece shares practical patterns and caveats for production use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Memcached 凭借简洁性和低延迟缓存能力在大规模场景中依然有效。文中给出实际使用中的模式与注意事项。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48660579' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660579' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.greptile.com/blog/prs-on-openclaw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PR spam today looks like email spam in the early 2000s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PR 垃圾邮件如今看起来像 2000 年代初的邮件垃圾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 262</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660579' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PR outreach today mirrors early-2000s email spam, with generic, mass-targeted pitches and clickbait subject lines. The piece argues that quality, relevance, and user respect in PR is eroding, creating noise that developers and journalists skip.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>如今的公关推送像上世纪初的邮件垃圾一样泛滥、缺乏针对性，充满低质的标题与大规模群发。文章指出，PR 的质量与对用户的尊重正在下降，造成开发者和记者对信息的忽略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://greptile.com/opengraph/greptile-blog.png' data-full='https://greptile.com/opengraph/greptile-blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48674096' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48674096' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/0f067265-2baf-4b6e-8fb2-ed56daef6f3c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple increases MacBook and iPad prices by 20%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果将MacBook和iPad价格上涨约20%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48674096' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple lifts prices for MacBook and iPad by about 20%, reflecting cost pressures and premium branding. The move pressures buyers in a competitive laptop-tablet market and underscores Apple&#x27;s focus on protecting margins amid inflation and supply chain dynamics. It signals continued resilience of premium hardware demand despite macro headwinds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果将MacBook与iPad的价格上调约20%，显示成本压力与高端品牌定位。此举可能压缩在竞争激烈的笔记本和平板市场中的购买力，并凸显苹果在通胀与供应链环境中的利润保护策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F10ccfde3-e441-47a1-b78c-58c8d5da34c0.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' data-full='https://images.ft.com/v3/image/raw/https%3A%2F%2Fd1e00ek4ebabms.cloudfront.net%2Fproduction%2F10ccfde3-e441-47a1-b78c-58c8d5da34c0.jpg?source=next-barrier-page' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48654676' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48654676' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/baiyibai/pico-usb-wifi' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Raspberry Pi Pico W as USB Wi-Fi Adapter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 Raspberry Pi Pico W 作为 USB 无线网卡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48654676' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project shows Pico W can act as a USB Wi-Fi adapter, enabling a host computer to use the Pico as a network interface. This demonstrates flexible USB gadget capabilities of RP2040 and opens DIY routes for portable networking or offline tethering. It is a handy trick for hobbyists building tiny, portable networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该项目展示了 Pico W 作为 USB 无线网卡工作，使主机可以通过 Pico 的 USB 端口获得网络接口。展示了 RP2040 的 USB gadget 能力，为便携网络和离线共享等场景提供 DIY 方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' data-full='https://gitlab.com/assets/twitter_card-570ddb06edf56a2312253c5872489847a0f385112ddbcd71ccfa1570febab5d2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48698151' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48698151' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/discontinuities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suspicious Discontinuities (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可疑的不连续性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48698151' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48698151' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Performance plots often hide abrupt jumps caused by microarchitectural effects and scheduling quirks; such discontinuities challenge assumptions in benchmarking and optimization. The piece urges deeper measurement discipline and contextual understanding of hardware when tuning systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>性能曲线中的微观架构效应和调度差异常常导致突然而显著的跳变，给基准测试和优化带来挑战。文章呼吁在调优系统时进行更深入的测量与硬件背景理解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danluu.com/images/discontinuities/pell-2008.jpg' data-full='https://danluu.com/images/discontinuities/pell-2008.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48697958' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/27/asian-ai-startups-launch-mythos-like-models-as-anthropics-export-ban-drags-on/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asian AI startups launch Mythos-like models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚洲 AI 初创推出类似 Mythos 的模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697958' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48697958' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Several Asian startups roll out Mythos-like models as export bans bite, signaling a rapid regional AI ecosystem maturation. The shift hints at localized data, compute constraints, and a push to reduce dependence on Western platforms, potentially reshaping global AI competition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多家亚洲初创推出与 Mythos 相似的大模型，以应对出口禁令的冲击，表明区域 AI 生态系统快速成熟。此举折射出本地数据与算力的压力，以及减少对西方平台依赖的努力，可能重塑全球 AI 竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2278736523.jpeg?resize=1200,798' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2278736523.jpeg?resize=1200,798' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48660021' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660021' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/ai-radio-chip-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI learns the “dark art” of RFIC design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 学习 RFIC 设计的“黑暗艺术”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660021' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems are now tackling RFIC design, optimizing tradeoffs in noise, linearity, and parasitics that are hard for humans to hand-tune. The approach could accelerate RFIC development and push performance, but verification and explainability challenges remain as designs become increasingly opaque.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 系统现正参与射频集成电路设计，在噪声、线性度和寄生效应等方面进行高难度的优化。此举可能加速设计进程、提升性能，但验证、可解释性等问题需警惕，因为设计过程越来越不透明。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.png?id=67001745&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C255%2C0%2C244' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.png?id=67001745&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C255%2C0%2C244' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48658300' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/23/us/politics/nsa-lost-access-anthropic-tool.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NSA lost access to Mythos amid Anthropic dispute</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NSA 在与 Anthropic 的纠纷中失去 Mythos 访问权限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658300' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amid a dispute with Anthropic, the NSA reportedly lost access to Mythos, a tool used for AI-assisted reasoning. The incident highlights tensions around government use of private AI services, data sovereignty, and potential gaps in access for national security work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在与 Anthropic 的纠纷中，NSA 据报失去对 Mythos 的访问权限，凸显政府对私有 AI 服务的使用、数据主权及敏感任务获取权限的矛盾与挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/windows-10-quietly-gets-one-more-year-of-support-and-updates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 10 quietly gets one more year of support and updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 10再获一年官方支持与更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48675369' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows 10 has been granted another year of support and updates, extending its life for organizations not ready to migrate to Windows 11. The extension highlights slow enterprise upgrade cycles and the ongoing coexistence of legacy systems with newer platforms. It also buys time for businesses with custom ecosystems built around Windows 10.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 10再获一年官方支持与更新，帮助尚未迁移到Windows 11的企业维持系统稳定。此延期凸显企业升级节奏放缓，以及旧平台与新平台并存的现实。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48596488' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(138) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://plotnine.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plotnine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Plotnine 数据可视化库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48596488' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plotnine is a Python implementation of the grammar of graphics, offering a ggplot2-like API for layered, declarative visualizations. It enables composing plots with aesthetics, geoms, and facets, making complex charting accessible without heavy matplotlib boilerplate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plotnine 是一个在 Python 中实现的图形语法，提供类似 ggplot2 的分层、声明式绘图 API。通过美学映射、几何对象与分面等组件，便于构建复杂图表，减少对 matplotlib 的冗长代码。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48662999' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48662999' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/introducing-computer-use-gemini-3-5-flash/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer use in Gemini 3.5 Flash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.5 Flash 引入“计算机使用”能力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662999' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 167</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48662999' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Gemini 3.5 Flash adds a &#x27;computer use&#x27; capability, letting the model interact with a host environment to run commands, access files, and call tools. This expands AI agents from theory to action, boosting productivity while expanding safety and privacy considerations. It signals a broader trend toward tool-using assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.5 Flash 增强了“使用计算机”的能力，使模型能够在宿主环境中运行命令、访问文件和调用工具。这让 AI 代理从单纯的推理走向对外部世界的实际行动，同时也带来安全与隐私方面的新挑战。这标志着更强大、能调用外部工具的 AI 助手正成为趋势。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48659165' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reflects on the craft of stealing as a skill, analyzing how attackers cultivate methods and how defenders can adapt. The piece emphasizes practical lessons for security professionals and the importance of empathy to understand attacker mindset.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章从攻击者角度探讨盗窃这门技能，分析他们如何培养手段以及防守方如何应对。强调对安全从业者的现实教训以及理解攻击者思维的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(141) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/business/qualcomm-buy-ai-startup-modular-2026-06-24/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qualcomm to Acquire Modular</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>高通将收购 Modular</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659798' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48659798' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qualcomm plans to acquire Modular, an AI startup focused on modular software for on-device or edge AI. The deal expands Qualcomm&#x27;s AI software and hardware stack, aiming to boost on-device inference and system integration. It underscores a trend of chipmakers consolidating to offer end-to-end edge AI solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高通计划收购专注于在设备端运行的模组化 AI 软件的初创公司 Modular。此次交易扩展了高通在 AI 软件与硬件的整合能力，旨在提升设备端推理性能和系统协同。此举反映芯片厂商在边缘 AI 解决方案上的并购整合趋势。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48679934' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/06/military-branches-restore-flu-shot-requirement-after-virus-swept-through-base/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Military branches restore flu shot requirement after virus swept through base</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>军方各基地在疫情蔓延后恢复流感疫苗接种要求</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48679934' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After a virus swept through bases, military branches reinstated flu shot requirements to protect personnel and readiness. The change underscores ongoing prioritization of vaccination in large, centralized organizations and how outbreaks influence policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在基地疫情蔓延后，军方各分支恢复了流感疫苗接种要求，以保护人员健康与备战。此举显示大规模、集中的机构仍将疫苗接种视为关键的健康与安全策略，疫情也推动了相关政策调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2224665122-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2224665122-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48628731' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48628731' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.washingtonpost.com/obituaries/2026/06/22/alan-greenspan-most-powerful-central-banker-modern-times-dies-100/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alan Greenspan has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾伦·格林斯潘去世</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48628731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48628731' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 227</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48628731' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alan Greenspan, the influential long-time chair of the Federal Reserve, has died at age 100. His tenure shaped monetary policy through decades of inflation dynamics, globalization, and deregulation, leaving a lasting mark on macroeconomics and financial markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深美联储主席艾伦·格林斯潘去世，享年100岁。他的任期深刻影响了多年来的货币政策、通胀与全球化进程，以及金融市场的演变，对宏观经济学与市场格局产生持续影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622788' data-tags='Health,Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622788' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/06/fda-advisors-unanimously-vote-to-approve-modernas-mrna-after-agency-drama/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FDA advisors unanimously vote to approve Moderna&#x27;s mRNA after agency drama</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FDA 顾问因此一致通过 Moderna 的 mRNA 疫苗，经历机构风波</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622788' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FDA advisory panel unanimously approved Moderna&#x27;s mRNA vaccine after a period of regulatory drama, signaling progress for the company&#x27;s mRNA portfolio. The outcome illustrates the tension between science advisory processes and agency decisions, with implications for rollout timelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FDA 专家组在经历监管风波后的一致意见通过了 Moderna 的 mRNA 疫苗审批，标志着其 mRNA 组合的进展。此结果反映了科学顾问过程与监管决策之间的博弈，对后续推广时间表具有影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/GettyImages-1290558683.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2020/12/GettyImages-1290558683.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48648779' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48648779' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://swiftpackageindex.com/blog/swift-package-index-joins-apple' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swift Package Index joins Apple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Swift Package Index 与 Apple 合作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648779' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swift Package Index, a community-maintained registry of Swift packages, has joined Apple to broaden its integration with Apple developer tooling. The move could streamline package discovery and consistency across the Swift ecosystem, accelerating adoption on Apple platforms and easing maintenance for package authors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为社区维护的 Swift 包注册表，Swift Package Index 宣布加入苹果，旨在加强与苹果开发工具链的整合。此举可能提升包的发现性和跨生态的一致性，帮助开发者在苹果平台上更快采用 Swift，并简化包作者的维护工作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48610137' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A newly unearthed handwritten notebook from Mozart, dated when he was 22, promises fresh insights into his compositional process and early works.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>挖掘出22岁时莫扎特的手写笔记，为研究他的创作过程与早期作品提供新线索。该发现料将推动学者和演绎者重新解读其音乐文本。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48660839' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48660839' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techstrong.ai/articles/for-most-of-the-world-open-source-ai-is-the-only-way-forward/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For most of the world, open-source AI is the only way forward</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对于全球大多数地区，开源 AI 是唯一出路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 233</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48660839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48660839' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that open-source AI is essential for most of the world due to access constraints and sovereignty concerns around proprietary models. Open-source ecosystems enable local deployment, customization, and resilient AI tooling where access to commercial providers is restricted or controlled.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，由于对专有模型的获取限制与数据主权考量，开源 AI 对全球大多数地区至关重要。开源生态系统使本地部署、定制化和韧性 AI 工具成为可能，尤其在依赖商业提供商受限的场景下。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techstrong.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/open_source_ai_global_connectivity_770x330.jpg' data-full='https://techstrong.ai/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/open_source_ai_global_connectivity_770x330.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48658647' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/06/24/reid-hoffman-spacex-musk-openai-anthropic-gen-z-mistake/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reid Hoffman says SpaceX &#x27;not an AI company&#x27;, xAI &#x27;complete train wreck&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里德·霍夫曼：SpaceX 不是 AI 公司，xAI 完全灾难</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 232</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658647' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Venture investor Reid Hoffman criticized SpaceX as not an AI company and described xAI as a &#x27;complete train wreck&#x27;, signaling skepticism toward Musk-backed AI ventures. The remarks amplify discussions about who leads AI innovation and how capital allocators evaluate ambitious AI bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>风险投资人里德·霍夫曼批评 SpaceX 不是 AI 公司，称 xAI 为“完全灾难”，对马斯克旗下的 AI 投资表达了怀疑。这一评价加剧了关于 AI 创新主导者和投资者如何评估高风险 AI 项目的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2206159992-e1782250620624.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/GettyImages-2206159992-e1782250620624.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48647444' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://finance.yahoo.com/markets/currencies/articles/ecb-secures-key-parliamentary-backing-102718449.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Digital euro clears key hurdle as EU seeks to break free from U.S. credit cards</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数字欧元通过关键门槛，欧盟寻求摆脱对美国信用卡的依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48647444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 375</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48647444' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The digital euro has cleared a significant regulatory hurdle, advancing an EU-wide alternative to US-issued card rails. The effort targets faster cross-border payments, privacy, and strategic autonomy from US payment networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数字欧元获得关键立法进展，欧盟推动建立统一的数字货币以减少对美国信用卡网络的依赖。此举意在提升跨境支付效率、隐私保护与金融主权。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s.yimg.com/os/en/reuters-finance.com/433caafdaded4d5d25db94317076a334' data-full='https://s.yimg.com/os/en/reuters-finance.com/433caafdaded4d5d25db94317076a334' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48680370' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48680370' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gchandbook.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Garbage Collection Handbook: The Art of Automatic Memory Management (2nd Ed) (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>垃圾回收手册：自动内存管理的艺术（第2版）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48680370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 231</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48680370' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48680370' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Garbage Collection Handbook (2nd ed., 2023) is a thorough reference on automatic memory management, detailing garbage collection algorithms, memory models, and practical tuning across languages. It surveys tracing, reference counting, generational collectors, and real-world trade-offs, equipping developers to optimize latency, throughput, and memory safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本《垃圾回收手册》是对自动内存管理的全面指南，涵盖垃圾回收算法、内存模型以及跨语言的实用调优。书中比较了 tracing、引用计数、代际收集等不同方案的优劣，帮助开发者在性能、延迟和内存安全之间做出权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gchandbook.org/images/gc-logo.png' data-full='https://gchandbook.org/images/gc-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48664063' data-tags='Science,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48664063' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S1877959X26000476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reducing tick density along recreational trails in Ottawa, Canada</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在加拿大渥太华的休闲步道降低蜱虫密度</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664063' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48664063' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study tests strategies to reduce tick density along Ottawa&#x27;s recreational trails, with results suggesting habitat management and targeted interventions can lower tick exposure and disease risk for park-goers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究在渥太华的休闲步道实施干预，以降低蜱虫密度，从而降低步行者的莱姆病等风险。结果表明一些栖息地管理和有针对性的措施能有效降低暴露率，并为公园和公共卫生项目提供指导。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48666100' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48666100' aria-label='Show summary and image'>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Elastic announced layoffs totaling about 7% of its employees as it reshapes the company toward profitability and cloud-centric growth. The leadership aims to reduce operating costs, simplify the org, and accelerate product delivery, with some roles impacted across functions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Elastic 宣布裁减约 7% 的员工，以重塑为更偏向云端的增长模式并提升盈利能力。管理层表示将精简运营、加速产品交付，裁员涉及多个职能领域的岗位。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48678495' data-tags='Security,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48678495' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rozumem.xyz/posts/17' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Doorman&#x27;s Fallacy in action</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>看门人谬论在现实中的应用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 230</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48678495' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Doorman&#x27;s Fallacy in action shows how a single gatekeeper or simplistic access check can give a false sense of security, leaving deeper flaws unchecked. It uses real-world examples—security design, UX, and policy—to illustrate why layering, threat modeling, and provenance matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>看门人谬论在现实中的应用揭示了依赖单一门控或过于简单的访问检查，可能让人产生虚假的安全感而忽略更深层的漏洞。通过真实世界的例子，说明层次化防护、威胁建模和来源可追溯性的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.davidrevoy.com/article1154/why-drawing-tablet-brands-wont-collaborate-on-linux-floss-drivers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Drawing Tablet Brands Won&#x27;t Collaborate on Linux Floss Drivers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么绘图平板品牌不会在 Linux FLOSS 驱动上合作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48629064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48629064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48629064' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that drawing tablet vendors rarely collaborate on open-source Linux drivers due to competitive, licensing, and IP concerns, leaving users with fragmented support. It discusses the impact on developers and the broader FLOSS ecosystem and possible workarounds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为绘图平板厂商很少在 Linux 的开源驱动上合作，原因包括商业竞争、许可与知识产权等因素，导致用户获得的驱动支持分散。并分析对开发者和开源生态的影响，以及可能的解决路径。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《陀思妥耶夫斯基》其实并不难读</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631366' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues Dostoyevsky isn&#x27;t as hard to read as common wisdom suggests, offering practical reading approaches, translation choices, and pacing to make the novels approachable. The piece lowers barriers for readers and students to tackle classic literature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张《陀思妥耶夫斯基》其实并不如人们想象中那么难读，提供可行的阅读策略、译本选择与节奏建议，帮助读者更易接触经典文学。文章旨在降低门槛，鼓励读者和学生去阅读。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48631888' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48631888' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://role-confusion.github.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prompt Injection as Role Confusion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将提示注入描述为角色混淆的视角</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631888' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48631888' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new framing for prompt injection treats attacker prompts as role-confusion, where the model misinterprets instruction as part of its role. This reframing helps analyze security boundaries in AI apps and suggests mitigations around system prompts and role constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将提示注入描述为角色混淆的视角，指出攻击者的指令可能被模型错误地纳入其角色定义中。此分析有助于理解 AI 应用的安全边界，并提出对系统提示和角色约束的缓解策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://role-confusion.github.io/assets/figures/role-perception.png' data-full='https://role-confusion.github.io/assets/figures/role-perception.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707769' data-tags='Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48707769' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbsnews.com/detroit/news/workplace-boundaries-act-employees-after-hours/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Michigan bill would bar employers from requiring after-hours coms with workers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>密歇根州法案拟禁止雇主在工作时间外沟通</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48707769' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Michigan bill seeks to bar employers from requiring after-hours communication with workers, encroaching on personal time. If enacted, it could reshape workplace boundaries, overtime expectations, and how employers manage availability in the digital era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该法案拟禁止雇主在工作时间之外要求员工沟通，以保护个人时间。若通过，或将改变工作场域的边界、加班期望，以及数字时代对工作可用性的管理方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets1.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/06/24/9b48b90e-f513-4e46-8570-c5c84176a82d/thumbnail/1200x630/590a05cc61fb964846830134064fc5d1/gettyimages-2218579503.jpg' data-full='https://assets1.cbsnewsstatic.com/hub/i/r/2026/06/24/9b48b90e-f513-4e46-8570-c5c84176a82d/thumbnail/1200x630/590a05cc61fb964846830134064fc5d1/gettyimages-2218579503.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48703258' data-tags='Hardware,DevOps,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48703258' aria-label='Show summary and image'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48713832' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48713832' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danunparsed.com/p/hackerrank-open-source-ats' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HackerRank open sourced its ATS. My resume scored 90/100. Oh wait 74. No – 88</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HackerRank 开源其 ATS，简历评分波动引发关注</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48713832' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice. A single resume yielded a string of conflicting scores (90, then 74, then 88), illustrating instability and potential bias in automated screening. The takeaway: hiring AI needs better transparency, reproducibility, and fairness checks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。一次测试的简历竟得到 90、74、88 等不同分数，凸显自动筛选的不稳定性和潜在偏见。要点是招聘 AI 需加强透明性、可重复性与公平性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!S-dx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fbab22a57-acb5-4fe4-929f-132059bcb466_1800x900.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://people.idsia.ch/~juergen/ai-boom-roots-munich-1991.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Munich 1991: The Roots of the Current AI Boom</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 219</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48599998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48599998' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at Munich in 1991 uncovers early ideas and cross-disciplinary conversations that seeded today’s AI boom. It connects discussions about neural networks, compute, and industry interest to the modern AI trajectory. The piece shows how historical moments helped shape today’s AI ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾1991年的慕尼黑，揭示推动当今AI 繁荣的早期思想和跨学科对话，尤其是对神经网络、计算资源及产业界关注的讨论如何孕育今天的 AI 生态。文章把历史节点与当前 AI 发展轨迹联系起来，揭示了早期想法如何转化为如今的规模与热潮。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky GitHub gist proposes a workaround for MacBook Neo cursor lag by forcing a single pixel update every 10 seconds. The hack illustrates how developers tinker with OS rendering to squeeze perceived smoothness, revealing the fragility of UI performance on new hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个有趣的 GitHub 速记提出了一个修复 MacBook Neo 光标卡顿的变通方法：每 10 秒强制刷新一个像素。此类 hacks 展现了开发者在新硬件上为提升视觉流畅性而进行的微调，也暴露了 UI 性能的脆弱性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposed HTTP QUERY method treats server-side queries as a first-class operation, letting clients submit structured queries and receive results in a standardized way. The write-up outlines expected semantics, payload formats, and caching considerations, and discusses potential benefits for API ergonomics alongside adoption challenges for tooling and proxies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出了一种将服务器端查询作为 HTTP 端点的新方法，让客户端提交结构化查询并获得标准化结果。文中讨论了该方法的语义、负载格式与缓存行为，以及它可能带来的 API 设计简化，同时也提出工具链和代理的采用挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48664733' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://infosec.exchange/@mttaggart/116806641273303255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub shouldn&#x27;t be a dependency for publishing Rust on crates.io</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub不应成为在crates.io发布Rust包的依赖项</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48664733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48664733' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Calls to decouple Rust crate publishing from GitHub argue for more resilient publishing workflows, reducing outages and rate limits from a single platform. Proposals include alternative registries or packaging approaches to improve supply-chain reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁将 Rust 包在 crates.io 的发布与 GitHub 解绑，以提高发布流程的韧性，降低单点故障对生态的冲击。建议采用替代注册库或打包方法来提升供应链的可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://infosec.exchange/packs/assets/logo-DoPHobHA.svg' data-full='https://infosec.exchange/packs/assets/logo-DoPHobHA.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.interceptfund.com/p/ending-respiratory-infections' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ending respiratory infections</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48667588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48667588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 134</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48667588' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores funding and strategies aimed at reducing respiratory infections, from vaccines and antivirals to global health initiatives. The discussion emphasizes multi-pronged, equity-focused approaches to tamp down preventable diseases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨减少呼吸道感染的资金与策略，涵盖疫苗、抗病毒药物以及全球公共卫生举措。强调以多方位、关注公平的方式来降低可预防疾病的负担。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.matthewbrunelle.com/my-steam-machine-is-a-50ft-hdmi-cable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My Steam Machine is a 50ft HDMI cable</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648550' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Long HDMI cables can introduce latency and signal integrity challenges, and the post chronicles a playful setup where a Steam machine is connected via a 50-foot HDMI link. It offers practical takeaways for hobbyists about approaching extreme cable runs and alternative streaming options.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者以玩味的口吻记录用一条50英尺HDMI线搭建Steam机的尝试，聚焦信号损耗与延迟等现实问题。文章给出对极限距离布线的实际要点，以及对爱好者的替代方案与实现注意事项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newsweek.com/cost-me-the-election-data-centers-trigger-voter-backlash-12118327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data centers trigger voter backlash</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48689275' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Communities are voicing concerns as data centers draw criticism for electricity consumption and local environmental impact. The story outlines the voter pushback and signals potential policy changes affecting data-center siting and incentives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于数据中心的用电和对本地环境的影响，部分选民对数据基础设施扩展表示反对。报道梳理了社区的担忧和可能的政策应对，强调在数据中心选址与激励政策方面的变化可能性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utaw.tech/news/wikipedia-recognition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia Workers in Britain set global first by seeking union recognition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国维基百科员工成为全球首例寻求工会承认</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48669932' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>British Wikipedia staff seek union recognition, marking a first within the Wikimedia ecosystem and potentially shaping labor organizing across volunteer-driven projects. The move underscores tensions between paid labor and community governance in collaborative platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国维基百科员工寻求工会承认，成为维基媒体生态中的全球首例，可能影响依赖志愿者的跨国协作项目的劳动组织与治理格局。</p>
