<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48648371' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/75-more-pedestrians-have-been-killed-since-2009-giant-trucks-and-suvs-are-why' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>75% More Pedestrians Have Been Killed Since 2009. Giant Trucks and SUVs Are Why</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自2009年以来行人死亡率上升 75%，巨型卡车和 SUV 是原因</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648371' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648371' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A safety analysis finds pedestrian fatalities up 75% since 2009, with the surge largely tied to the growth of large trucks and SUVs on roads. The trend underscores debates over vehicle design, urban infrastructure, and safety tech that could mitigate risks for vulnerable road users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项安全分析发现自 2009 年以来行人死亡率上升了 75%，主要与道路上大型卡车和 SUV 的增多有关。这一趋势推动了关于车辆设计、城市基础设施和可提升脆弱行人安全的技术的讨论与政策辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thedrive.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/SUPERDUTY.jpeg?w=1200' data-full='https://www.thedrive.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/SUPERDUTY.jpeg?w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 介绍的 bsharp 项目提供一套教孩子训练绝对音感的方法，包含练习与进度跟踪，面向青少年音乐学习者。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48654351' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24597' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen-AgentWorld: Language World Models for General Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen-AgentWorld：面向通用代理的语言世界模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 196</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48654351' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes Qwen-AgentWorld, a language-powered world model framework for general agents that can reason and act across domains. By integrating natural language with environmental modeling, it aims to advance autonomous AI capable of versatile, cross-domain tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出 Qwen-AgentWorld，一种以语言驱动的通用代理世界模型框架，支持跨领域的推理与行动。通过把自然语言与环境建模结合，旨在推动具备跨域能力的自主 AI。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arun.is/blog/japan-symbols/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japanese symbols that speak without words</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无需语言也能表达意义的日本符号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634803' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48634803' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece surveys Japanese symbols that convey meaning without words—kanji, kana, and visual iconography—and examines how typography and cultural context shape interpretation for design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理日本符号在不依赖语言的情况下传达意义的方式——包括汉字、假名与视觉符号学——以及设计师能从中学到的表达技巧。讨论文化语境如何影响解读，并将这些信号应用于 UI/UX 与品牌设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48650311' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48650311' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/legal/privacy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic updates their terms to verify age or identity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 更新条款，需验证年龄或身份</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48650311' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic updates its terms to require age or identity verification for use of its AI services. The move raises privacy and accessibility questions, as well as implications for compliance with child-protection and data-ownership rules across jurisdictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 更新其使用条款，要求对用户进行年龄或身份验证。这一举措引发隐私与可访问性方面的讨论，并影响在不同司法辖区内对儿童保护与数据所有权等法规的合规性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48706714' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48706714' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/2847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A way to exclude sensitive files issue still open for OpenAI Codex</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI Codex 的排除敏感文件方法仍未解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48706714' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved. The discussion highlights ongoing data governance and privacy challenges in deploying code-generating AI, where guarding private or proprietary content is critical.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。此议题凸显了代码生成模型在数据治理和隐私保护方面的持续挑战，以及对私有或机密内容的防护需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eccbe5e1f2a2e82c834163352ad07ef046187667a06e623afc4bd509047618d9/openai/codex/issues/2847' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/eccbe5e1f2a2e82c834163352ad07ef046187667a06e623afc4bd509047618d9/openai/codex/issues/2847' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇经典教程展示了如何用 Python 构建一个 Lisp 解释器，从词法分析到求值的完整实现。通过简洁的示例，揭示环境、递归与求值等解释器核心理念，便于入门学习。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to reviving aging PCs with Linux, covering lightweight distributions, driver compatibility, and performance tips. By breathing new life into old hardware, the guide underscores how Linux can extend device lifespans and curb e-waste, even on machines with limited specs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供一份实用指南，教你用 Linuxrevive 旧PC：从选择轻量级发行版、驱动兼容到性能优化。通过延长设备寿命和减少电子垃圾，文章强调在硬件规格有限的情况下依然可以获得可用的体验，并给出可行的注意事项。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48650837' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48650837' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://milek7.pl/mailverifyspam/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t verify email addresses by sending spam to them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要通过向邮箱发送垃圾邮件来验证地址</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48650837' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues against email verification methods that rely on blasting spam to addresses. It advocates safer alternatives like confirmed opt-in, invite-based verification, or leveraging trusted channels to balance security with deliverability and user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中反对通过向未核实的邮箱发送垃圾邮件来验证地址的做法，提倡使用确认订阅、受邀验证或信任渠道等更安全、兼顾可投递性与隐私的验证方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unconv.ai/blog/introducing-un-0-generating-images-with-coupled-oscillators/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Un-0: Generating Images with Coupled Oscillators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Un-0：用耦合振荡器生成图像</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48679007' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hardware-oriented approach to image generation using coupled oscillators challenges the dominance of neural nets and diffusion models. The project showcases how physical dynamics can produce visuals, offering a potential path to efficient, low-latency generators and new hardware-software co-design opportunities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过耦合振荡器的物理动态来生成图像，挑战了神经网络和扩散模型的主导地位。该方法展示了利用物理系统产生图像的可能性，为实现低延迟、节能的生成方法以及软硬件协同设计打开了新路径。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/06/26/meta-wynn-williams-surveillance-gag-order-lawsuit-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Careless People&#x27; author claims Meta surveilled her for 12mos to enforce silence</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48701822' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Careless People author Wynn Williams alleges Meta surveilled her for 12 months to enforce a silence order. The claim highlights how large platforms can monitor public figures during litigation, raising privacy and freedom-of-expression concerns for creators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Careless People》作者 Wynn Williams 指控 Meta 在12个月内对她进行监控，以强制保持沉默。此事凸显大型平台在诉讼期间对发言者的监控带来的隐私与言论自由担忧，可能影响创作者权利及网络言论环境。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://economist.com/interactive/1843/2026/06/19/the-strange-disappearance-of-japans-animators' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The disappearance of Japan&#x27;s animators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本动画师的消失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620422' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620422' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Economist reports a troubling exodus of Japan&#x27;s animators driven by low pay, long hours, and outsourcing pressures. The piece maps how talent drain threatens production pace, quality, and the future of domestic animation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《经济学人》报道日本动画师大量流失，原因包括低薪、长工时和外包压力。文章分析了人才外流对产出速度、质量以及日本动漫产业未来的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622234' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a negotiation tactic: asking for a &#x27;no&#x27; can reduce anxiety and yield clearer commitments. The piece offers practical tips for framing requests and handling responses in professional settings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了一种谈判技巧：先让对方说“不”可以降低心理防备并促成更清晰的承诺。文中给出在专业情境中提出请求、处理回应的实用建议。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48703412' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Decomp Academy 教你把 GameCube 二进制反编译回 C 语言，提供工具与教程。展示了软件考古学的日益活跃，以及对开源反编译工作流的兴趣。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48668255' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48668255' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jamesoclaire.com/2026/06/25/the-unbearable-cheapness-of-open-weight-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The unbearable cheapness of open weight models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源权重模型的难以承受的便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48668255' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Unbearable Cheapness of Open Weight Models argues that while open-weight LLMs dramatically reduce runtime API costs and vendor lock-in, there are hidden costs in compute, energy, governance, and safety. Open weights enable experimentation but shift responsibility to maintain and secure models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《开源权重模型的难以承受的便宜》指出，开源权重模型虽然显著降低运行成本和对供应商的依赖，但也带来计算、能耗、治理与安全等隐性成本。开放权重促成更多实验，却把维护与安全责任转移到用户端。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jamesoclaire.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-From-2026-06-25-10-14-52-850x291.png' data-full='https://jamesoclaire.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Screenshot-From-2026-06-25-10-14-52-850x291.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48661535' data-tags='Science,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48661535' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/research/2026/06/24/boffin-claims-microsofts-supposed-quantum-leap-does-not-compute-due-to-basic-python-errors/5260489' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Boffin claims Microsoft’s “quantum leap” is invalid due to “basic Python errors”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>研究员称微软的‘量子跃迁’无效，原因是“基本Python错误”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48661535' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher argues that Microsoft’s quantum computing claims don’t hold up due to basic Python errors in demonstrations. The critique adds noise to corporate hype around quantum progress and stresses the need for reproducible evidence in cutting-edge research.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位研究者称微软在量子计算方面的说法因演示中的基本Python错误而站不住脚。这一质疑加剧了对量子技术企业宣称的质疑，强调在前沿研究中需要可重复的证据。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/225000.jpg?imageId=225000&amp;x=0&amp;y=28.33&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=71.67&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=28.33&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=71.67&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/225000.jpg?imageId=225000&amp;x=0&amp;y=28.33&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=71.67&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=28.33&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=71.67&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48585852' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Usbliter8 exposes a SecureROM exploit affecting A12/A13 devices, illustrating the ongoing cat-and-mouse in iPhone security. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Usbliter8 揭示影响 A12/A13 SecureROM 的漏洞利用，展示对安全启动的突破。本文强调此类漏洞带来的风险、对固件防御的需求，以及若被滥用可能产生的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>密歇根花费18亿美元却仅创造602个就业岗位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48702060' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>State spending awarded a large financing package yet produced only a fraction of promised jobs, prompting questions about program design, effectiveness, and ROI. The case underscores how incentives must be paired with measurable impact and transparent reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该州投入巨额资金却只实现了承诺就业岗位的一小部分，引发对项目设计、有效性和投资回报率的质疑。案例强调在激励政策中需要可衡量的影响和透明的结果报告。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 模型中的政治偏见进行评估，判断主流系统在意识形态中立性、输出偏见的表现以及工程师正在采用的缓解手段。文章强调偏见并非已被消除，持续审计和透明度至关重要。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Boeing 747, long the icon of global air travel, is nearing retirement as fleets and routes shift to more efficient jets. The piece traces the 747&#x27;s engineering legacy, economic drivers of its sunset, and the challenges of maintaining aging aircraft while demand evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作为全球航空旅行的象征，波音 747 即将退役，全球航线与机队的更新推动其退出市场。文章回顾了 747 在工程与全球经济中的影响，以及在需求和维护成本变化背景下退役所面临的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how Ozempic and related GLP-1 drugs influence gut-brain signaling, appetite, and possibly mood, with implications for long-term health and metabolic outcomes. It clarifies mechanisms and cautions about variability across individuals.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48638660' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/06/polymarkets-viral-videos-showed-people-winning-big-but-the-bets-were-fake/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Polymarket&#x27;s viral videos showed people winning big, but the bets were fake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Polymarket 的病毒视频显示有人赢大钱，但投注为假</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48638660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48638660' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48638660' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket&#x27;s viral videos suggested large winnings, but investigators found that the underlying bets were fake, pointing to deceptive marketing and potential fraud. The incident intensifies scrutiny of prediction markets and underscores the need for better transparency and oversight in tokenized betting platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polymarket 的病毒式视频显示有人获得巨额收益，然而调查发现相关赌注为虚假，指向误导性营销与可能的欺诈。这一事件加剧了对预测市场的监管关注，并凸显对代币化赌注平台透明度与监管的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/polymarket-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/polymarket-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfwriter.com/wordstar.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WordStar: A Writer&#x27;s Word Processor (1996)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WordStar：一位作家的文字处理器（1996）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48694853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48694853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48694853' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WordStar was a pioneering, keyboard-centric word processor that shaped early writing workflows. This retrospective revisits its design choices, user interface quirks, and influence on later editors and author workflows, contrasting it with modern editors and markup tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WordStar 是一款开创性的、以键盘为中心的文字处理器，深刻影响了早期写作工作流。回顾其设计选择、界面特征及对后续编辑器与作者工作流的影响，并与现代编辑器和标记工具进行对比。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48678873' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/06/25/technology/openai-ipo-artificial-intelligence.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI leans toward waiting until next year for IPO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 倾向推迟至明年上市</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48678873' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI is reportedly postponing its IPO to next year, signaling a cautious approach to going public as it weighs governance, regulatory readiness, and market conditions. The decision reflects the complex path for AI-focused firms seeking public markets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 计划将上市时间推迟到明年，权衡治理、监管就绪与市场条件，显现出对面向公开市场的谨慎策略。此举反映了专注于 AI 的公司在进入公开市场时面临的复杂路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48619140' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48619140' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(219) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinak.se/blog/who-actually-owns-your-atproto-identity-hint-its-probably-not-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who owns your ATProto identity?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谁拥有你的 ATProto 身份？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619140' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619140' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that ownership of ATProto identities is not straightforward and elusive for users. It examines implications for privacy, portability, and platform control, urging readers to consider where their digital identity truly resides.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出 ATProto 身份的拥有权并非对用户而言清晰易定，涉及隐私、可移植性与平台控制等问题。呼吁读者关注数字身份的实际掌控权与去中心化可能性。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48668119' data-tags='Science,Education,AI,Security,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48668119' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/medical-students-are-using-popular-research-tool-pump-out-misleading-studies' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Medical students are using popular research tool to pump out misleading studies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>医学生利用热门研究工具炮制误导性研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48668119' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Medical students are allegedly using a popular research tool to generate misleading studies, exposing vulnerabilities in how easily AI-assisted or automated tools can distort medical literature. The incident underscores the need for stronger verification, education, and safeguards in research workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称医学生在流行的研究工具中快速生成误导性研究，暴露了 AI 辅助工具对医学文献造成扭曲的风险。此事强调需要加强核查、教育与研究工作流中的安全防护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48606096' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48606096' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fil-c.org/inlineasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memory Safe Inline Assembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>内存安全的内联汇编</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48606096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48606096' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a path to memory-safe inline assembly, proposing language or tooling that preserves low-level performance while preventing common memory bugs. Key ideas include safe interfaces, formal verification, and guarding against buffer overruns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在保持低级别性能的同时实现内存安全的内联汇编路径，提出了可保证内存安全的语言或工具。核心思路包括安全接口、形式化验证，以及防止缓冲区溢出等常见漏洞。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Post-mythos cybersecurity argues for pragmatic, resilient security practices over alarmist narratives. It emphasizes steady risk management, observable controls, and calm incident response in a landscape of evolving threats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在持续演变的威胁环境中，后神话时代的网络安全倡导务实、具韧性的安全做法，强调稳健的风险管理、可观测的控制以及冷静的事件响应。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.minecraft.net/en-us/article/minecraft-java-edition-26-2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minecraft: Java Edition 26.2, the first version with Vulkan 1.2</a></p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Java Edition 26.2 adds Vulkan 1.2 support, enabling better GPU acceleration, reduced CPU bottlenecks, and improved cross-platform performance. The change signals continued graphics updates and performance improvements in a long-running title.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48670103' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>During WWII, the U.S. Army distributed inexpensive ocarinas to soldiers, using music to boost morale, signal in the field, or reinforce drills. The article shows how a simple instrument found practical wartime uses beyond entertainment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二战时期美军向士兵发放便宜的陶笛，用音乐提振士气、进行野战信号传递以及加强训练。文章展示了这样一个简单乐器在战时的实际应用与价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thedrive.com/news/feds-killed-polestar-and-spared-volvo-that-should-terrify-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds Killed Polestar and Spared Volvo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦监管击垮 Polestar，放过沃尔沃，这应让你感到不安</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48703613' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues federal actions targeted Polestar while sparing Volvo, a dynamic that could upend trust in regulatory consistency for tech-forward automakers. It warns that uneven enforcement may stifle competition and innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章称联邦监管让 Polestar 失败、却对沃尔沃网开一面，这种差异化执法让人对科技/汽车领域的监管一致性产生担忧。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48677564' data-tags='Science,Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48677564' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(228) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/britain/2026/06/25/the-bbc-switches-off-its-oldest-service' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Long Wave radio era set to end with switch-off</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>长波广播时代将因关停而结束</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48677564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48677564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48677564' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The world&#x27;s oldest long‑wave service is being shut down, signaling the end of an era in broadcast infrastructure. The switch-off reflects ongoing cost-cutting and modernization, with listeners urged to update to digital or alternative bands, reshaping how rural and regional audiences stay informed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球最老的长波广播服务即将被切断，标志着这一代广播基础设施的告别。此举反映了成本节约与数字化升级的趋势，听众可能需要转向数字广播或其他频段获取信息，农村和地区性收听也将受影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48623419' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48623419' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://underreacted.leaflet.pub/3mmevu6woys27' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japanese verb conjugation the simple hard way</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日语动词变形：简单而难的方式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48623419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48623419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 263</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48623419' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a practical route to mastering Japanese verb conjugations by focusing on patterns and rules rather than rote memorization of dozens of forms. The approach aims to make irregulars approachable and give learners a working framework.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供一种实用的方法来掌握日语动词变形，强调模式和规则，而非死记硬背大量形式。该方法旨在让不规则变化更易于掌握，并给学习者一个可落地的框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Afpruhuo22xkm5o7ttr2ktxdo/3m23dstduds2v/3mmevu6woys27/opengraph-image-vzkbb4?edc23816ae45539e' data-full='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Afpruhuo22xkm5o7ttr2ktxdo/3m23dstduds2v/3mmevu6woys27/opengraph-image-vzkbb4?edc23816ae45539e' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48619394' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48619394' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/06/16/shipping-freight-energy-fossil-cargo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fossil Fuels Are 40% of Freight Shipping Tonnage, but Half Its Fuel Use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>化石燃料占货运吨位的40%，却占用一半燃料消耗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619394' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new analysis shows fossil fuels represent about 40% of freight tonnage but account for roughly half the energy use, underscoring efficiency gains and decarbonization opportunities in shipping. The finding suggests focusing on vessel design, routes, and energy mix to cut emissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示化石燃料约占货运吨位的40%，却约占一半的能源消耗，凸显航运业在减排方面的潜在改进点。应聚焦船舶设计、航线优化和能源结构以降低排放。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/07101180-df6c-43af-ac8b-bacaddd3be58_1600x900.webp' data-full='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/07101180-df6c-43af-ac8b-bacaddd3be58_1600x900.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An individual with spinal muscular atrophy explains how they actually play video games today, detailing assistive technologies, adaptive controllers, and daily setup. The account highlights accessible-gaming tools and broader implications for inclusive design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位患有脊髓性肌萎缩症的人分享了自己实际的游戏玩法，介绍了辅助技术、适应性控制器以及日常使用的设备。此文凸显了无障碍游戏工具的重要性以及对包容性设计的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(232) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/exclusive-nsf-slashes-research-programs-support-new-tech-initiative-insiders-say' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NSF slashes research programs to support new tech initiative, insiders say</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NSF 削减研究项目以支持新技术倡议，内部人士称</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48632327' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NSF is reportedly slashing some research programs to fund a new tech initiative, with insiders noting concerns about how this shift will affect foundational research. The move could realign funding priorities and ripple through academia and industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 NSF 正削减部分研究项目以资助一项新技术倡议，内部人士对这次调整对基础研究的影响表达担忧。此举可能改变资金优先级，并对学术界与产业生态产生连锁效应。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencenews.org/article/deadly-fungus-cats-people-spreading' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A deadly fungus that can infect cats and people is spreading</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种能感染猫和人类的致命真菌正在传播</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658186' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deadly fungus capable of infecting both cats and people is spreading, raising concerns about zoonotic transmission and treatment options. The piece underscores the importance of surveillance, veterinary health, and cross-species pathogen awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种能够同时感染猫和人类的致命真菌正在蔓延，引发对人畜共患病传播和治疗选择的担忧。文章强调监测、兽医卫生和跨物种病原体意识的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A nationwide communication-system outage halted trains across Germany, demonstrating how rail operations depend on centralized tech. Operators rerouted services and workers investigate the root cause, with potential safety and timetable ripple effects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国因通信系统故障导致全国铁路停运，暴露了铁路运营对集中式通信的高度依赖。运营方正在调整线路、处理延误，并调查根本原因，事件对安全与时刻表造成连锁影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟开源了一套十年期的电网发展规划工具，用于建模与优化未来的能源网络。通过开源，研究者、政策制定者和产业参与者可以协作、验证情景，并推进电网现代化以实现去碳目标并提升可靠性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>僵尸独角兽正在困扰硅谷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 148</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48668020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48668020' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zombie unicorns refer to startups that retain billion-dollar valuations while burning cash and showing weak fundamentals, propped up by a late-stage funding appetite. The piece argues this dynamic distorts capital markets and may force a reckoning in Silicon Valley as funding conditions tighten.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>所谓‘僵尸独角兽’指在资金持续支撑下仍维持十亿美元级估值、但基本面薄弱的初创企业。此现象扭曲资本市场，随着融资环境收紧，硅谷可能面临重新定价与投资谨慎的局面。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(240) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.chevron.com/newsroom/2026/q2/chevron-signs-20-year-power-agreement-with-microsoft-for-west-texas-data-center' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chevron signs 20-year power agreement with Microsoft for West Texas data center</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chevron 与微软签订 West Texas 数据中心20年用电协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630029' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chevron signs a 20-year power purchase agreement with Microsoft to power a West Texas data center, signaling corporate demand for clean energy and long-term reliability. The deal highlights how large buyers coordinate with utilities to decarbonize and stabilize energy costs for cloud infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>雪佛龙与微软签署在西德克萨斯州数据中心的20年购电协议，显示企业对清洁能源与长期供电可靠性的需求。此交易体现大型买家如何与公用事业方协作，以实现云基础设施的低碳化与成本稳定。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48689277' data-tags='Data,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48689277' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lefakkomies.github.io/pynomo-doc/introduction/introduction.html#what-is-a-nomogram-and-why-would-it-interest-me' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Is a Nomogram and Why Would It Interest Me?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是列线图（Nomogram）以及它为何重要？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48689277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48689277' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nomograms are visual calculators that map a statistical model to a simple ruler, making predictions easier to interpret. The linked guide shows how to construct them in Python and why they remain useful for explainable AI and clinical decision support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>列线图是一种将统计模型转化为直观尺尺的可视化工具，便于理解预测结果。指南介绍如何用 Python 构建列线图，以及它在可解释 AI 与临床决策支持中的应用价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lefakkomies.github.io/pynomo-doc/_images/meyer_nomogram.png' data-full='https://lefakkomies.github.io/pynomo-doc/_images/meyer_nomogram.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48683021' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/LocalLLaMA/comments/1ufo0un/us_govt_to_individually_approve_who_gets_gpt_56/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Govt to individually approve who gets GPT 5.6</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国政府对 GPT-5.6 实施逐人审批</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 143</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48683021' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Government proposes per-user approval for access to GPT-5.6, effectively turning access control into a license-per-person regime. The policy raises concerns about innovation, inclusivity, and safety governance in next-gen AI models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政府拟对 GPT-5.6 的使用实行逐人审批，实际变成按人许可的制度。这一政策引发对创新、包容性与未来 AI 模型安全治理的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(244) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/20260625-00/?p=112467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DLL that was not present in memory despite not being formally unloaded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未正式卸载却不在内存中的 DLL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48705910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48705910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48705910' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Windows debugging note discusses a DLL that wasn&#x27;t present in memory even though the loader hadn&#x27;t formally unloaded it, revealing quirks in module management. It highlights nuances between loaded modules and actual memory residency useful for debugging.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇微软技术博客记录了一个 DLL 即使未被正式卸载，内存中却并不存在的情况，揭示了模块管理中的细微差别。此现象对调试、内存映射和性能分析具有参考价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/oldnewthing/wp-content/uploads/sites/38/2019/02/ShowCover.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48708795' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(245) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://12gramsofcarbon.com/p/agentics-tech-things-tokenmaxxing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tokenmaxxing is dead, long live tokenmaxxing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tokenmaxxing 已死，Tokenmaxxing 将长存</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 137</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 165</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708795' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tokenmaxxing has seen a pivot: the old form may be fading, but the core mindset remains—optimizing prompts, tool use, and token budgets will continue under new guises as models and pricing evolve. The post argues that while the practice changes, the incentives to maximize efficiency persist.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文认为“Tokenmaxxing”的旧形式或已衰退，但核心思维仍在：在新模型与定价结构下，优化提示、工具使用和 token 使用的动机将以新的形式继续存在。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A job application asked for SAT scores, prompting debate on whether such data is relevant or appropriate for employment. It underscores the broader push for privacy-respecting, skills-based hiring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份求职申请竟要求提供 SAT 成绩，引发关于隐私和公平招聘的讨论。标准化考试成绩并非评估工作能力的可靠依据，收集这类信息可能带来歧视风险，推动以技能为基础的招聘。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Underarm_bowling_incident_of_1981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Underarm bowling incident of 1981</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1981 年的下臂投球事件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 135</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48650794' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 1981 underarm bowling incident remains a watershed moment in cricket diplomacy: a contrived ball delivered underarm to prevent a win sparked global outrage and led to stronger sportsmanship norms. It also pushed changes in rules and etiquette around fair play.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1981 年的新西兰下臂投球事件成为板球外交史上的分水岭。为避免对手获胜而以下臂投球，激起全球愤慨并推动了对公平竞赛规范和裁判准则的反馈与修订。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48634787' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL uses the IANA tz database to handle time zone rules, including changes in British Columbia. The piece stresses keeping tzdata up to date and using proper time zone handling (TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE) to avoid DST-related surprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 通过 IANA tz 数据库处理时区规则，包括不列颠哥伦比亚省的最新时区调整。文章强调保持 tzdata 更新，并正确使用带时区的时间字段，以避免夏令时等变更带来的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imagedelivery.net/lPM0ntuwQfh8VQgJRu0mFg/20d3ec66-0a11-4da9-51c6-2fba2b821900/public' data-full='https://imagedelivery.net/lPM0ntuwQfh8VQgJRu0mFg/20d3ec66-0a11-4da9-51c6-2fba2b821900/public' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48693420' data-tags='Finance,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48693420' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.mccombs.utexas.edu/research/foreign-funds-help-make-housing-unaffordable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>International investment and local rules push prices up faster than supply</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>国际投资与地方规则让房价上涨快于供应</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48693420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48693420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48693420' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Foreign investment and local regulation can push housing prices up faster than new supply, according to the study. The findings suggest capital flows and zoning constraints magnify affordability challenges, highlighting policy gaps that can trap price growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，国际投资与地方监管共同推动房价上涨速度超过新房供应的增速。资金流动和土地使用限制叠加放大了可负担性问题，凸显相关政策缺口。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.mccombs.utexas.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/iStock-1495524595-1200x800.jpg' data-full='https://news.mccombs.utexas.edu/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/iStock-1495524595-1200x800.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48697746' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48697746' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-many-elementary-particles-are-there-really-20260615/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Many Elementary Particles Are There, Really?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>真正有多少基本粒子？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48697746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48697746' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physicists debate what counts as an elementary particle, given scale and theoretical definitions. The piece surveys how definitions shift with new experiments and models, and what that means for our understanding of the fundamental building blocks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>物理学家在基本粒子定义上存在分歧，是否真的“基本”取决于能量尺度与理论框架。文章梳理定义、实验约束，以及在前沿领域如何将复合态模糊化为基本自由度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Qualia-ElementaryParticles-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Qualia-ElementaryParticles-crKristinaArmitage-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48634559' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48634559' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/personal-tech/2026/06/22/the-memory-crisis-is-getting-so-bad-that-even-retro-ram-prices-are-going-to-the-moon/5259627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Memory crisis is getting so bad that even retro RAM prices are going to the Moon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>记忆危机推动复古 RAM 价格上涨</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48634559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48634559' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A memory shortage is driving up prices for both cutting-edge DRAM and retro RAM as demand outpaces supply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>内存短缺推动了前沿 DRAM 以及复古 RAM 的价格上涨，需求超过供应。文章评估对爱好者、数据中心等行业的影响，以及为何遗留内存的价格上涨速度可能高于预期。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5259665.jpg?imageId=5259665&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5259665.jpg?imageId=5259665&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708859' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48708859' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openculture.com/2020/10/daisugi.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Daisugi, the Japanese technique of growing trees out of other trees (2020)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大杉术：日本通过让树从其他树上长出新树的做法（2020）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 129</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708859' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708859' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Daisugi is a Japanese forestry technique where live trees are grown from the branches of other trees to harvest straight timber without cutting down the parent. It represents an early example of sustainable forestry and clever resource management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大杉术是日本的一种林业技术，通过让活树从其他树的枝干上生长出新树，从而在不砍伐母树的情况下获取笔直木材。这一做法是可持续林业与资源管理的早期典范。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2020/10/22225805/Daisugi.png' data-full='https://cdn8.openculture.com/2020/10/22225805/Daisugi.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48636380' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48636380' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(256) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xenodium.com/ytr-youtube-radio-for-emacs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ytr: YouTube Radio for Emacs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ytr：供 Emacs 使用的 YouTube Radio</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48636380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48636380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48636380' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ytr implements a lightweight YouTube radio experience inside Emacs, enabling keyboard-driven browsing and playback of YouTube audio. For power users, it&#x27;s an example of integrating modern streaming services into a programmable editor, though reliability and licensing caveats apply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ytr 在 Emacs 中实现了一个轻量的 YouTube Radio 体验，便于在编辑器内通过键盘浏览并播放 YouTube 音频。对于爱好者来说，这是将现代流媒体服务与可编程编辑器结合的有趣案例，但在可靠性和授权方面存在潜在约束。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://xenodium.com/share.png' data-full='https://xenodium.com/share.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48617782' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48617782' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexkritchevsky.com/2024/02/28/geometric-algebra.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case against geometric algebra (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反对几何代数的论证（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48617782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 123</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48617782' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical take on geometric algebra argues it offers limited practical advantages over conventional vector calculus and can complicate understanding. The piece weighs its claimed unifying power against real-world adoption in physics and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对几何代数的质疑指出，它在实际应用中相较传统向量微积分并无明显优势，反而可能增加理解难度。文章权衡其所宣称的统一性与在物理和工程领域的实际应用证据。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48665125' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(258) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://texashighways.com/culture/how-heb-became-texas-most-beloved-brand/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How H-E-B became Texas&#x27; most beloved brand (2024)</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48665125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48665125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48665125' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece traces how H-E-B built a deeply rooted Texas brand through customer-first operations, local engagement, and distinctive shopping experiences. The result is a grocery chain that&#x27;s become a cultural touchstone with fiercely loyal customers and resilient growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理了 H-E-B 如何通过以客户为中心的运营、深入的社区参与，以及独特的门店体验，建立起扎根德州的品牌形象，赢得深厚的顾客忠诚与稳健增长。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.outsideonline.com/outdoor-adventure/environment/nps-internal-memo-deaths/?link_source=ta_first_comment&amp;taid=6a3dae4f4d2dce00016deef8&amp;utm_content=trueanthem&amp;utm_medium=social&amp;utm_source=facebook' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The National Parks Were Reportedly Told to Stay Silent on Deaths</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>据报道，国家公园被告知对死亡事件保持沉默</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692098' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Internal memos reportedly instructed staff to stay silent about deaths, raising questions about transparency in public lands. The episode underscores tensions between crisis communications and public accountability in public lands.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，国家公园内部备忘录要求工作人员对死亡事件保持沉默，对外信息传播受到限制。此事引发对公共土地透明度、危机沟通和问责问题的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>罗马时代的影响仍在现代文化、法律和基础设施中延续。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48612025' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://glyphcss.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: An ASCII 3D Rendering Engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一个 ASCII 3D 渲染引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48612025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48612025' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48612025' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ASCII 3D rendering engine demonstrates a pure CPU pipeline that outputs 3D scenes as ASCII art in real time. It showcases techniques like ray marching or voxel rendering adapted to character grids, offering a playful baseline for terminals and low-res displays. It is a striking example of creative coding that runs without a GPU.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个纯 CPU 渲染管线的 ASCII 3D 引擎把 3D 场景实时输出为字符画，适合在终端或低分辨率显示上使用。它展示了将光线行进、体素渲染等技术适配到字符网格的实现方式，提供了一个有趣的创意编码基线。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48702848' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48702848' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/walrus-postgres-backups-in-rust' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WAL-RUS: a Rust Rewrite of WAL-G for PostgreSQL Backups</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WAL-RUS：PostgreSQL 备份的 WAL-G 的 Rust 重写</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48702848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48702848' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WAL-RUS reimplements WAL-G in Rust to back up PostgreSQL, promising safer memory handling and performance improvements over older runtimes. It&#x27;s a concrete example of modern language rewrites unlocking reliability in critical operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WAL-RUS 将 WAL-G 重写为 Rust 实现 PostgreSQL 备份，期待带来更高的安全性和性能优势。此举是用现代语言改写老牌工具以提升可靠性的一个案例。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-powered hiring tools can embed racial bias and create systemic rejection patterns, risking reduced diversity. The piece calls for auditing, transparency, and human oversight to mitigate harm.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.hydroponictrash.solar/cyberdecks-going-analog-and-convivial-technology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyberdecks, going analog, and convivial technology</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 119</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48605776' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48605776' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a trend toward analog, offline-friendly cyberdecks and a convivial tech culture that prioritizes privacy and local-first workflows. The piece argues these setups can foster resilience and community in a connected world.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨走向模拟化、离线友好型的赛博甲板以及更为友善的技术文化，强调隐私与本地优先工作流的重要性。这种风格的设备被视为在互联世界中提升韧性与社区感的途径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48633941' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48633941' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1029767/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux and Secure Boot certificate expiration (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 与 Secure Boot 证书在 2025 年到期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48633941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48633941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48633941' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Secure Boot certificates are set to expire in 2025, risking boot failures on systems relying on older shim and Mok certificates. Admins should plan to update bootloaders, re-sign kernels, and validate firmware upgrades before the expiration window hits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2025 年将到期的 Secure Boot 证书可能导致使用旧 shim 和 MokKey 的系统无法启动。文章建议管理员尽早更新引导链的证书、重新签名内核，并测试固件升级路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48631058' data-tags='Energy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48631058' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/pro/sustainable-business/dhl-set-to-transport-goods-on-new-wind-powered-cargo-ships-eca5d5a0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DHL Set to Transport Goods on New Wind-Powered Cargo Ships</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DHL 将使用风力辅助的货船运输货物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631058' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48631058' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DHL plans to transport goods on wind-powered cargo ships, signaling a bold step toward decarbonizing logistics. While wind-assisted ships face scalability and energy efficiency questions, this move could push the industry toward greener maritime practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DHL 将使用以风力辅助的货船运输货物，标志着物流领域向低碳化迈出的大胆一步。尽管风力辅助船在规模化和效率方面仍有挑战，但此举可能推动航运业走向更绿色的未来。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48637433' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48637433' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scottaaronson.blog/?p=9851' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bipartite Matching Is in NC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>双部图匹配属于 NC 类</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48637433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48637433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48637433' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A breakthrough in complexity theory shows bipartite matching resides in NC, indicating highly parallelizable algorithms for solving this classic problem. The result tightens our understanding of parallel computation limits and could influence practical huge-scale graph processing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在复杂性理论中取得突破性进展，证明双部图匹配问题属于 NC 类，意味着可并行化程度很高的求解算法。该结果拓展了对并行计算极限的理解，并可能影响大规模图处理的实际应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvc2NvdHRhYXJvbnNvbi5ibG9nXC93cC1jb250ZW50XC91cGxvYWRzXC8yMDIxXC8xMFwvY3JvcHBlZC1KYWNrZXQuZ2lmIiwidHh0IjoiU2h0ZXRsLU9wdGltaXplZCIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxMjk1MjA1ODB9.siOtN7gHw4tefA_rZickBw4GfI6tPxGgOQ1AXr2ZoOQMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvc2NvdHRhYXJvbnNvbi5ibG9nXC93cC1jb250ZW50XC91cGxvYWRzXC8yMDIxXC8xMFwvY3JvcHBlZC1KYWNrZXQuZ2lmIiwidHh0IjoiU2h0ZXRsLU9wdGltaXplZCIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiZWRnZSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjoxMjk1MjA1ODB9.siOtN7gHw4tefA_rZickBw4GfI6tPxGgOQ1AXr2ZoOQMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48583407' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48583407' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://konradreiche.com/blog/excessive-nil-pointer-checks-in-go/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Excessive nil pointer checks in Go</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 语言中过度 nil 指针检查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48583407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48583407' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that overusing nil-pointer checks in Go can bloat code and obscure logic, recommending a balance between defensive checks and relying on proper nil-safe patterns or error handling. Practical tips include leveraging early returns and natural zero values to reduce noise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在 Go 语言中过度使用 nil 指针检查会让代码臃肿、降低可读性，建议在防御性检查和使用正确的 nil 安全模式或错误处理之间取得平衡。实用建议包括提前返回、利用零值自然避免冗余检查等。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
