<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48713697' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/business/2026/06/28/ai-boom-risks-global-financial-crash-central-bankers-warn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI boom risks global financial crash, warn central bankers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 热潮可能引发全球金融崩溃？央行警告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48713697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48713697' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Central bankers warn that the AI boom could destabilize markets and trigger a global financial crash, citing inflated tech valuations, funding risk, and productivity misreads. They urge macroprudential tools and careful policy framing to prevent a tech-driven downturn.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>央行高层警告，AI 热潮可能推高科技估值、融资风险并扭曲生产力评估，带来全球金融崩溃的隐患。建议强化宏观审慎工具与政策框架，以防范由技术泡沫引发的系统性风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/business/2026/06/28/TELEMMGLPICT000492564394_17826523826990_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqpVlberWd9EgFPZtcLiMQf0Rf_Wk3V23H2268P_XkPxc.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' data-full='https://www.telegraph.co.uk/content/dam/business/2026/06/28/TELEMMGLPICT000492564394_17826523826990_trans_NvBQzQNjv4BqpVlberWd9EgFPZtcLiMQf0Rf_Wk3V23H2268P_XkPxc.jpeg?impolicy=OG-Standard' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Provides an overview of PID controllers, showing how proportional, integral, and derivative terms work together to regulate systems. Includes common tuning methods and examples across robotics and process control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>概述 PID 控制器，解释比例、积分、微分三项如何协同作用以稳定系统，并介绍常见的调参方法及在机器人和过程控制中的应用示例。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48701512' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kageroumado/adrafinil' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Adrafinil – keep a lid-closed Mac awake only while agents work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Adrafinil——让合上盖的 Mac 仅在任务代理运行时保持唤醒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48701512' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lightweight tool lets a Mac with the lid closed stay awake only when specified background agents are running. This highlights how macOS manages sleep states for headless workflows and could simplify remote automation setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个轻量工具，允许合上盖的 Mac 在指定后台代理运行时保持唤醒。它揭示了 macOS 睡眠管理在无头工作流中的行为，并可能简化远程自动化的部署。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48678623' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48678623' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://williamdue.github.io/blog/parallel-parentheses-matching' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parallel Parentheses Matching</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>并行括号匹配</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48678623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48678623' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post presents a parallel approach to matching parentheses, illustrating how concurrency ideas can speed up a classic parsing problem. It highlights trade-offs in synchronization and workload distribution relevant to compilers and text-processing pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中给出一种并行化的括号匹配思路，展示并发如何加速这一经典解析问题。讨论了同步与任务分配的权衡，对编译器和文本处理流水线具有现实意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州立法机构同意将驾照信息上传至国家数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708139' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708139' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California lawmakers approve plans to upload driver&#x27;s license data to a national database, signaling a push toward centralized identity verification. The move raises privacy, security, and civil liberties concerns while improving interoperability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州立法机构批准将驾照信息上传至国家数据库，显示出向集中身份验证的趋势。此举提升互操作性，但也引发隐私、安全与公民自由方面的担忧。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48645721' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48645721' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lift4d.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lift4D: Harmonizing Single-View 3D Estimation for 4D Reconstruction In-the-Wild</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lift4D：将单视角3D估计整合以实现野外场景的4D重建</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 110</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48645721' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lift4D harmonizes single-view 3D estimation to enable robust 4D reconstruction in-the-wild, producing temporally coherent dynamic scenes from unconstrained footage. This advances AR/VR, robotics, and film workflows by enabling reliable monocular-based 4D models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lift4D 将单视角 3D 估计协调起来，实现对未受控场景的时序一致的 4D 重建。利用单目线索在时间上保持一致，能够从自由拍摄的素材中构建动态场景模型，推动 AR/VR、机器人和影视工作流的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lift4d.github.io/static/methodology/eq_z00.png' data-full='https://lift4d.github.io/static/methodology/eq_z00.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48685967' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines how human cognitive limits shape engineering decisions, from UI to decision support. Emphasizes reducing cognitive load with simpler defaults and clearer feedback to prevent mistakes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨人类认知边界如何影响工程决策，从用户界面到决策支持系统。强调通过更简单的默认设置和清晰反馈来降低认知负荷，减少错误。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ShowDirectoryPicker enables web apps to access a user-chosen directory, unlocking batch processing, file enumeration, and better offline workflows in a secure, permission-controlled way. It opens new patterns for web apps that need filesystem access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>showDirectoryPicker 让网页应用能访问用户选择的目录，支持批处理、文件枚举与更完整的离线工作流，并在权限控制下保证安全。为需要文件系统访问的网页应用带来新用例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Tessil/hopscotch-map' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A C++ implementation of a fast hash map and hash set using hopscotch hashing</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48692090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48692090' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tessil&#x27;s hopscotch-map provides a fast, cache-friendly C++ hash map and hash set using hopscotch hashing. The implementation emphasizes low latency and strong performance for systems programming and high-throughput workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tessil 的 hopscotch-map 提供使用跳跃哈希的高速哈希映射与集合，缓存友好、冲突代价低，适合对性能敏感的系统级或高吞吐量应用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48704373' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/itsthelore/wayfinder-router' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wayfinder Router: deterministic routing of queries between local and hosted LLM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wayfinder 路由器：在本地与托管大模型之间的确定性查询路由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48704373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48704373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48704373' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wayfinder Router targets deterministic routing between a local LLM and a hosted one, aiming to optimize latency, privacy, and cost for hybrid AI setups. The approach helps designers build edge-friendly AI flows where data privacy and responsiveness matter most.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wayfinder 路由器在本地与托管大模型之间实现确定性查询路由，意在降低延迟、提升隐私并控制成本。该思路有助于设计混合 AI 部署，提升边缘计算场景的响应速度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1271892689/88c43e02-cd49-45ba-9926-2f82880b57a0' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1271892689/88c43e02-cd49-45ba-9926-2f82880b57a0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48658148' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fusion-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fusion Programming Language</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fusion 编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658148' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fusion is a new programming language aiming to blend functional concepts with high-performance, data-centric programming. It emphasizes unified syntax and compiler optimizations to target CPUs and accelerators, enabling safer, faster parallel code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fusion 是一门新型编程语言，旨在将函数式思想与高性能、数据密集型编程结合。它强调统一的语法与编译器优化，目标是在 CPU 与加速器上高效并行执行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fusion-lang.org/fut-preview.png' data-full='https://fusion-lang.org/fut-preview.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48646077' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://markdy.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Markdy: Like Mermaid Diagrams, but for Motion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Markdy：像 Mermaid 图一样处理运动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48646077' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48646077' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Markdy offers a textual DSL resembling Mermaid diagrams but focused on motion and animation workflows. It lets creators describe timing, transitions, and dependencies in a readable format, producing diagrams that aid planning and collaboration on complex motion systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Markdy 提供一种类似 Mermaid 的文本语言，专注于运动与动画工作流。它让创作者用易读的格式描述时序、过渡和依赖，生成的图表有助于规划与协同处理复杂的运动系统。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thomasliao.com/eval-startups' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why eval startups fail (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>评估型初创企业为何失败（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48637868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48637868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48637868' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines why evaluation-focused startups struggle to scale in 2025, highlighting issues like ambiguous value propositions, high customer acquisition costs, and data or compliance hurdles. It distills actionable lessons for founders, investors, and ecosystem builders to improve go-to-market discipline and unit economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>分析了2025年以评估服务为核心的初创企业为何难以扩张，指出价值定位不清、获客成本高、数据与合规风险等常见问题。提供对创始人、投资人和生态建设者的可操作建议，帮助提升市场定位与单位经济效益。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48709187' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48709187' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://oldvcr.blogspot.com/2026/06/working-around-dragons-with-lemote.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Working around dragons with the Lemote Yeeloong laptop and OpenBSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Lemote Yeeloong 笔记本与 OpenBSD 的生存笔记</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48709187' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retro-tech diary recounts installing OpenBSD on a Lemote Yeeloong laptop, a machine built around the Loongson CPU. It details the hardware quirks, drivers, and boot issues encountered, plus workarounds and patches. The post underscores the enduring appeal of free software tinkering on obscure hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇回顾性笔记记录了在以龙芯处理器为基础的 Lemote Yeeloong 笔记本上安装 OpenBSD 的过程，面临硬件兼容性、驱动与引导问题等挑战，并给出相应的解决方法与补丁。文章凸显了在冷门硬件上坚持开源、动手实践的魅力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710437' data-tags='Politics,Business,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48710437' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pcmag.com/opinions/the-us-used-to-demand-the-best-tech-now-we-ban-it' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The US Used to Demand the Best Tech. Now We Ban It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国曾追求最先进科技，如今却在实施禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710437' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that U.S. policy shifted from procuring cutting-edge tech to banning or constraining it, with implications for domestic innovation, supply chains, and competitiveness. It calls for balancing national security with maintaining leadership in tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为美国的政策已从追求最先进技术转向禁止或限制使用，影响国内创新、供应链和全球竞争力。呼吁在国家安全与科技领先之间取得更好的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/04ltqIf4ihFMDfo38F0qNgS-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1782561174.png' data-full='https://i.pcmag.com/imagery/articles/04ltqIf4ihFMDfo38F0qNgS-1.fit_lim.size_1200x630.v1782561174.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48650550' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48650550' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.swiftlatex.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LaTeX.wasm: LaTeX Engines in Browsers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LaTeX.wasm：浏览器中的 LaTeX 引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48650550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48650550' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>LaTeX.wasm brings LaTeX processing into the browser, enabling client-side typesetting without server round-trips. This improves privacy and offline capabilities for editors and learners, while challenging server-centric workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LaTeX.wasm 将 LaTeX 处理移至浏览器端，使前端即可完成排版，无需来回服务器。对编辑器和学习者而言提高了隐私与离线能力，同时也对以服务器为中心的工作流提出挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48631511' data-tags='Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48631511' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.patrickdomanico.com/bpm/2026/06/16/inventing-the-future-one-lisp-machine-at-a-time/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inventing the Future, One Lisp Machine at a Time</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48631511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inventing the Future, One Lisp Machine at a Time threads nostalgia for Lisp machines into a meditation on language and hardware design. It uses historical machines to reflect on modern computing and the value of specialized architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过 Lisp 机器的历史回顾，将对过去的情感融入对现代计算的思考，探讨语言与体系结构的价值，以及专业化架构在未来中的角色。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48622433' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48622433' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dmtrKovalenko/ffs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F* file system – file search that reads SSD directly bypassing OS kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F* 文件系统——直接读取SSD以绕过内核的文件搜索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48622433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48622433' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>F* is a file system project that reads SSDs directly to perform fast searches, bypassing the OS kernel. It trades portability and broad compatibility for speed and custom indexing, raising questions about stability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>F* 文件系统通过直接读取 SSD 来实现快速搜索，绕过操作系统内核。它以提升速度和自定义索引为代价，牺牲了一定的可移植性与安全性，引发对稳定性与安全性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e88c4f5b439ece17139e20e334c47931f717db763a725c46cfdc464969eba4ce/dmtrKovalenko/ffs' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e88c4f5b439ece17139e20e334c47931f717db763a725c46cfdc464969eba4ce/dmtrKovalenko/ffs' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48653338' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/naver/lispe/wiki/2.7-FEXPR-vs.-vtable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FEXPRs vs. vtable: how LispE interpreter works</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 99</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48653338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48653338' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The LispE interpreter discussion contrasts FEXPRs and vtables, explaining how LispE handles dynamic evaluation and function calls. It sheds light on design choices that balance expressiveness, performance, and memory use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文对 LispE 解释器中的 FEXPR 与 vtable 进行对比，解释其在动态求值和函数调用方面的实现方式。解析这些设计取舍如何在表达能力、性能和内存消耗之间取得平衡。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into Space Shuttle I/O Processor boards reveals the hardware that ran critical avionics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章深入解读航天飞机 I/O 处理器的电路板，展示了在关键飞行控制中运行的硬件。文中介绍了板卡的架构、设计细节与挑战，并从历史 NASA 系统中提取了可靠性与硬件安全性的经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(311) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://media-centre.canyon.com/en-INT/266866-new-canyon-heads-up-display-helmet-could-be-a-safety-gamechanger-for-road-riding/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canyon HUD helmet for road riding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canyon 公路骑行抬头显示头盔</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48609129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48609129' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canyon&#x27;s road-hiking helmet includes a built-in HUD that projects ride data and navigation into the rider&#x27;s line of sight. While it could boost awareness and route follow-through, engineers warn about distraction, battery drain, and privacy implications as sensor data may be collected.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canyon 推出集成抬头显示的公路骑行头盔，实现在骑行中实时显示速度、导航等信息。该设计有望提升骑手安全与决策能力，但也引发分心、续航以及传感数据隐私等问题的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/science-and-technology/2026/06/24/why-big-ai-labs-are-hiring-so-many-philosophers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Big AI labs are hiring philosophers</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662452' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48662452' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI labs are hiring philosophers to grapple with ethics, alignment, and long-term governance. This trend signals a growing emphasis on interdisciplinary thinking to address safety, policy, and societal impact as AI systems scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 实验室正在招聘哲学家以应对伦理、对齐与长期治理等议题。这一趋势表明，随着 AI 系统规模扩大，跨学科思维在安全、政策与社会影响方面的重要性日益增强。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(313) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.usenix.org/legacy/event/woot10/tech/full_papers/Wolchok.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Crawling BitTorrent DHTs for Fun and Profit [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为乐与利润：对 BitTorrent DHT 的爬取研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619159' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe scalable techniques for crawling BitTorrent&#x27;s DHT to map peers and content, enabling large-scale measurement of decentralized file sharing. While providing valuable insights into network dynamics and resilience, the work also raises privacy and abuse concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇研究论文提出对 BitTorrent DHT 进行大规模爬取的技术方法，用以观测对等节点、内容分布和网络动态。研究提供了对去中心化文件分享的可观测性与鲁棒性见解，同时也暴露了隐私与滥用的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/52-hertz_whale' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>52-hertz whale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>52赫兹鲸鱼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48665093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 96</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48665093' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48665093' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 52-hertz whale is famous for vocalizations at a frequency far from other whales, making it a symbol of loneliness and scientific mystery in ocean acoustics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>52赫兹鲸被认为是发出远超常见鲸鳴的频率的孤独鲸，成为海洋声学研究与科普的神话象征。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YOLO26 is the latest in the YOLO family, delivering real-time object detection with improved speed and accuracy. The post outlines architectural tweaks and training ideas that enable end-to-end performance.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.politico.com/news/2026/06/21/rent-collections-are-down-in-new-york-and-no-ones-sure-why-00966982' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rent collections are down in New York</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rent collections in New York are down, with economists and landlords searching for explanations amid data gaps and shifting rental markets. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约的租金收取率下降，数据不清与市场变化使经济学家和房东寻找原因。下滑可能反映宏观趋势、地方政策或经济因素对房东与租客现金流的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频演示将任天堂 Wii U 游戏在1980年代的 Bernoulli 磁盘上运行，彰显对老旧硬件与软件的保护与再利用的创新。此现象凸显仿真与向后兼容性在游戏保存领域的重要性与挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tw-fade demonstrates a pure CSS technique for scroll-driven edge masking, enabling subtle fade effects on scroll without JavaScript. It showcases how CSS variables and lightweight techniques can deliver dynamic UI polish with zero script.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48626918' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.fca.org.uk/news/press-releases/investors-get-real-time-view-uk-bond-market-activity-first-time' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investors get real-time view of UK bond market activity for the first time</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>投资者首次获得英国债券市场实时交易活动视图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48626918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48626918' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>For the first time, real-time data on UK gilt trading activity is accessible to investors. Regulators enable near-instant visibility into liquidity and price formation, improving transparency in a historically opaque market. Expect shifts in trading strategies and market-maker behavior as participants adapt to live information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>投资者首次获得英国国债交易活动的实时数据，提升市场透明度。监管方实现对流动性与价格形成的近实时监控，这一市场过去相对不透明。随着参与者适应实时信息，交易策略与做市商行为可能发生变化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.fca.org.uk/sites/default/files/media/images/fca_social_share.jpg' data-full='https://www.fca.org.uk/sites/default/files/media/images/fca_social_share.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 91</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48674290' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful exploration of early dreams and REM sleep in infants, leveraging science communication to question what babies dream about. The piece implies that while we don&#x27;t know what babies dream about, studying infant sleep sheds light on cognitive development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以玩笑笔触探讨婴儿的梦境与快速发展的睡眠阶段，讨论婴儿在梦境中可能的内容。文章指出，虽然尚无法确定婴儿梦见何物，研究婴儿睡眠有助于理解认知发展的早期阶段。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48661536' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Quebec town recognizes trees as living beings with legal rights, granting them protection in municipal decisions. The move raises questions about environmental ethics, governance, and how such rights interact with development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>魁北克小镇将树木视为具法律权利的存在，在城政决策中给予保护。这一举措引发关于环境伦理、治理以及树木权利与发展之间关系的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48703418' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48703418' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://the.scapegoat.dev/turning-music-into-a-chore-is-what-made-me-an-artist/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turning music into a chore is how I became a musician (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把练习变成“家务活”，是我成为音乐家的原因（2022）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48703418' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48703418' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on how reframing music practice as a daily routine helped reduce friction and deepen skill, ultimately shaping the author into a musician. It argues that disciplined, repeatable habits foster creativity rather than kill it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过将音乐练习日常化、制度化来降低难度、提升技艺的个体反思。作者认为有纪律、可重复的习惯能激发创造力，而非抹煞它。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

The outcome underscores sustainability risks for open editors dealing with proprietary formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>描述一款在 Hacker News 上不久前被讨论的开源 DOCX 编辑器已被删除的情况，反映出小众开源工具在维护与治理方面的脆弱性。事件凸显了在处理受专利和格式限制的工具时，OSS 项目在可持续性方面的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48656986' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/DDecoene/WebBaseIII' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: WebBase-III – dBASE III rebuilt in the browser with its own interpreter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Swsim：软件化 SIM 卡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48656986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48656986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48656986' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swsim presents a software SIM card approach, allowing SIM credentials to be managed in software rather than a physical SIM. The project explores implications for carrier provisioning, device flexibility, and security risk in software-defined SIMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Swsim 提供软件化 SIM 卡方案，使 SIM 证书在软件中管理，而非物理 SIM。该项目探讨对运营商配置、设备灵活性以及软件定义 SIM 的安全风险的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1261150371/15e15e6f-f1f2-4342-b06b-56df4cec77a6' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1261150371/15e15e6f-f1f2-4342-b06b-56df4cec77a6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48706877' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the historical roots of schooling designed to cultivate independent, critical thinking. The piece traces reform movements and pedagogical ideas that shifted authority away from rote learning toward analytical inquiry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文追溯了旨在培养独立、批判性思维的教育制度的历史根源。回顾改革运动与教学理念如何从灌输式教育转向以分析性探究为核心的教学模式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(336) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kirill.korins.ky/articles/hellishly-slow-level-13-deflate-compression/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hellishly Slow Level 13 Deflate Compression</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>地狱般缓慢的 Level 13 Deflate 压缩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48633839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48633839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48633839' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece investigates why Deflate level 13 compression is exceptionally slow, highlighting the computational complexity and trade-offs in implementation. It shows that higher compression levels bring steep CPU costs with diminishing returns on real-world data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探究了 Deflate 在 Level 13 时异常缓慢的原因，强调了计算复杂性与实现取舍。结果表明更高的压缩等级会带来极高的 CPU 开销，在实际数据上收益往往递减。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48596834' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/open-source/maintainers/how-pull-request-limits-are-cutting-down-the-noise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pull request limits are cutting down the noise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pull request 限制正在降低噪声</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48596834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48596834' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raising PR limits helps maintainers focus on meaningful changes and reduces review fatigue. By throttling contributed changes, teams can speed up CI cycles and improve code quality. The piece shares practical guidelines for applying limits without stifling collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提升拉取请求限制有助于维护者专注于有意义的改动，降低评审疲劳。通过对提交数量设限，团队可以加快持续集成循环并提升代码质量。文章给出在不扼杀协作的前提下落实现限的方法与要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48688438' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(338) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/software/2026/06/26/one-man-two-kernels-and-a-lot-of-risc-v/5262858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>One man, two kernels, and a lot of RISC-V</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一人两种内核，以及大量的 RISC‑V</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48688438' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A solo developer experiments with running two kernels on the same RISC-V setup, illustrating the practicality and limits of multi-kernel experimentation on an open ISA. The work underscores RISC-V&#x27;s openness to unconventional designs and the ongoing challenge of kernel portability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名开发者在同一 RISC‑V 平台上尝试运行两种内核，展示在开放指令集架构上进行多内核试验的可行性与局限性。此举凸显 RISC‑V 的开放性为非常规设计留出空间，同时也暴露内核移植和安全性方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5262971.jpg?imageId=5262971&amp;x=0&amp;y=28.33&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=71.67&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=28.33&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=71.67&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5262971.jpg?imageId=5262971&amp;x=0&amp;y=28.33&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=71.67&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=28.33&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=71.67&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jan-herlyn.com/blog/doing-a-masters-while-working/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Doing a masters while working in Spain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在西班牙工作同时攻读硕士学位</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48627027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48627027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在西班牙工作的人同时攻读硕士学位是可行的，需要选对课程、做好时间规划。文章讨论了课程形式、签证与工作安排等实际要点，以及在线与现场学习的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710082' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48710082' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://newsletter.kentbeck.com/p/the-cost-yagni-was-never-about' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The cost YAGNI was never about</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>YAGNI 从来不是关于成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710082' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>YAGNI&#x27;s cost isn&#x27;t a simple price tag. The piece reframes the &#x27;cost&#x27; as the hidden maintenance burden and reduced future flexibility that come from over-simplifying early, urging a nuanced balance between foregone features and long-term adaptability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>YAGNI 的代价并非简单的金钱或时间成本。文章将“成本”重新界定为因过早简化而带来的隐藏维护负担和未来灵活性的降低，强调在实现与扩展之间需要更细致的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48712420' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48712420' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.07013' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Knowledge Distillation of Black-Box Large Language Models (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对黑箱大语言模型的知识蒸馏（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48712420' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48712420' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 2024 work studies distilling knowledge from black-box LLMs into smaller, trainable models without access to internal weights. Through comparisons of techniques like output-based distillation and data augmentation, it shows trade-offs between fidelity and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇 2024 年的工作研究在无法访问内部权重的前提下，将黑箱大语言模型的知识蒸馏到更小的模型中。通过对比输出蒸馏、数据增强等方法，揭示保真度与效率之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gossamer-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gossamer: a Rust-flavoured language with real goroutines and pause-free memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gossamer：一种带有真实协程与无暂停内存的 Rust 风格语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48690231' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gossamer presents a Rust‑flavored language that provides real goroutines and pause‑free memory, aiming to improve concurrency without long GC pauses. The project envisions high‑throughput systems programming and easier asynchronous code without traditional boilerplate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gossamer 提供一种受 Rust 启发、具备真实协程与无暂停内存的语言，目标是在不依赖长 GC 暂停的情况下提升并发性。该项目定位高吞吐系统编程，并让异步代码编写变得更简洁。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48659257' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48659257' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/openai-broadcom-jalapeno-inference-chip/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI and Broadcom unveil LLM-optimized inference chip</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 与 Broadcom 公布面向大语言模型的推理芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48659257' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI and Broadcom unveil an inference chip optimized for large language models, aiming to accelerate and reduce the cost of LLM deployments. The collaboration signals how chipmakers are aligning hardware to AI workloads and could influence data-center architectures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 与 Broadcom 推出面向大语言模型推理的定制芯片，目标是加速并降低成本，提升数据中心对 AI 工作负载的适配。此举反映了硬件厂商正围绕 AI 任务进行更紧密的软硬件协同。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/4DeZq8e24W4qgIrtAu4Xmg/54189176ae1c6150d94cac02fb104f79/openai-broadcom-jalapeno-inference-chip-image-16_9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/4DeZq8e24W4qgIrtAu4Xmg/54189176ae1c6150d94cac02fb104f79/openai-broadcom-jalapeno-inference-chip-image-16_9.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48683588' data-tags='Energy,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48683588' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aditya.patadia.org/p/ai-and-cloud-costs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why current LLM costs are not sustainable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么当前大型语言模型成本不可持续</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48683588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48683588' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Current LLM costs for real-world inference and serving are rising faster than value. The piece calls out compute, storage, and energy as the bottlenecks and suggests strategies such as model optimization, selective inference, and smarter deployment to curb expenses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当前大型语言模型在推理与部署上的成本上升，难以持续。文章指出计算、存储与能源是瓶颈，并提出模型优化、按需推理与更高效部署等降低成本的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0_fU!,w_1200,h_600,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd4b10bb3-234b-43c3-aa85-8ac23ceb7a35_1200x1017.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0_fU!,w_1200,h_600,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd4b10bb3-234b-43c3-aa85-8ac23ceb7a35_1200x1017.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48644463' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48644463' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48644463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Overfitted a 900KB Transformer to Compress a 100MB CSV into 7MB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 900KB 的 Transformer 将 100MB 的 CSV 压缩到 7MB</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48644463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48644463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48644463' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers trained an ultra-compact transformer (about 900KB) that compresses a 100MB CSV down to 7MB, using learned representations rather than traditional codecs. While impressive, the result raises questions about generalization, decoding speed, and lossiness, yet hints at practical gains for edge storage and bandwidth-limited pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者训练出一个约 900KB 的超紧凑 Transformer，能够把 100MB 的 CSV 压缩到 7MB，依赖学习得到的表示而非传统编解码。尽管效果震撼，但也引发关于泛化性、解码速度和丢失信息等方面的质疑，同时为边缘存储和带宽受限的数据处理带来潜在收益。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' data-full='https://news.ycombinator.com/y18.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48708721' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48708721' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adiamond.me/2026/06/software-engineering-in-the-age-of-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reflections on software engineering in the age of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 时代的软件工程反思</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48708721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48708721' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay evaluating how AI changes software development—automation, code generation, and collaboration—and proposing new practices, guardrails, and learning paradigms to stay effective in the AI era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文评估 AI 如何改变软件开发中的自动化、代码生成和协作，并提出在 AI 时代保持高效所需的新实践、风险控制方法与学习范式。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rochus-keller/MUMPS/blob/main/docs/MUMPS_Primer.adoc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The MUMPS 76 Primer – anniversary edition</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MUMPS 76 入门教程——周年纪念版</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48706796' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MUMPS is a programming language with deep ties to healthcare databases. The anniversary primer revisits the language’s syntax and usage, highlighting its enduring relevance and the quirks that have shaped legacy medical software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MUMPS 是一种在医疗保健数据库领域有深远影响的编程语言。周年纪念版入门书回顾了它的语法与使用场景，强调该语言在遗留医疗软件中的持续相关性与独特之处。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48618536' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>承认对 Midjourney 超声扫描仪功能的误判，阐述系统的实际能力与局限，以及从中得到的经验教训。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48667560' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The arXiv paper explores combining visual representations with textual code to improve comprehension and collaboration in programming. It discusses design considerations, benefits, and potential pitfalls of hybrid code modalities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇论文探讨用视觉表示与文本代码相结合来提升编程的理解与协作，讨论了混合代码形式的设计要点、潜在好处以及可能的风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(358) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://haystack.deepset.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Haystack: Open-Source AI Framework for Production Ready Agents, RAG</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Haystack：面向生产就绪代理与 RAG 的开源 AI 框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658095' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Haystack provides a production-focused AI framework for building agents and RAG pipelines, with components for orchestrating prompts, memory, and retrieval. It helps teams move from prototype to deployment with tested connectors and evaluation tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Haystack 提供一个面向生产的 AI 框架，专注于构建代理与检索增强生成（RAG）管线，包含编排、记忆与检索等组件，帮助团队从原型过渡到生产部署。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48706554' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/radio/quirks/more-evidence-of-life-on-mars-but-still-no-life-1.7649645' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>More evidence is consistent with possible ancient life on Mars (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>更多证据支持火星存在古代生命的可能性（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48706554' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings add to the growing but inconclusive evidence for ancient life on Mars, strengthening biosignature arguments while stopping short of confirming life.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的发现进一步强化了关于火星存在古代生命的证据，但仍未达到确定性，只是增加了生物标志信号的可能性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48699778' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48699778' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/society/2026/jun/27/screen-time-damage-under-twos-development-study' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Screen time can damage under-twos&#x27; development, landmark study suggests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>里程碑式研究表明，屏幕时间可能损害两岁以下儿童发育</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48699778' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A landmark study links screen time in toddlers to potential developmental harms, reinforcing guidelines to limit passive screen exposure and prioritize interactive, caregiver-led activities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>里程碑式研究显示，幼儿的屏幕使用时间与潜在的发育风险相关，强调应限制被动屏幕暴露，优先进行互动性和在家长引导下的活动。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/80926b962ff832a320bb64479ff8effa6baaa3ba/637_0_4995_3995/master/4995.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=e7c4e6089037de66e0c363359bfadcdf' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/80926b962ff832a320bb64479ff8effa6baaa3ba/637_0_4995_3995/master/4995.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=e7c4e6089037de66e0c363359bfadcdf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(361) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mlc.ai/modern-gpu-programming-for-mlsys/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Modern GPU Programming for MLSys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MLSys 的现代 GPU 编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48643459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48643459' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48643459' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An overview of modern GPU programming for MLSys, highlighting advances in programming models, memory hierarchies, and performance portability across GPUs. The discussion matters for ML workloads, scaling models, and building portable solutions that run on diverse accelerators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文概述了 MLSys 圆桌/论文中的现代 GPU 编程，聚焦编程模型、内存层次结构与跨 GPU 的性能可移植性等进展。对机器学习工作负载、扩展模型和在多种加速器上实现可移植解决方案具有重要意义。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48638120' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48638120' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(362) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idlemachines.co.uk/essays/pytorch-training-loop' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The annotated PyTorch training loop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>带注释的 PyTorch 训练循环</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇带注释的 PyTorch 训练循环清晰分解了数据流、损失、反向传播和优化步骤，并给出实践注释。对理解梯度累积、设备放置和训练调试等细节很有帮助。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特刊回顾 Matt&#x27;s Script Archive，这批1990年代初的网页脚本推动了客户端效果（如计数器、闪烁文本、图像翻转等），深刻影响了早期的网站设计与开发者文化。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports the switch-off of the Droitwich long-wave transmitter, marking the end of an era for long-range radio broadcasting in Britain. <div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48615277' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(368) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medicalxpress.com/news/2026-06-early-life-adversity-molecular-imprint.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Early adversity leaves lasting molecular imprint across the body: primate study</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>早期逆境在体内留下持久分子印记：灵长类动物研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48615277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48615277' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A primate study links early-life adversity to lasting molecular imprints across multiple tissues, suggesting stress can leave durable epigenetic marks. The findings help explain how childhood stress affects long-term health and may guide interventions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>灵长类动物研究发现，早期生活的逆境会在多处组织中留下持久的分子级印记，提示压力可以产生耐久性分子信号。这些发现有助于理解童年压力对长期健康的影响，并可能为干预策略提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/early-life-adversity-l.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/early-life-adversity-l.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

The project provides a approachable, historical context for understanding modern video pipelines.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48662988' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dbos-inc/dbosify-py' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: DBOSify – Drop-in Temporal replacement built on Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DBOSify：在 Postgres 上的可替换时态数据解决方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48662988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48662988' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DBOSify offers a drop-in temporal replacement built on PostgreSQL, enabling time-aware data models without a separate store. It provides native Postgres-based temporal features to version data and run temporal queries with familiar tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DBOSify 提供一个基于 PostgreSQL 的可插拔时态替代方案，支持在不引入分离存储的情况下实现时间感知的数据建模。通过 Postgres 原生实现，便于对数据进行版本控制和时态查询。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/84ab3e27b04857c28afda65ee664a0931e8f6575af21df693f19e22ab7a7a49a/dbos-inc/dbosify-py' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/84ab3e27b04857c28afda65ee664a0931e8f6575af21df693f19e22ab7a7a49a/dbos-inc/dbosify-py' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48605085' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48605085' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://6it.dev/blog/infographics-operation-costs-in-cpu-clock-cycles-take-2-80736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Efficient C++ Programming for Modern C++ CPUs, Chapter 4/part 2</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示面向现代 CPU 的 C++ 操作成本信息图，帮助开发者把握优化方向，如分支、内存访问和指令级选择。内容将理论转化为面向高性能代码的实用、数据驱动的决策。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a human-focused postmortem of the 1996 AOL outage, examining misconfigurations, cascade effects, and organizational response. Highlights lessons for resilience in large-scale online services, such as incident response practices and redundancy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了对 1996 年 AOL 故障的人为原因的事后分析，聚焦配置失误、级联故障与组织应对。强调大型在线服务的韧性：应急响应、冗余与变更管理等方面的经验教训。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48648493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48648493' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 2019 paper surveys Bronze Age trade routes and merchant networks, shedding light on how early economies connected distant communities and shaped technology diffusion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇2019年的论文梳理了青铜时代的贸易路线与商人网络，揭示早期经济如何连接远距离的社群并影响技术传播。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nijho.lt/post/proxmox-to-nixos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating from Proxmox to NixOS and Incus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 Proxmox 迁移到 NixOS 与 Incus</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48679385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48679385' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The migration story compares declarative, reproducible system configurations of NixOS with Proxmox’s traditional setup. It covers practical steps, potential pitfalls, and the benefits of immutable system states for long-run maintenance and automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比了使用 NixOS 的声明式、可重复配置与 Proxmox 的传统做法，讲述迁移中的具体步骤、可能的坑，以及不可变系统状态在长期运维与自动化中的优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nijho.lt/media/icon_hu80d4068d4a221917e6e98566e3ddf93f_419855_512x512_fill_lanczos_center_3.png' data-full='https://www.nijho.lt/media/icon_hu80d4068d4a221917e6e98566e3ddf93f_419855_512x512_fill_lanczos_center_3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48635075' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48635075' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09498' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-Harness: Harnesses That Improve Themselves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自我增强的 Harnesses：能够自行改进的 Harness</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48635075' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paper proposes Self-Harness: harnesses that can improve themselves via self-feedback loops, enabling autonomous optimization of their own behavior. If practical, this could accelerate agent capability, though it raises safety and control challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出自我增强的 Harnesses，通过自我反馈循环自行改进自身行为，以实现自主优化。若落地，可能提升代理能力，但也带来安全与控制方面的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48685677' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48685677' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mondaycom/HATCHA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Captcha proves you&#x27;re human. HATCHA proves you&#x27;re not</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CAPTCHA 证明你是人，HATCHA 证明你不是</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48685677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48685677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48685677' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CAPTCHA proves you&#x27;re human; HATCHA aims to prove you&#x27;re not, offering a new angle on bot verification. The project explores more robust or alternative challenges to distinguish humans from automated agents, with implications for security and accessibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CAPTCHA 用以证明你是人，HATCHA 则试图证明你不是，为机器人验证带来新的思路。该项目探讨更强韧的挑战或替代方案来区分人类与自动化程序，并对安全性与可访问性带来影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1166637261/fe31656d-fd67-46b4-bdd1-f4084470adc7' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1166637261/fe31656d-fd67-46b4-bdd1-f4084470adc7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48701936' data-tags='Security,DevOps,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48701936' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dobrowolski.dev/article/enhancing-x11-application-security-with-lxc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Enhancing X11 Application Security with LXC (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 LXC 提升 X11 应用安全性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48701936' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isolating X11 apps in LXC containers can limit the blast radius of exploits and reduce host compromise. The approach leverages user namespaces, seccomp, and app-based confinement, but may introduce performance and GPU access trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>将 X11 应用放入 LXC 容器实现沙箱化，可以限制攻击蔓延、降低宿主机风险。该方法利用用户命名空间、seccomp 等机制，但也会带来性能与 GPU 访问方面的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dobrowolski.dev/static/img/rss.png' data-full='https://dobrowolski.dev/static/img/rss.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
