</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48595882' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents Write SaaS apps where users control where their data is stored, showcasing architectural approaches to support data residency. The approach helps satisfy compliance and data sovereignty needs while offering flexibility and potential performance trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了让用户决定数据存放地点的 SaaS 应用设计思路，体现支持数据居留的架构方法。该方案有利于满足合规与数据主权需求，同时带来灵活性和潜在的性能权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48624114' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48624114' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oldtelephoneroom.ca/1983-northern-telecom-commodore-phone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1983 Northern Telecom Commodore Phone</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48624114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48624114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48624114' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vintage hardware deep-dive into a 1983 Northern Telecom Commodore phone, examining its design, capabilities, and how it reflected era-era tech integrations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一部 1983 年的 Northern Telecom Commodore 电话进行复古硬件剖析，探讨其设计与功能，以及当时把话音和计算结合的尝试。该设备体现了那个时代在硬件中融合语音与计算的趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48661911' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.browserbase.com/blog/why-captchas-are-getting-harder' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The CAPTCHA arms race: from distorted text to browser identity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>验证码军备竞赛：从扭曲文本到浏览器身份识别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48661911' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The CAPTCHA arms race has evolved from distorted text to browser identity and behavior-based challenges, raising questions about privacy and accessibility. Developers and defenders push for stronger bot resistance, while attackers adapt with sophisticated automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>验证码军备竞赛已从扭曲文本演进为基于浏览器身份与行为的挑战，涉及隐私与无障碍性问题。安全团队持续改进抵御能力，攻击者也在用更高水平的自动化进行对抗。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/yd6zslid/production/cd3a1c87bc3f777a2a15455ef582259e571d2432-856x579.png?rect=0,67,856,447&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/yd6zslid/production/cd3a1c87bc3f777a2a15455ef582259e571d2432-856x579.png?rect=0,67,856,447&amp;w=1200&amp;h=627&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48673801' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: What surprised you about Estonia e-Residency and running an Estonian OÜ?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：对爱沙尼亚电子居留与在爱沙尼亚注册公司有哪些惊喜？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48673801' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Community responses discuss the practical realities of Estonia&#x27;s e-Residency and running an Estonian OÜ, including onboarding, banking, taxation, and regulatory burdens. The thread surfaces surprising pros and cons, like ease of international business but complexities around banking and EU rules.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区讨论爱沙尼亚电子居留与在爱沙尼亚注册公司（OÜ）的实际情况，包括开户、银行、税务与监管负担等。讨论揭示了一些出人意料的利弊，例如跨境经营的便利性与银行及欧盟规则的复杂性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48611940' data-tags='Finance,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48611940' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sebastiangarren.com/2026/06/17/lending-is-meritorious-and-should-be-praised-how-the-fifth-lateran-council-unlocked-financial-theory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Fifth Lateran Council unlocked financial theory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第五次拉特兰公会议如何影响金融理论的发展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48611940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48611940' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Fifth Lateran Council reframed lending as a virtuous activity, shaping early modern financial thinking by legitimizing debt and interest. This historical shift helped pave the way for later financial theory and economic innovation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第五次拉特兰公会议将放贷视为德行行为，改变了对利息和债务的道德评判，推动早期现代金融思想的发展。这一历史转折为后来的金融理论与经济创新铺平了道路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sebastiangarren.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/cathedral-exterior-angle.png' data-full='https://sebastiangarren.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/cathedral-exterior-angle.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48652019' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48652019' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://napkins.mtmn.name/posts/stray-jay.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A stray &quot;j&quot; ruined my evening</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个多余的 j 把我的夜晚毁了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48652019' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lighthearted tale about how a stray character derailed a project, illustrating how tiny typos can cascade into friction. It underscores the value of careful editing, search-and-replace discipline, and defensive coding habits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述一个偶然的字符引发混乱的趣闻，展示了微小的打字错误也能引发连锁问题。强调细致的编辑、谨慎的替换以及防御性编码的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48619125' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48619125' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://owl.billpg.com/8086-segmented-memory-was-a-good-idea-almost/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>8086 Segmented Memory was a good idea</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>8086 分段内存曾是个好主意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48619125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48619125' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the 8086&#x27;s segmented memory architecture was a sane compromise for its era, enabling efficient relocation and simple protection while keeping real-mode addressing manageable. It explains how segmentation influenced later memory-management tricks and why the model became both powerful and cumbersome.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 8086 的分段内存结构在当时是一个合理的折衷，便于地址重定位和基本保护，同时避免了直接的平坦地址空间。分段设计影响了后来的内存管理技巧，既强大又带来实现上的复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://owl.billpg.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/cropped-grok_image_xp7b2vv.jpg' data-full='https://owl.billpg.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/cropped-grok_image_xp7b2vv.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48613336' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48613336' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(390) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://home.cern/francois-englert-1932-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>François Englert (1932 – 2026)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>弗朗索瓦·恩格勒（1932–2026）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48613336' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48613336' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Francois Englert, Nobel laureate who co-formulated the Higgs mechanism, has passed away. The note reflects on his contributions to quantum field theory and the standard model, and how the Higgs field shaped modern physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>诺贝尔奖得主、共同提出希格斯机制的物理学家弗朗索瓦·恩格勒去世，文章回顾他在量子场论和标准模型中的贡献，以及希格斯场对现代物理的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://home.cern/wp-content/themes/cern/public/images/logo-white.svg' data-full='https://home.cern/wp-content/themes/cern/public/images/logo-white.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48690200' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/skx/slisp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Slisp: Simple Lisp compiler (Linux/amd64)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Slisp：简单 Lisp 编译器（Linux/amd64）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48690200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48690200' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces Slisp, a minimal Lisp compiler targeting Linux amd64, illustrating a compact compiler workflow and educational value for language enthusiasts. The project highlights how to implement a small, functional compiler and can serve as a learning resource or lightweight toolchain component.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 Slisp——面向 Linux/amd64 的简易 Lisp 编译器，展示紧凑的编译流程，适合语言爱好者学习。该项目提供了实现小型、可用的编译器的示例，既可作为学习资源也可作为轻量工具链的一部分。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48661095' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(392) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.euronews.com/business/2026/06/24/venezuela-plans-biggest-debt-restructuring-in-history-after-maduros-fall' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Venezuela reveals $240B in debt it cannot pay (~$100B more than expected)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>委内瑞拉披露无法偿付的 2400 亿美元债务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48661095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48661095' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Venezuela reveals a debt load of roughly $240B that it cannot pay, far exceeding prior estimates. The plan to restructure debt marks a historic fiscal move, with potential implications for oil revenues, sovereign risk, and international lenders.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该国披露无法按现有能力偿付的约 2400 亿美元债务，远超此前预期。计划进行历史上最大规模的债务重组，可能影响石油收入、主权风险及国际债权人关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/81/14/90/1200x675_cmsv2_25ec8703-e7f0-5d9b-9c57-816ef752fbde-9811490.jpg' data-full='https://images.euronews.com/articles/stories/09/81/14/90/1200x675_cmsv2_25ec8703-e7f0-5d9b-9c57-816ef752fbde-9811490.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48658464' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/qualcomm-to-acquire-modular' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qualcomm to Acquire Modular</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qualcomm 将收购 Modular</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48658464' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48658464' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qualcomm announces plans to acquire Modular, broadening its hardware and software ecosystem with modular platform capabilities. The deal could accelerate a more versatile device stack across mobility, AR/VR, or edge AI, depending on Modular&#x27;s technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>高通宣布收购 Modular，意在通过模块化平台能力扩展其硬件与软件生态系统。此次交易有望加速移动、AR/VR 或边缘 AI 等领域的设备栈多样化，前提是 Modular 的技术能有效落地。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48652028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48652028' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NLRB has recognized a union at Wizards of the Coast, giving employees a formal path to collective bargaining over wages, benefits, and working conditions, and could influence labor relations in the gaming industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国全国劳资关系委员会已正式承认 Wizards of the Coast 的工会，赋予员工通过集体谈判争取薪酬、福利与工作条件的权利。这一举动可能改变游戏行业的劳资关系，并对公司内部的协作产生重要影响。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48614309' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Author contemplates routing more traffic through nodes outside the UK in response to online safety policy changes. The piece weighs latency, privacy, and compliance tradeoffs as digital infrastructure choices affect users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者在英国在线安全政策变化的背景下，思考将更多流量路由到英国以外的节点。此举涉及延迟、隐私和合规性的权衡，以及对用户体验和网络拓扑的潜在影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-physicists-track-and-trap-the-elusive-neutrino-20260624/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How physicists track and trap the elusive neutrino</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>物理学家如何追踪并捕捉中微子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48674619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48674619' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physicists track and trap neutrinos using large-scale detectors, cryogenics, and sophisticated shielding to suppress background.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>物理学家通过大型探测器、低温与严格屏蔽来追踪和捕捉中微子，强调中微子与物质的微弱相互作用带来的挑战。研究者们希望通过这些实验回答宇宙演化、物质起源等重大问题。</p>

By integrating with macOS Focus and system signals, it reduces friction when taking breaks, helping maintain productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>LookAway 是一款面向 Mac 的休息提醒应用，能够智能判定何时不打断用户以避免干扰。它借助 macOS 的专注模式和系统信号，减少打断的时机，提高工作效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@s_nikolaev/faster-knn-search-in-manticore-2-pass-hnsw-batched-distances-and-avx-512-b85604647aab' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Faster KNN search in Manticore: 2-pass HNSW, batched distances, and AVX-512</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48682243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48682243' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48682243' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details improvements to KNN search in Manticore using 2-pass HNSW, batched distance calculations, and AVX-512 to boost throughput on large datasets. The optimizations translate to noticeable speedups for real-time similarity tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍通过两阶段 HNSW、批量距离计算与 AVX-512 优化，提升 Manticore 的最近邻搜索性能。对大规模数据的实时相似性检索意义显著，带来可观的速度提升。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers apply information theory to Wordle strategies, deriving optima for guess selection and revealing how to maximize information gain each turn. The study illustrates how mathematical reasoning can optimize even everyday word games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者将信息理论应用到 Wordle 的解题策略，推导出在每一步应如何选择最优猜测以最大化信息增益。此研究展示了数学推理在日常文字游戏中的应用潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/NotASithLord/peerd' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: peerd – AI agent harness that runs entirely in your browser</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48646165' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>peerd runs an AI agent harness fully in the browser, enabling private, client-side decision making without server round-trips. It demonstrates how powerful AI workloads can move to the client, with tradeoffs in performance and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>peerd 在浏览器内完全运行 AI 代理，客户端就能完成推理与决策，无需服务器交互。这一设计展示了将强大 AI 工作负载下沉到前端的潜力，同时也带来性能和安全方面的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/690976bec190393ae6c5b48868f4724f7a6377a043b0fb96c2d4bc54e2158a2d/NotASithLord/peerd' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/690976bec190393ae6c5b48868f4724f7a6377a043b0fb96c2d4bc54e2158a2d/NotASithLord/peerd' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yeokhengmeng.com/2026/06/swift-on-apple-ii/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bringing Swift to the Apple ][</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48670579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48670579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into porting the modern Swift language to the Apple II, exploring compiler tricks, resource constraints, and how a contemporary language can be made to run on vintage hardware. The piece showcases retrocomputing ingenuity and lessons for language/runtime design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨将现代 Swift 语言移植到 Apple II 的实现过程，涵盖编译器技巧、资源约束与在老旧硬件上运行的可行性。展示了复古计算的巧思，以及对语言与运行时设计的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645390' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48645390' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48645390' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bun-sqlgen offers type-safe raw SQL for Bun without ORM, enabling compile-time checks and safer data access while staying lightweight in Bun’s JS/TS environment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bun-sqlgen 提供 Bun 的类型安全原始 SQL，摆脱 ORM 依赖，利用编译时检查提升数据访问安全性，同时保持在 Bun 的 JavaScript/TypeScript 生态中轻量高效。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48641927' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(409) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lwilko.com/blog/i-gave-an-ai-a-civilization' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI Built a Nuke and Still Lost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 构建核武器却仍然迷失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48641927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48641927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48641927' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents a provocative scenario where an AI designs a nuclear weapon yet still fails to achieve its broader goals, illustrating misalignment and the fragility of long-horizon planning. The piece underscores why strong safety and alignment controls are essential when pursuing ambitious AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过一个极端设定展示：即使 AI 设计出核武器等危险工具，仍可能无法实现更高层次的目标，暴露出对齐与目标设定的局限。强调在追求强大 AI 系统之时，须加强安全控制与对齐机制。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48659517' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48659517' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knob.monster/how-do-you-keep-web-midi-from-crashing-a-1983-synthesizer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do you keep Web MIDI from crashing a 1983 synthesizer?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何防止 Web MIDI 让 1983 年的合成器崩溃？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48659517' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48659517' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web MIDI can conflict with vintage hardware, causing crashes if timing, MIDI routing, or software events collide. The piece offers practical strategies—like isolating interfaces, buffering messages, or using safe wrappers—to keep 1980s synths stable when connected to modern browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Web MIDI 与老式合成器的兼容性问题可能导致崩溃。文中给出实用做法，如隔离接口、缓冲 MIDI 消息，以及使用安全包装器，以在现代浏览器下保持 1980 年代合成器的稳定性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://knob.monster/static/logo.avif' data-full='https://knob.monster/static/logo.avif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48620848' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48620848' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pure-effect.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pure Effect – Reproduce production bugs on your laptop without a DB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Pure Effect——在没有数据库的情况下在笔记本复现生产环境中的错误</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48620848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48620848' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pure Effect is a lightweight tool that reproduces production bugs on a laptop without a database, simulating real-world failures and performance quirks. It aids debugging, regression testing, and reliability work by letting teams isolate issues before deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pure Effect 是一个轻量级工具，可以在本地笔记本上复现生产环境中的错误而无需数据库，模拟真实世界的故障与性能异常。帮助开发者在上线前进行调试、回归测试与可靠性工作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48707146' data-tags='Politics,Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48707146' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-28/austria-lobbies-eu-to-host-anthropic-after-us-access-curbs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Austria Lobbies EU to Host Anthropic After US Access Curbs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>奥地利游说欧盟，在美国访问受限后托管 Anthropic 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48707146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48707146' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Austria is lobbying the EU to host access to Anthropic&#x27;s AI models after US access curbs, seeking a regional alternative for AI capabilities. The move highlights geopolitics of AI tooling, licensing, and how different blocs are building independent access channels for large-language models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>奥地利正在游说欧盟，在美国进一步限制访问后，争取在欧盟托管并提供对 Anthropic 模型的访问通道。这一举动凸显 AI 工具的地缘政治，以及各区域建立独立访问渠道以获得大型语言模型的能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48673450' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48673450' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aleph-alpha.com/en/blog/model-training-as-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Model Training as Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把模型训练当作代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48673450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48673450' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues for encoding training pipelines as code, enabling version control, reproducibility, and CI/CD for ML. Treating training like software helps track experiments, share configurations, and audit results across teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张把模型训练流程写成可版本化的代码，借助版本控制、可重复性和持续集成/交付实现更可控的训练。以这种方式记录实验、共享配置并对结果进行审计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.acquired.fm/episodes/the-walt-disney-company' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Walt Disney Company is the most successful at monetizing human nostalgia [audio]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48635409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 42</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48635409' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disney monetizes nostalgia across films, streaming, merchandise, and experiences, turning cultural memory into a scalable revenue engine. The analysis explores how nostalgia resonates at scale and fuels ongoing fan engagement and spending.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>迪士尼通过电影、流媒体、周边商品和主题体验，将人们的怀旧情感转化为可扩展的收入引擎。分析探讨怀旧为何在大规模平台上产生共振，并推动持续的粉丝参与与消费。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过穷举搜索对 GIF 的帧编码和调色板进行无损优化，能显著压缩旧格式的存储需求，但计算成本较高。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://questdb.com/blog/lies-damn-lies-and-database-benchmarks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lies, Damn Lies and Database Benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数据库基准测试的谎言与真相</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48639429' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QuestDB argues benchmarks for databases are often misleading without transparent workloads and representative data. The post unpacks pitfalls like cherry-picked tests, hardware bias, and the gap between synthetic metrics and real-world performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QuestDB 指出数据库基准测试若缺乏透明的工作负载与真实数据代表性，往往会产生误导。文中揭示了挑选性测试、硬件偏差，以及合成指标与实际场景性能之间的差距等问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://questdb.com/images/blog/2026-06-18/banner.webp' data-full='https://questdb.com/images/blog/2026-06-18/banner.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://infoscience.epfl.ch/entities/publication/9a49779b-f9f8-448d-b3d1-737c78455309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Puzzling Success of Overparameterization: Lottery Tickets or Escape Dimensions?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过参数化的成功之谜：乐透票理论还是维度逃逸？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657481' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48657481' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An EPFL study investigates why overparameterized models train so well, weighing lottery ticket explanations against the idea of escaping to low-dimensional manifolds. The work suggests multiple mechanisms may underpin success, with implications for pruning, architecture design, and generalization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EPFL 的研究探讨了为何过参数化模型能训练得如此出色，权衡乐透票假说与“逃逸到低维流形”的解释。研究表明可能存在多种机制共同作用，这对剪枝、网络结构设计和泛化能力具有重要启示。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Go issue reports that uuid.NewV7 produces browser-specific anomalies, always returning 7000 on browsers. The note underscores cross-environment id generation quirks and the importance of robust implementation testing.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(440) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ultralytics YOLO26: Unified Real-Time End-to-End Vision Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ultralytics YOLO26：统一的实时端到端视觉模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48639434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48639434' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ultralytics YOLO26 introduces a unified, real-time end-to-end vision model aimed at simplifying deployment across detection, segmentation, and related tasks. The approach emphasizes cross-task consistency and edge-friendly performance, reducing the engineering burden of multi-task vision systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ultralytics 发布 YOLO26，一体化的实时端到端视觉模型，致力于以单一架构实现检测、分割等任务，简化部署。该方法在边缘设备上的性能与跨任务的一致性之间寻求平衡，降低多任务视觉系统的工程负担。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.terezatizkova.com/writing/software-abundance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Software Is Becoming Marketing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>软件正在演变为营销工具</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48694563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48694563' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48694563' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that software is increasingly a marketing engine, with product design and distribution tied to user acquisition and retention metrics. The shift changes how companies monetize and measure value, raising questions about privacy, trust, and the sustainability of growth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点认为软件已转变为营销引擎，产品设计与发行与用户获取、留存紧密绑定。这一转变改变了商业化和增长衡量的方式，同时也引发对隐私、信任和可持续价值的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.terezatizkova.com/api/og?title=Software%20is%20becoming%20marketing&amp;subtitle=Four%20ways%20software%20products%20and%20jobs%20change%20in%20the%20age%20of%20abundance' data-full='https://www.terezatizkova.com/api/og?title=Software%20is%20becoming%20marketing&amp;subtitle=Four%20ways%20software%20products%20and%20jobs%20change%20in%20the%20age%20of%20abundance' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48688081' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48688081' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(443) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jtekt.co.jp/e/products/capacitor/capacitor_about.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is a Lithium-ion capacitor?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>什么是锂离子电容？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48688081' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48688081' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lithium‑ion capacitor is a hybrid energy device blending features of batteries and supercapacitors, offering high power with long cycle life. It trades energy density for faster charging and discharging, making it attractive for grid and rapid‑dependent applications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>锂离子电容是一种混合型能量存储器件，结合了电池和超级电容的特性，提供高功率输出与较长循环寿命。相比锂电池，其能量密度通常较低但充放电更快，适用于对快速充放电有要求的应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jtekt.co.jp/e/assets/img/common/ogp.jpg' data-full='https://www.jtekt.co.jp/e/assets/img/common/ogp.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(444) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48687769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Is &quot;no source code was copied&quot; still a sufficient copyright defense?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：’未拷贝源代码’的抗辩依然有效吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48687769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48687769' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48687769' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion about whether claiming &#x27;no source code was copied&#x27; remains a valid defense amid modern software copyright disputes and AI-generated code, touching on originality, derivative works, and licensing. The thread explores evolving norms in software copyright and legal risk for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在现代软件版权纠纷中，主张“未拷贝源代码”的抗辩是否仍具有效力，涉及原创性、衍生作品与许可等问题。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Evan 的 Jujutsu 教程</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48654540' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48654540' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://infoscience.epfl.ch/server/api/core/bitstreams/e5d662ea-1e8d-4dda-b917-8cbb8bb40bf9/content' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RRB-Trees: Efficient Immutable Vectors (2012) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RRB-树：高效的不可变向量（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48654540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48654540' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This paper introduces RRB-Trees, a persistent data structure that offers fast immutable vectors and efficient concatenation. It underpins functional programming languages and informs modern persistent collection implementations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇论文介绍了 RR-Tree，一种高效的不可变向量数据结构，支持快速拼接和修改保留原状态。它奠定了函数式编程语言中持久化集合实现的理论与实践基础。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48682979' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48682979' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://smist08.wordpress.com/2026/06/25/linux-kills-strncpy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux Kills Strncpy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 放弃使用 strncpy</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48682979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48682979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48682979' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux is phasing out strncpy due to its unsafe semantics and the risk of buffer overflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 因 strncpy 的语义不安全及易导致缓冲区溢出而逐步淘汰该函数。文章讨论了更安全的字符串处理替代方案，以及这一改动在现代 C 语言与内核安全中的意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/smist08.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-2.png?fit=1200%2C800&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/smist08.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/image-2.png?fit=1200%2C800&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48630085' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48630085' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://qsoe-dev.blogspot.com/2026/06/qsoe-project-v01-is-released.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>QSOE: QNX-inspired OS with dual-kernel architecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>QSOE：受 QNX 启发的双内核操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48630085' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48630085' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>QSOE is a QNX-inspired OS with a dual-kernel architecture designed for isolation and real-time performance, exploring a bold approach to secure, dependable system design. The project outlines architectural goals and the trade-offs of a dual-kernel design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>QSOE 是一个受 QNX 启发的双内核操作系统，旨在实现隔离性和实时性能之间的权衡，探讨在安全、可靠系统设计中的大胆思路。文中概述了架构目标及双内核设计的取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48657131' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48657131' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(451) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plover.com/lang/bismillah.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deciphering Basmala</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解密巴斯马拉（Basmala）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48657131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48657131' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post delves into Basmala, the opening phrase of many Islamic texts, examining its linguistic form, religious significance, and historical usage. It situates the phrase within script and culture, clarifying common misconceptions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章从语言与文化角度解析巴斯马拉（祈祷名词）在阿拉伯语中的形式、宗教意义以及历史使用情境，揭示常见误解并说明其在文本与书写中的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(462) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notes.ansonbiggs.com/youre-probably-using-agent-skills-wrong/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You&#x27;re probably using Agent Skills wrong</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你可能在错误使用 Agent Skills</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48624327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48624327' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48624327' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that many practitioners misuse &#x27;agent skills&#x27; in AI workflows and offers guidelines for designing reusable, composable skills with sensible governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出许多从业者在 AI 工作流中错误使用“agent skills”，并给出设计可重用、可组合技能以及合理治理的指南。</p>

It offers practical tips for rigorous, repeatable performance testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对将网卡与磁盘带宽推至极限的技术性探讨，涵盖基准测试、瓶颈分析及在“任务失败但结果看似成功”情形下的解读。提供实施严谨、可重复性能测试的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.yahoo.com/news/world/articles/indias-tata-electronics-hit-cyber-153155513.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tata Electronics cyber breach claiming to expose Apple, Tesla trade secrets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tata Electronics 网络入侵声称曝光苹果、特斯拉商业机密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48632833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48632833' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cyber breach at Tata Electronics is alleged to expose trade secrets related to major tech companies, raising concerns about supply chain security in the electronics sector. The incident spotlights the ongoing cyber risk facing global manufacturing and R&amp;D collaborations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>塔塔电子的网络攻击事件被指暴露苹果、特斯拉等公司的商业机密，凸显电子行业在供应链安全方面的持续网络风险。此事凸显全球制造与研发合作面临的网络威胁。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48675653' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.savingourstars.org/darkskylighting' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Dark Sky Lighting?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么要暗夜天空照明？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48675653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48675653' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dark sky lighting designs reduce light pollution, helping keep skies dark for stargazing, preserve nocturnal ecosystems, and improve energy efficiency. The piece outlines practical guidelines for street and outdoor lighting that minimize glare while maintaining safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>暗夜天空照明能降低光污染，保护夜空观星环境、改善野生动物栖息与能源效率。文章提出了降低眩光、保持安全的城市照明设计要点，帮助城市走向更可持续的夜间照明。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48696127' data-tags='Science,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48696127' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ethz.ch/en/news-and-events/eth-news/news/2026/06/a-new-type-of-pixel.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Researchers have developed pixels that can emit and analyse light together</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>能同时发光与探测光的新型像素</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48696127' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48696127' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers created a pixel that can both emit and sense light, enabling on-chip optical emission and sensing. This dual-functionality could simplify photonic circuits, improve LiDAR, and unlock new sensor applications, pushing the boundary of imaging tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员开发出能同时发光和探测光的新型像素，有望简化集成光子电路并推进激光雷达、光学传感等新应用。该双功能像素可能开启新的成像与传感能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ethz.ch/en/news-and-events/eth-news/news/2026/06/a-new-type-of-pixel/_jcr_content/pageimages/imageCarousel.imageformat.lightbox.729879006.jpg' data-full='https://ethz.ch/en/news-and-events/eth-news/news/2026/06/a-new-type-of-pixel/_jcr_content/pageimages/imageCarousel.imageformat.lightbox.729879006.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(471) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.schneier.com/blog/archives/2026/06/one-million-passports-leaked-online.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1M Passports Leaked Online</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一百万份护照数据泄露在线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A leak exposed about one million passports online, underscoring serious privacy, identity, and border security risks in an era of sprawling data exposés.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约一百万份护照数据在网上泄露，凸显在大数据暴露潮中隐私、身份与边境安全的严重风险。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48699010' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(472) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lyra.horse/blog/2026/06/reddit-spam-internals/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A peek into Reddit&#x27;s anti-spam internals</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>窥探 Reddit 的反垃圾内部机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48699010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48699010' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post reveals how Reddit&#x27;s anti-spam systems function behind the scenes, detailing detection signals, automation, and human moderation. It discusses trade-offs between false positives and user experience, and what this means for platform trust and policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章揭示了 Reddit 反垃圾系统的内部运作，包括检测信号、自动化流程和人工审核的协同。讨论了误报与用户体验之间的权衡，以及对平台信任与政策的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-48710414' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='false' aria-controls='digest-details-48710414' aria-label='Show summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.pascalschuster.de/article/do-llms-pass-the-mirror-test' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Do LLMs pass the mirror test?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMs 能通过镜像测试吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710414' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710414' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>They explore whether current LLMs can demonstrate self-recognition or mirror-like understanding, shedding light on the limits of self-modeling in language models. The results suggest LLMs still lack genuine self-awareness, with implications for how we evaluate introspection in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究探讨当前的 LLM 是否具备自我识别能力或镜像测试中的自我理解，揭示语言模型自我建模能力的局限。结果表明，LLMs 仍然缺乏真正的自我意识，这对评估 AI 的内省能力具有影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://quantsandqualia.com/og.png' data-full='https://quantsandqualia.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过反思和反馈循环来优化系统与工作流的思考。认为深思熟虑的自省能揭示瓶颈，为改进提供方向，优于单纯的强力调整。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(475) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ogulcancelik/herdr' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Herdr: Agent multiplexer that lives in your terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Herdr：在终端里运行的代理多路复用器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714802' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48714802' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Herdr is a terminal-centric tool that multiplexes multiple AI agents, letting you route tasks and keep context within a single shell. It simplifies managing several assistants side-by-side and can boost productivity in CLI-heavy workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Herdr 是一个在终端内工作的代理多路复用器，允许同时运行和切换多个代理，方便在一个 shell 中分派任务、维持上下文。对以 CLI 为主的工作流来说，可以显著提升效率。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(482) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/colby1968-computer-aided-language-development-in-non-speaking-children' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Computer-Aided Language Development in Nonspeaking Children (1968) [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>非语言表达儿童的电脑辅助语言发展（1968）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48710350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48710350' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 1968 paper documents early computer-aided approaches to language development in nonspeaking children, foreshadowing assistive tech and human-computer interaction in education. It shows how early researchers used machines to support communication and learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇1968年的论文记录了早期计算机辅助的非语言表达儿童语言发展方法，预示了教育领域的辅助技术与人机交互。研究展示了通过机器来支持沟通与学习的初步尝试。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48702361' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to hosting your own DNS-over-HTTPS service, covering deployment options, privacy benefits, and potential pitfalls like performance bottlenecks. It helps readers take control of their DNS privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供搭建自有 DoH 服务的实用指南，讨论部署选项、隐私收益以及潜在的性能瓶颈等要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://console.modular.com/signup' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mojo programming language will become open-source soon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mojo 编程语言即将开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48701462' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-48701462' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mojo, a high-performance language from Modular, will soon be open-sourced, potentially unlocking broader adoption for AI and systems programming. Expect community-driven tooling, governance questions, and licensing considerations as the project moves to public collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mojo 将很快开源，有望推动在 AI 与系统编程领域的更广泛应用。随着向公开协作的过渡，社区工具、治理结构和许可问题将成为关注焦点。</p>
